                                {measurement.valueType === 'NUMBER' && <Field label="Unit" name="unit" defaultValue={measurement.unit ?? ''} />}
                                {measurement.valueType === 'BOOLEAN' ? <label className="field"><span>Target</span><select name="targetValue" defaultValue={measurement.targetValue === null ? '' : String(measurement.targetValue)}><option value="">No target</option><option value="true">Yes</option><option value="false">No</option></select></label> : <Field label="Target" name="targetValue" type={measurement.valueType === 'NUMBER' ? 'number' : 'text'} defaultValue={measurement.targetValue === null ? '' : String(measurement.targetValue)} />}
                                <Button>Save changes</Button>
                              </form>
                            )}
                            <div className="measurement-card__current"><span>Latest recorded</span><strong>{current ? `${current.value}${measurement.unit ? ` ${measurement.unit}` : ''}` : 'No values yet'}</strong>{current && <small>{dateLabel(current.recordedAt)}</small>}</div>
                            {history.length >= 2 && measurement.valueType === 'NUMBER' && (() => {
                              const series = [...history].reverse().filter((record) => typeof record.value === 'number');
                              if (series.length < 2) return null;
                              const values = series.map((record) => record.value as number);
                              const min = Math.min(...values); const max = Math.max(...values);
                              const points = values.map((value, index) => `${(index / (values.length - 1)) * 100},${28 - (max === min ? 0.5 : (value - min) / (max - min)) * 22}`).join(' ');
                              return <svg className="measurement-sparkline" viewBox="0 0 100 32" role="img" aria-label={`${measurement.label} history, ${series.length} recorded values`} preserveAspectRatio="none"><polyline points={points} fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke" /></svg>;
                            })()}
                            {measurement.valueType === 'BOOLEAN' ? (
                              <label className="field"><span>Record value</span><select value={measurementValues[measurement.id] ?? ''} onChange={(event) => setMeasurementValues((items) => ({ ...items, [measurement.id]: event.target.value }))}><option value="">Choose…</option><option value="true">Yes</option><option value="false">No</option></select></label>
                            ) : (
                              <Field label="Record value" type={measurement.valueType === 'NUMBER' ? 'number' : 'text'} value={measurementValues[measurement.id] ?? ''} onChange={(event) => setMeasurementValues((items) => ({ ...items, [measurement.id]: event.target.value }))} />
                            )}
                            <Button type="button" variant="secondary" disabled={!measurementValues[measurement.id]} onClick={() => void recordMeasurementValue(measurement)}>Record</Button>
                            {history.length > 1 && <details className="measurement-history"><summary>History · {history.length}</summary><ol>{history.map((record) => <li key={record.id}><strong>{record.value}{measurement.unit ? ` ${measurement.unit}` : ''}</strong><time>{dateLabel(record.recordedAt)}</time></li>)}</ol></details>}
                          </article>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                    <div className="detail-section">
                      <div className="section-heading"><h3>Review</h3><span>Saved evidence</span></div>
                      <div className="review-evidence">
                        <div><span>Actions complete</span><strong>{progress?.actionCompletion?.totalActions ? `${progress.actionCompletion.completedActions} of ${progress.actionCompletion.totalActions}` : progress ? 'No active Actions' : 'Loading'}</strong></div>
                        <div><span>Measurements with values</span><strong>{measurements.filter((measurement) => (measurementHistory[measurement.id]?.length ?? 0) > 0).length} of {measurementLoadState === 'READY' ? measurements.length : '—'}</strong></div>
                      </div>
                      <small className="review-evidence__note">Goal outcome progress is not calculated until its rule is configured.</small>
                    </div>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <EmptyState
                    icon="◎"
                    title="Choose a goal"
                    body="Select a goal from the map to see its milestones, actions, and progress."
                  />
                )}
              </section>
            </div>
          )}

          {page === 'planning' && (
            <PlanningCalendar
              actions={availableActions}
              goals={goals}
              actionsLoaded={actionsLoaded}
              actionsLoadError={actionsLoadError}
              initialDate={planningDate}
              onRefreshActions={() => setActionRefresh((value) => value + 1)}
              onOpenGoal={(goalId) => {
                const goal = goals.find((item) => item.id === goalId);
                if (goal) {
                  setSelectedGoal(goal);
                  setSelectedProject(projects.find((project) => project.id === goal.projectId) ?? null);
                }
                setPage('goals');
              }}
              onLogExecution={(action) => void openExecution(action)}
            />
          )}

          {notice && (
            <div className={`toast toast--${notice.kind}`} role="status">
              {notice.text}
              <button
                aria-label="Dismiss notification"
                onClick={() => setNotice(null)}
              >
                ×
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </main>

      {modal === 'project' && (
        <Modal title="Create a project" onClose={() => setModal(null)}>
          <form className="form-stack" onSubmit={submitProject}>
            <Field
              label="Project name"
              name="name"
              placeholder="e.g. Build a healthier routine"
              required
              maxLength={120}
              autoFocus
            />
            <TextArea
              label="Description"
              name="description"
              placeholder="What would you like to make happen?"
            />
            <div className="modal__actions">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="quiet"
                onClick={() => setModal(null)}
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button disabled={busy}>
                {busy ? 'Creating…' : 'Create project'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
      {(modal === 'goal' || modal === 'child') && (
        <Modal
          title={modal === 'child' ? 'Add a child goal' : 'Create a goal'}
          onClose={() => setModal(null)}
        >
          <form
            className="form-stack"
            onSubmit={(event) => submitGoal(event, modal === 'child')}
          >
            <Field
              label="Goal title"
              name="title"
              placeholder="What do you want to achieve?"
              required
              maxLength={200}
              autoFocus
            />
            <TextArea
              label="Description"
              name="description"
              placeholder="Why does this matter to you?"
            />
            <Field label="Target date" name="targetDate" type="date" />
            <div className="modal__actions">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="quiet"
                onClick={() => setModal(null)}
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Saving…' : 'Save goal'}</Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
      {modal === 'milestone' && (
        <Modal title="Add a milestone" onClose={() => setModal(null)}>
          <form className="form-stack" onSubmit={submitMilestone}>
            <Field
              label="Milestone name"
              name="title"
              placeholder="A meaningful checkpoint"
              required
              maxLength={200}
              autoFocus
            />
            <TextArea
              label="Description"
              name="description"
              placeholder="What will be true when you reach it?"
            />
            <Field label="Target date" name="targetDate" type="date" />
            <div className="modal__actions">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="quiet"
                onClick={() => setModal(null)}
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button disabled={busy}>Add milestone</Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
      {modal === 'action' && (
        <Modal title="Add an action" onClose={() => setModal(null)}>
          <form className="form-stack" onSubmit={submitAction}>
            <Field
              label="Action"
              name="title"
              placeholder="A small next step"
              required
              maxLength={200}
              autoFocus
            />
            <TextArea
              label="Details"
              name="description"
              placeholder="Optional details"
            />
            <label className="field">
              <span>Milestone</span>
              <select name="milestoneId" defaultValue="">
                <option value="">No milestone</option>
                {milestones
                  .filter((item) => item.status !== 'COMPLETED')
                  .map((item) => (
                    <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>
                      {item.title}
                    </option>
                  ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <div className="modal__actions">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="quiet"
                onClick={() => setModal(null)}
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button disabled={busy}>Add action</Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
      {modal === 'execution' && executionAction && (
        <Modal
          title={`Log execution · ${executionAction.title}`}
          onClose={() => setModal(null)}
        >
          <form className="form-stack" onSubmit={submitExecution}>
            {executionData?.executionNote && (
              <p className="inline-success">
                Existing note: {executionData.executionNote}
              </p>
            )}
            <Field
              label="Started at"
              name="actualStartedAt"
              type="datetime-local"
              defaultValue={localDateTimeInput(executionData?.actualStartedAt)}
            />
            <Field
              label="Completed at"
              name="actualCompletedAt"
              type="datetime-local"
              defaultValue={localDateTimeInput(
                executionData?.actualCompletedAt,
              )}
            />
            <TextArea
              label="Execution note"
              name="executionNote"
              placeholder="What happened while working on this action?"
              defaultValue={executionData?.executionNote ?? ''}
            />
            <div className="modal__actions">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="quiet"
                onClick={() => setModal(null)}
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button disabled={busy}>
                {busy ? 'Saving…' : 'Save execution'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function EmptyState({
  icon,
  title,
  body,
  action,
}: {
  icon: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="empty-state">
      <span className="empty-state__icon">{icon}</span>
      <h3>{title}</h3>
      <p>{body}</p>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}




============================================================
FILE: src/app/DashboardHome.tsx
============================================================

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type { Action, Goal, Project, Schedule } from '../lib/api';
import './dashboard-home.css';

interface DashboardHomeProps {
  email: string | null;
  goals: Goal[];
  projects: Project[];
  actions: Action[];
  nextAction?: Action;
  nextSchedule: Schedule | null;
  baseDataLoaded: boolean;
  actionsLoaded: boolean;
  actionsLoadError: boolean;
  schedulesLoaded: boolean;
  schedulesLoadError: boolean;
  onNewGoal: () => void;
  onNewProject: () => void;
  onOpenGoal: (goal: Goal) => void;
  onOpenProject: (project: Project) => void;
  onOpenExecution: (action: Action) => void;
  onOpenPlanning: () => void;
  onRetryActions: () => void;
  onRetrySchedules: () => void;
  onViewGoals: () => void;
  onViewProjects: () => void;
}

function formatDate(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) return 'No target date';

  const date = new Date(`${value.slice(0, 10)}T12:00:00`);

  return date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
}

function formatSchedule(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);

  return date.toLocaleString(undefined, {
    weekday: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
}

function getGreeting(): string {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();

  if (hour < 12) return 'Good morning';
  if (hour < 18) return 'Good afternoon';
  return 'Good evening';
}

function getGoalStatus(status: string): string {
  return status.toLowerCase().replaceAll('_', ' ');
}

export function DashboardHome({
  email,
  goals,
  projects,
  actions,
  nextAction,
  nextSchedule,
  baseDataLoaded,
  actionsLoaded,
  actionsLoadError,
  schedulesLoaded,
  schedulesLoadError,
  onNewGoal,
  onNewProject,
  onOpenGoal,
  onOpenProject,
  onOpenExecution,
  onOpenPlanning,
  onRetryActions,
  onRetrySchedules,
  onViewGoals,
  onViewProjects,
}: DashboardHomeProps) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');

  const filteredGoals = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!keyword) return goals;

    return goals.filter((goal) => {
      const projectName =
        projects.find((project) => project.id === goal.projectId)?.name ?? '';

      return (
        goal.title.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        (goal.description ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        projectName.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)
      );
    });
  }, [goals, projects, search]);

  const filteredProjects = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!keyword) return projects;

    return projects.filter(
      (project) =>
        project.name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        (project.description ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(keyword),
    );
  }, [projects, search]);

  const completedActions = actions.filter(
    (action) => action.status === 'COMPLETED',
  ).length;

  const openActions = actions.filter(
    (action) => action.status !== 'COMPLETED',
  ).length;

  const independentGoals = goals.filter(
    (goal) => goal.projectId === null,
  ).length;

  const nextActionGoal = nextAction
    ? goals.find((goal) => goal.id === nextAction.goalId)
    : null;

  const scheduledAction = nextSchedule
    ? actions.find((action) => action.id === nextSchedule.actionId)
    : null;

  return (
    <div className="dashboard-home">
      <section className="dh-welcome">
        <div className="dh-welcome__content">
          <span className="dh-eyebrow">YOUR PERSONAL OVERVIEW</span>

          <h2>{getGreeting()}{email ? `, ${email.split('@')[0]}` : ''}.</h2>

          <p>
            Keep your intentions visible and move one meaningful step forward.
          </p>

          <div className="dh-welcome__actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="dh-button dh-button--primary"
              onClick={onNewGoal}
            >
              <span>＋</span> Create a goal
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="dh-button dh-button--secondary"
              onClick={onNewProject}
            >
              <span>＋</span> New project
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="dh-welcome__decoration" aria-hidden="true">
          <div className="dh-orbit dh-orbit--one" />
          <div className="dh-orbit dh-orbit--two" />
          <div className="dh-orbit__center">f</div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="dh-stats">
        <article className="dh-stat">
          <span className="dh-stat__label">GOALS</span>
          <strong>{baseDataLoaded ? goals.length : '—'}</strong>
          <small>
            {baseDataLoaded
              ? `${independentGoals} independent`
              : 'Loading goals'}
          </small>
        </article>

        <article className="dh-stat">
          <span className="dh-stat__label">PROJECTS</span>
          <strong>{baseDataLoaded ? projects.length : '—'}</strong>
          <small>Organized areas of work</small>
        </article>

        <article className="dh-stat">
          <span className="dh-stat__label">OPEN ACTIONS</span>
          <strong>{actionsLoaded ? openActions : '—'}</strong>
          <small>Steps still to complete</small>
        </article>

        <article className="dh-stat">
          <span className="dh-stat__label">COMPLETED ACTIONS</span>
          <strong>{actionsLoaded ? completedActions : '—'}</strong>
          <small>Completed steps</small>
        </article>
      </section>

      <section className="dh-focus-grid">
        <article className="dh-focus-card dh-focus-card--action">
          <div className="dh-section-label">
            <span className="dh-section-label__icon">↗</span>
            <span>NEXT ACTION</span>
          </div>

          {actionsLoadError ? (
            <div className="dh-state">
              <strong>Actions could not be loaded</strong>
              <button type="button" onClick={onRetryActions}>
                Try again
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : !actionsLoaded ? (
            <p className="dh-muted">Loading your actions…</p>
          ) : nextAction ? (
            <div className="dh-focus-content">
              <h3>{nextAction.title}</h3>

              <p>
                {nextActionGoal?.title ?? 'Goal'} ·{' '}
                {nextAction.status === 'IN_PROGRESS'
                  ? 'In progress'
                  : 'To do'}
              </p>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="dh-inline-link"
                onClick={() => onOpenExecution(nextAction)}
              >
                Open execution <span>→</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="dh-state">
              <strong>No open actions</strong>
              <p>Create a small action under one of your goals.</p>
              <button type="button" onClick={onViewGoals}>
                Explore goals
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </article>

        <article className="dh-focus-card dh-focus-card--schedule">
          <div className="dh-section-label">
            <span className="dh-section-label__icon">▤</span>
            <span>UPCOMING SCHEDULE</span>
          </div>

          {schedulesLoadError ? (
            <div className="dh-state">
              <strong>Schedule could not be loaded</strong>
              <button type="button" onClick={onRetrySchedules}>
                Try again
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : !schedulesLoaded ? (
            <p className="dh-muted">Loading your schedule…</p>
          ) : nextSchedule ? (
            <div className="dh-focus-content">
              <h3>{scheduledAction?.title ?? 'Scheduled action'}</h3>

              <p>{formatSchedule(nextSchedule.startAt)}</p>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="dh-inline-link"
                onClick={onOpenPlanning}
              >
                Open planning <span>→</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="dh-state">
              <strong>Nothing scheduled this week</strong>
              <p>Choose a time for one of your open actions.</p>
              <button type="button" onClick={onOpenPlanning}>
                Open planning
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </article>
      </section>

      <section className="dh-workspace-section">
        <div className="dh-section-heading">
          <div>
            <span className="dh-eyebrow">YOUR WORKSPACE</span>
            <h2>Everything in motion</h2>
            <p>Find the goal or project you want to move forward.</p>
          </div>

          <label className="dh-search">
            <span aria-hidden="true">⌕</span>
            <input
              type="search"
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search goals and projects…"
              aria-label="Search goals and projects"
            />
            {search && (
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Clear search"
                onClick={() => setSearch('')}
              >
                ×
              </button>
            )}
          </label>
        </div>

        <div className="dh-content-grid">
          <section className="dh-list-panel">
            <div className="dh-panel-heading">
              <div>
                <h3>Goals</h3>
                <span>{baseDataLoaded ? goals.length : '—'} total</span>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="dh-text-button"
                onClick={onViewGoals}
              >
                View all <span>→</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {!baseDataLoaded ? (
              <p className="dh-empty-line">Loading goals…</p>
            ) : filteredGoals.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="dh-empty">
                <span className="dh-empty__icon">◎</span>
                <strong>
                  {search ? 'No matching goals' : 'No goals yet'}
                </strong>
                <p>
                  {search
                    ? 'Try another search term.'
                    : 'Start with an outcome that matters to you.'}
                </p>
                {!search && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="dh-button dh-button--secondary"
                    onClick={onNewGoal}
                  >
                    Create a goal
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="dh-goal-list">
                {filteredGoals.slice(0, 6).map((goal) => {
                  const project = projects.find(
                    (item) => item.id === goal.projectId,
                  );

                  return (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="dh-goal-row"
                      key={goal.id}
                      onClick={() => onOpenGoal(goal)}
                    >
                      <span className="dh-goal-row__mark">◎</span>

                      <span className="dh-row-main">
                        <strong>{goal.title}</strong>
                        <small>
                          {project?.name ?? 'Independent goal'}
                        </small>
                      </span>

                      <span className="dh-row-meta">
                        <span className="dh-status">
                          {getGoalStatus(goal.status)}
                        </span>
                        <small>{formatDate(goal.targetDate)}</small>
                      </span>

                      <span className="dh-row-arrow">→</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}

            {filteredGoals.length > 6 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="dh-list-footer"
                onClick={onViewGoals}
              >
                View all {filteredGoals.length} goals →
              </button>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="dh-list-panel">
            <div className="dh-panel-heading">
              <div>
                <h3>Projects</h3>
                <span>{baseDataLoaded ? projects.length : '—'} total</span>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="dh-text-button"
                onClick={onViewProjects}
              >
                View all <span>→</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {!baseDataLoaded ? (
              <p className="dh-empty-line">Loading projects…</p>
            ) : filteredProjects.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="dh-empty">
                <span className="dh-empty__icon">▦</span>
                <strong>
                  {search ? 'No matching projects' : 'No projects yet'}
                </strong>
                <p>
                  {search
                    ? 'Try another search term.'
                    : 'Create a project when you need to group related goals.'}
                </p>
                {!search && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="dh-button dh-button--secondary"
                    onClick={onNewProject}
                  >
                    Create a project
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="dh-project-list">
                {filteredProjects.slice(0, 6).map((project, index) => {
                  const projectGoals = goals.filter(
                    (goal) => goal.projectId === project.id,
                  );

                  return (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="dh-project-row"
                      key={project.id}
                      onClick={() => onOpenProject(project)}
                    >
                      <span
                        className={`dh-project-mark dh-project-mark--${index % 3}`}
                      >
                        {project.name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}
                      </span>

                      <span className="dh-row-main">
                        <strong>{project.name}</strong>
                        <small>
                          {project.description || 'No description'}
                        </small>
                      </span>

                      <span className="dh-project-count">
                        {projectGoals.length} goals
                      </span>

                      <span className="dh-row-arrow">→</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}

            {filteredProjects.length > 6 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="dh-list-footer"
                onClick={onViewProjects}
              >
                View all {filteredProjects.length} projects →
              </button>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

const API_BASE_URL = (
  import.meta.env.DEV ? '' : (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? '')
)
  .replace(/\/api\/?$/, '')
  .replace(/\/$/, '');
const TOKEN_KEY = 'flow-up.access-token';
const WORKSPACE_KEY = 'flow-up.active-workspace';

export interface UserContext {
  userId: string;
  email: string | null;
  isAuthenticated: true;
}
export interface Workspace {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  name: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
export interface Project {
  id: string;
  workspaceId: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
export interface Goal {
  id: string;
  workspaceId: string;
  projectId: string | null;
  parentGoalId: string | null;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  targetDate: string | null;
  status: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  archivedAt: string | null;
}
export interface GoalNode {
  goal: Goal;
  children: GoalNode[];
}
export interface Milestone {
  id: string;
  goalId: string;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  targetDate: string | null;
  status: 'PENDING' | 'COMPLETED';
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  completedAt: string | null;
}
export type ActionStatus = 'TODO' | 'IN_PROGRESS' | 'COMPLETED';
export interface Action {
  id: string;
  goalId: string;
  milestoneId: string | null;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  status: ActionStatus;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  completedAt: string | null;
  archivedAt: string | null;
}
export interface Execution {
  actionId: string;
  actualStartedAt: string | null;
  actualCompletedAt: string | null;
  actualDurationMinutes: number | null;
  executionNote: string | null;
}
export interface Measurement {
  id: string; goalId: string; label: string; description: string | null;
  valueType: 'NUMBER' | 'TEXT' | 'BOOLEAN'; unit: string | null;
  targetValue: number | string | boolean | null; createdAt: string; updatedAt: string; archivedAt: string | null;
}
export interface MeasurementRecord {
  id: string; measurementId: string; value: number | string | boolean;
  recordedAt: string; createdAt: string; note: string | null;
}
export interface DailyPlan {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  date: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
export interface Schedule {
  id: string;
  actionId: string;
  dailyPlanId: string | null;
  startAt: string;
  endAt: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
export interface ProgressSummary {
  goalId: string;
  goalProgress: {
    goalId: string;
    progressValue: number | null;
    calculationStatus: string;
    contributingSources: string[];
    calculationContext: Record<string, unknown>;
  };
  measurementProgress: Array<{
    measurementId: string;
    currentValue: unknown;
    targetValue: unknown;
    progressValue: number | null;
    calculationStatus: string;
  }>;
  actionCompletion: {
    totalActions: number | null;
    completedActions: number | null;
    incompleteActions?: number | null;
    completionValue: number | null;
    calculationStatus: string;
    calculationContext?: Record<string, unknown>;
  } | null;
  childGoalProgress: unknown[];
  calculationStatus: string;
}
export interface AIClarification {
  outcome: string;
  successCriteria: string[];
  knownFacts: string[];
  assumptions: string[];
  missingInformation: string[];
  questions: { id: string; prompt: string; why: string; answerType: string; required: boolean; options: string[] }[];
  provenance: 'NEEDS_CONFIRMATION';
}
export interface AIFeasibility {
  assessment: 'FEASIBLE' | 'CONCERN' | 'INSUFFICIENT_INFORMATION';
  summary: string;
  concerns: string[];
  alternatives: string[];
  assumptions: string[];
  missingInformation: string[];
  provenance: 'AI_PROPOSED';
}
export interface AIProposalItem {
  id: string;
  type: 'GOAL' | 'MILESTONE' | 'ACTION' | 'MEASUREMENT';
  title: string;
  description: string;
  targetDate: string | null;
  parentItemId: string | null;
  rationale: string;
  valueType?: 'NUMBER' | 'TEXT' | 'BOOLEAN' | null;
  unit?: string | null;
  targetValue?: number | string | boolean | null;
}
export interface AIProposal {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  proposalData: { summary: string; items: AIProposalItem[]; provenance: 'AI_PROPOSED'; applicationResults?: { operation: string; success: boolean; entityId?: string; errorCode?: string }[] };
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public readonly code: string,
    public readonly status: number,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export function getStoredToken(): string | null {
  return sessionStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
}
export function saveToken(token: string): void {
  sessionStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);
}
export function clearToken(): void {
  sessionStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  sessionStorage.removeItem(WORKSPACE_KEY);
}
export function getStoredWorkspaceId(): string | null {
  return sessionStorage.getItem(WORKSPACE_KEY);
}
export function saveWorkspaceId(workspaceId: string | null): void {
  if (workspaceId) sessionStorage.setItem(WORKSPACE_KEY, workspaceId);
  else sessionStorage.removeItem(WORKSPACE_KEY);
}

async function request<T>(
  path: string,
  options: { method?: string; body?: unknown; auth?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'application/json' };
  if (options.body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
  const token = getStoredToken();
  if (options.auth !== false && token)
    headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  const workspaceId = getStoredWorkspaceId();
  if (options.auth !== false && workspaceId) headers['X-Workspace-Id'] = workspaceId;
  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${path}`, {
      method: options.method ?? 'GET',
      headers,
      ...(options.body !== undefined
        ? { body: JSON.stringify(options.body) }
        : {}),
    });
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(
      'Could not reach FLOW-UP. Check that the backend is running.',
      'NETWORK_ERROR',
      0,
    );
  }
  const result = (await response.json()) as {
    success: boolean;
    data?: T;
    error?: { code?: string; message?: string };
  };
  if (!response.ok || !result.success) {
    const code = result.error?.code ?? 'REQUEST_FAILED';
    if (response.status === 401 && options.auth !== false) clearToken();
    throw new ApiError(
      result.error?.message ?? `Request failed (${response.status}).`,
      code,
      response.status,
    );
  }
  return result.data as T;
}

const get = <T>(path: string) => request<T>(path);
const post = <T>(path: string, body?: unknown) =>
  request<T>(path, { method: 'POST', body: body ?? {} });
const patch = <T>(path: string, body: unknown) =>
  request<T>(path, { method: 'PATCH', body });
const del = <T>(path: string) => request<T>(path, { method: 'DELETE' });

export const api = {
  register: (email: string, password: string) =>
    request<{
      status: string;
      token: string | null;
      user?: UserContext;
      email?: string;
    }>('/api/auth/register', {
      method: 'POST',
      auth: false,
      body: { email, password },
    }),
  login: (email: string, password: string) =>
    request<{ user: UserContext; token: string }>('/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      auth: false,
      body: { email, password },
    }),
  session: () => get<{ user: UserContext }>('/api/auth/session'),
  logout: () => post<{ message: string }>('/api/auth/logout'),
  initializeWorkspace: () => post<Workspace>('/api/workspaces/initialize'),
  workspaces: () => get<Workspace[]>('/api/workspaces'),
  createWorkspace: (name: string) => post<Workspace>('/api/workspaces', { name }),
  deleteWorkspace: (id: string) => del<{ deleted: boolean }>(`/api/workspaces/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
  currentWorkspace: () => get<Workspace>('/api/workspaces/current'),
  workspaceOverview: () =>
    get<{
      workspace: Workspace;

      goalSummary: { independentGoalCount: number } | null;
    }>('/api/workspaces/overview'),

    post<Project>('/api/projects', body),
  project: (id: string) =>
    get<Project>(`/api/projects/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
  goals: (projectId?: string) =>

  goal: (id: string) => get<Goal>(`/api/goals/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),

  milestones: (goalId: string) =>

  }) => post<Milestone>('/api/milestones', body),
  completeMilestone: (id: string) =>

  updateMilestone: (id: string, body: { title?: string; description?: string | null; targetDate?: string | null }) =>
    patch<Milestone>(`/api/milestones/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, body),
  actions: (goalId: string) =>
    get<Action[]>(`/api/goals/${encodeURIComponent(goalId)}/actions`),
  createAction: (body: {
    goalId: string;
    milestoneId?: string | null;
    title: string;
    description?: string;
  }) => post<Action>('/api/actions', body),
  updateActionStatus: (id: string, status: ActionStatus) =>
    patch<Action>(`/api/actions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/status`, { status }),
  updateAction: (id: string, body: { title?: string; description?: string | null }) =>
    patch<Action>(`/api/actions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, body),
  recordExecution: (
    id: string,
    body: {
      actualStartedAt?: string | null;

  ) =>
    post<Execution>(`/api/actions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/execution`, body),
  execution: (id: string) =>
    get<Execution | null>(`/api/actions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/execution`),
  progress: (goalId: string) =>
    get<ProgressSummary>(`/api/progress/goals/${encodeURIComponent(goalId)}`),
  measurements: (goalId: string) =>
    get<Measurement[]>(`/api/goals/${encodeURIComponent(goalId)}/measurements`),
  createMeasurement: (body: { goalId: string; label: string; valueType: Measurement['valueType']; unit?: string | null; targetValue?: number | string | boolean | null }) =>

  getAIProposal: (id: string) => get<AIProposal>(`/api/ai/proposals/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
  applyAIProposal: (id: string, selection: { selectedItemIds: string[]; editedData: unknown }) =>
    post<{ proposalId: string; status: string; operations: { operation: string; success: boolean; entityId?: string; errorCode?: string }[] }>(`/api/ai/proposals/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/apply`, { selection }),
  rejectAIProposal: (id: string) => post<AIProposal>(`/api/ai/proposals/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/reject`),
  createDailyPlan: (date: string) =>
    post<DailyPlan>('/api/planning/daily-plans', { date }),
  dailyPlan: (date: string) =>

      `/api/planning/daily-plans/${encodeURIComponent(date)}`,

    get<Schedule[]>(
      `/api/planning/schedules?startDate=${encodeURIComponent(startDate)}&endDate=${encodeURIComponent(endDate)}`,
    ),
  createSchedule: (body: {
    actionId: string;
    startAt: string;
    endAt: string;
    dailyPlanId?: string | null;
  }) => post<Schedule>('/api/planning/schedules', body),
  updateSchedule: (id: string, body: { startAt?: string; endAt?: string; dailyPlanId?: string | null }) =>
    patch<Schedule>(`/api/planning/schedules/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, body),
  deleteSchedule: (id: string) =>

      `/api/planning/schedules/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
    ),
};

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { api, type Action, type ActionStatus, type Goal, type Milestone, type Schedule } from '../lib/api';
import './planning.css';

type CalendarView = 'month' | 'week' | 'day';
type ScheduleDraft = { actionId: string; schedule?: Schedule; date: string; start: string; end: string };

function localDate(date: Date): string {
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
function dateAtNoon(value: string): Date { return new Date(`${value}T12:00:00`); }
function addDays(value: string, count: number): string {
  const result = dateAtNoon(value);
  result.setDate(result.getDate() + count);
  return localDate(result);
}
function weekStart(value: string): string {
  const date = dateAtNoon(value);
  return addDays(value, -((date.getDay() + 6) % 7));
}
function dateRange(date: string, view: CalendarView): { start: string; end: string } {
  if (view === 'day') return { start: date, end: date };
  if (view === 'week') { const start = weekStart(date); return { start, end: addDays(start, 6) }; }
  const first = dateAtNoon(date); first.setDate(1);
  const gridStart = addDays(localDate(first), -((first.getDay() + 6) % 7));
  return { start: gridStart, end: addDays(gridStart, 41) };
}
function timeAt(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  return `${String(date.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
function actionDate(value: string): string { return localDate(new Date(value)); }
function displayDate(value: string, options: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions): string {
  return dateAtNoon(value).toLocaleDateString(undefined, options);
}
function statusLabel(action: Action): string {
  return action.status === 'COMPLETED' ? 'Completed' : action.status === 'IN_PROGRESS' ? 'In progress' : 'To do';
}

function Button({ children, onClick, disabled, variant = 'secondary', ariaLabel }: {
  children: React.ReactNode; onClick: () => void; disabled?: boolean; variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'quiet'; ariaLabel?: string;
}) {
  return <button type="button" aria-label={ariaLabel} className={`button button--${variant}`} onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled}>{children}</button>;
}

export function PlanningCalendar({
  actions, goals, actionsLoaded, actionsLoadError, initialDate, onRefreshActions,
  onOpenGoal, onLogExecution,
}: {
  actions: Action[]; goals: Goal[]; actionsLoaded: boolean; actionsLoadError: boolean;
  initialDate: string; onRefreshActions: () => void; onOpenGoal: (goalId: string) => void;
  onLogExecution: (action: Action) => void;
}) {
  const [view, setView] = useState<CalendarView>('week');
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState(initialDate);
  const range = useMemo(() => dateRange(selectedDate, view), [selectedDate, view]);
  const [schedules, setSchedules] = useState<Schedule[]>([]);
  const [milestonesByGoal, setMilestonesByGoal] = useState<Record<string, Milestone[]>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState('');
  const [operationError, setOperationError] = useState('');
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ScheduleDraft | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [savingAction, setSavingAction] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true); setLoadError('');
    try {
      // Include UTC boundary spillover, then render only the selected local-date range.
      const items = await api.schedules(addDays(range.start, -1), addDays(range.end, 1));
      setSchedules(items.sort((a, b) => a.startAt.localeCompare(b.startAt)));
    } catch (error) {
      setLoadError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not load schedules.');
    } finally { setLoading(false); }
  }, [range.start, range.end]);
  useEffect(() => { void refresh(); }, [refresh]);
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    Promise.all(goals.map(async (goal) => [goal.id, await api.milestones(goal.id)] as const))
      .then((entries) => { if (active) setMilestonesByGoal(Object.fromEntries(entries)); })
      .catch(() => { if (active) setMilestonesByGoal({}); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [goals]);

  const days = useMemo(() => {
    const count = view === 'month' ? 42 : view === 'week' ? 7 : 1;
    const start = view === 'month' || view === 'week' ? range.start : selectedDate;
    return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => addDays(start, index));
  }, [range.start, selectedDate, view]);
  const visibleSchedules = schedules.filter((schedule) => actionDate(schedule.startAt) >= range.start && actionDate(schedule.startAt) <= range.end);
  const openActions = actions.filter((action) => action.status !== 'COMPLETED' && !action.archivedAt);
  const scheduledActionIds = new Set(visibleSchedules.map((schedule) => schedule.actionId));
  const unscheduled = openActions.filter((action) => !scheduledActionIds.has(action.id));

  function sessionsFor(date: string): Schedule[] {
    return visibleSchedules.filter((schedule) => actionDate(schedule.startAt) === date);
  }
  function movePeriod(offset: number) {
    const amount = view === 'month' ? 0 : view === 'week' ? 7 : 1;
    if (view === 'month') {
      const date = dateAtNoon(selectedDate); date.setDate(1); date.setMonth(date.getMonth() + offset);
      setSelectedDate(localDate(date));
    } else setSelectedDate(addDays(selectedDate, amount * offset));
  }
  function beginCreate(action: Action) {
    const entries = sessionsFor(selectedDate);
    const latestEnd = entries.reduce((latest, item) => Math.max(latest, Date.parse(item.endAt)), 0);
    const suggested = latestEnd > Date.parse(`${selectedDate}T08:00:00`) ? timeAt(new Date(latestEnd).toISOString()) : '09:00';
    const [hour, minute] = suggested.split(':').map(Number);
    const end = `${String((hour + 1) % 24).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(minute).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    setOperationError(''); setDraft({ actionId: action.id, date: selectedDate, start: suggested, end });
  }
  function beginEdit(schedule: Schedule) {
    setOperationError(''); setDraft({ actionId: schedule.actionId, schedule, date: actionDate(schedule.startAt), start: timeAt(schedule.startAt), end: timeAt(schedule.endAt) });
  }
  async function saveSchedule(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault(); if (!draft) return;
    setSaving(true); setOperationError('');
    try {
      const startAt = new Date(`${draft.date}T${draft.start}:00`).toISOString();
      const endAt = new Date(`${draft.date}T${draft.end}:00`).toISOString();
      if (Date.parse(endAt) <= Date.parse(startAt)) throw new Error('End time must be after start time.');
      if (draft.schedule) {
        const dateChanged = actionDate(draft.schedule.startAt) !== draft.date;
        const storedDateChanged = new Date(draft.schedule.startAt).toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== startAt.slice(0, 10);
        await api.updateSchedule(draft.schedule.id, { startAt, endAt, ...(dateChanged || storedDateChanged ? { dailyPlanId: null } : {}) });
      } else {
        await api.createSchedule({ actionId: draft.actionId, startAt, endAt, dailyPlanId: null });
      }
      const selectedRangeIncludesDate = draft.date >= range.start && draft.date <= range.end;
      setSelectedDate(draft.date);
      setDraft(null);
      if (selectedRangeIncludesDate) await refresh();
    } catch (error) {
      setOperationError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not save this schedule.');
    } finally { setSaving(false); }
  }
  async function removeSchedule(schedule: Schedule) {
    setOperationError('');
    try { await api.deleteSchedule(schedule.id); await refresh(); }
    catch (error) { setOperationError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not remove this schedule.'); }
  }
  async function updateStatus(action: Action, status: ActionStatus) {
    setSavingAction(action.id); setOperationError('');
    try {
      await api.updateActionStatus(action.id, status);
      onRefreshActions();
    } catch (error) { setOperationError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not update this Action.'); }
    finally { setSavingAction(null); }
  }
  function scheduleCard(schedule: Schedule, compact = false) {
    const action = actions.find((item) => item.id === schedule.actionId);
    if (!action) return <div className="calendar-event calendar-event--orphan" key={schedule.id}>Scheduled Action unavailable</div>;
    const goal = goals.find((item) => item.id === action.goalId);
    const milestone = action.milestoneId ? milestonesByGoal[action.goalId]?.find((item) => item.id === action.milestoneId) : null;
    const isPast = Date.parse(schedule.endAt) < Date.now() && action.status !== 'COMPLETED';
    return <article className={`calendar-event ${action.status === 'COMPLETED' ? 'calendar-event--complete' : ''}`} key={schedule.id}>
      <button type="button" className="calendar-event__main" onClick={() => onOpenGoal(action.goalId)} title="Open Goal">
        <time>{timeAt(schedule.startAt)}–{timeAt(schedule.endAt)}</time><strong>{action.title}</strong>
        {!compact && <small>{goal?.title ?? 'Goal'}{action.milestoneId ? ` · ${milestone?.title ?? 'Milestone'}` : ''}</small>}
        <span className="calendar-event__state">{action.status === 'COMPLETED' ? 'Completed' : isPast ? 'Past scheduled time' : 'Planned'}</span>
      </button>
      {!compact && <div className="calendar-event__actions">
        {action.status !== 'COMPLETED' && <select aria-label={`Update ${action.title} status`} value={action.status} disabled={savingAction === action.id} onChange={(event) => void updateStatus(action, event.target.value as ActionStatus)}>
          <option value="TODO">To do</option><option value="IN_PROGRESS">In progress</option><option value="COMPLETED">Completed</option>
        </select>}
        <button type="button" className="text-link" onClick={() => onLogExecution(action)}>Log execution</button>
        <button type="button" className="text-link" onClick={() => beginEdit(schedule)}>Edit time</button>
        <button type="button" className="text-link" onClick={() => void removeSchedule(schedule)}>Remove</button>
      </div>}
    </article>;
  }
  const periodLabel = view === 'month' ? displayDate(selectedDate, { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })
    : view === 'week' ? `${displayDate(range.start, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })} – ${displayDate(range.end, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })}`
      : displayDate(selectedDate, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });

  return <div className="calendar-workspace">
    <section className="panel calendar-panel">
      <div className="calendar-toolbar">
        <div className="calendar-toolbar__period"><h2>{periodLabel}</h2><div className="calendar-toolbar__nav">
          <Button onClick={() => movePeriod(-1)} ariaLabel="Previous period">‹</Button><Button onClick={() => setSelectedDate(initialDate)}>Today</Button><Button onClick={() => movePeriod(1)} ariaLabel="Next period">›</Button>
        </div></div>
        <div className="calendar-views" aria-label="Calendar view">{(['month', 'week', 'day'] as const).map((item) => <button type="button" className={view === item ? 'calendar-view--active' : ''} key={item} onClick={() => setView(item)}>{item[0].toUpperCase() + item.slice(1)}</button>)}</div>
      </div>
      {loadError && <div className="inline-error" role="alert">Calendar could not load: {loadError} <button type="button" className="text-link" onClick={() => void refresh()}>Try again</button></div>}
      {operationError && <div className="inline-error" role="alert">{operationError}</div>}
      {loading && <p className="calendar-feedback">Loading schedules…</p>}
      {!loading && !loadError && <>
        {view !== 'day' ? <div className={`calendar-grid calendar-grid--${view}`}>
          {view === 'month' && ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'].map((label) => <span className="calendar-grid__weekday" key={label}>{label}</span>)}
          {days.map((day) => {
            const items = sessionsFor(day);
            return <div key={day} className={`calendar-date ${day === selectedDate ? 'calendar-date--selected' : ''} ${dayAtMonth(day) !== dayAtMonth(selectedDate) && view === 'month' ? 'calendar-date--outside' : ''}`}>
              {view === 'week' && <span className="calendar-date__weekday">{displayDate(day, { weekday: 'short' })}</span>}
              <button type="button" className="calendar-date__number" aria-label={`Open ${displayDate(day, { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' })}`} onClick={() => { setSelectedDate(day); setView('day'); }}>{dateAtNoon(day).getDate()}</button>
              {view === 'week' ? <div className="calendar-date__events">{items.map((item) => scheduleCard(item, true))}{!items.length && <small>Open</small>}</div>
                : <div className="calendar-date__events">{items.slice(0, 2).map((item) => scheduleCard(item, true))}{items.length > 2 && <small>+{items.length - 2} more</small>}</div>}
            </div>;
          })}
        </div> : <div className="calendar-day-list">
          <header><span>{displayDate(selectedDate, { weekday: 'long' })}</span><strong>{sessionsFor(selectedDate).length} scheduled</strong></header>
          {sessionsFor(selectedDate).length ? sessionsFor(selectedDate).map((schedule) => scheduleCard(schedule)) : <p className="empty-line">No sessions planned. Choose an Action from the list to schedule it here.</p>}
        </div>}
      </>}
    </section>
    <aside className="panel calendar-unscheduled">
      <div className="panel__heading"><div><span className="eyebrow">NEXT ACTIONS</span><h2>Without a session</h2></div><span>{unscheduled.length}</span></div>
      {!actionsLoaded && !actionsLoadError && <p className="empty-line">Loading Actions…</p>}
      {actionsLoadError && <div className="inline-error" role="alert">Actions unavailable. <button type="button" className="text-link" onClick={onRefreshActions}>Retry</button></div>}
      {actionsLoaded && !actionsLoadError && (unscheduled.length ? <ul>{unscheduled.map((action) => <li key={action.id}>
        <div><strong>{action.title}</strong><button type="button" className="calendar-goal-link" onClick={() => onOpenGoal(action.goalId)}>{goals.find((goal) => goal.id === action.goalId)?.title ?? 'Open Goal'}{action.milestoneId ? ` · ${milestonesByGoal[action.goalId]?.find((milestone) => milestone.id === action.milestoneId)?.title ?? 'Milestone'}` : ''} · {statusLabel(action)}</button></div>
        <Button variant="quiet" onClick={() => beginCreate(action)}>Schedule</Button>
      </li>)}</ul> : <p className="empty-line">Every open Action has a session in this date range.</p>)}
      {draft && <form className="calendar-schedule-form" onSubmit={(event) => void saveSchedule(event)}>
        <strong>{draft.schedule ? 'Move scheduled work' : 'Schedule an Action'}</strong>
        <small>{actions.find((action) => action.id === draft.actionId)?.title}</small>
        <label className="field"><span>Date</span><input type="date" value={draft.date} required onChange={(event) => setDraft({ ...draft, date: event.target.value })} /></label>
        <div className="calendar-schedule-form__times"><label className="field"><span>Start</span><input type="time" value={draft.start} required onChange={(event) => setDraft({ ...draft, start: event.target.value })} /></label><label className="field"><span>End</span><input type="time" value={draft.end} required onChange={(event) => setDraft({ ...draft, end: event.target.value })} /></label></div>
        <div className="calendar-schedule-form__actions"><Button variant="quiet" onClick={() => setDraft(null)}>Cancel</Button><button className="button button--primary" disabled={saving}>{saving ? 'Saving…' : draft.schedule ? 'Save time' : 'Add to calendar'}</button></div>
      </form>}
    </aside>
  </div>;
}

function dayAtMonth(value: string): string { return value.slice(0, 7); }

FILE: backend/src/api/index.ts

import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import type { ApplicationContainer } from "../application/container.ts";
import { Router } from "./router.ts";
import { extractBearerToken, parseJsonBody, sendError } from "./http.ts";
import type { RequestContext } from "./types.ts";
import { registerHealthRoutes } from "./routes/health.routes.ts";
import { registerAuthRoutes } from "./routes/auth.routes.ts";
import { registerWorkspaceRoutes } from "./routes/workspace.routes.ts";
import { registerProjectRoutes } from "./routes/project.routes.ts";
import { registerGoalRoutes } from "./routes/goal.routes.ts";
import { registerMilestoneRoutes } from "./routes/milestone.routes.ts";
import { registerActionRoutes } from "./routes/action.routes.ts";
import { registerPlanningRoutes } from "./routes/planning.routes.ts";
import { registerProgressRoutes } from "./routes/progress.routes.ts";
import { registerAIRoutes } from "./routes/ai.routes.ts";
import { registerMeasurementRoutes } from "./routes/measurement.routes.ts";

export * from "./types.ts";
export * from "./http.ts";
export * from "./router.ts";

export function createRouter(): Router {
  const router = new Router();

  registerHealthRoutes(router);
  registerAuthRoutes(router);
  registerWorkspaceRoutes(router);
  registerProjectRoutes(router);
  registerGoalRoutes(router);
  registerMilestoneRoutes(router);
  registerActionRoutes(router);
  registerPlanningRoutes(router);
  registerProgressRoutes(router);
  registerAIRoutes(router);
  registerMeasurementRoutes(router);

  return router;
}

export function createApiHandler(
  container: ApplicationContainer,
  corsOrigin = "*"
): (req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) => Promise<void> {
  const router = createRouter();

  return async (req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse): Promise<void> => {
    // 1. Handle CORS preflight
    if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
      res.writeHead(204, {
        "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": corsOrigin,
        "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, PATCH, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS",
        "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, Authorization, X-Workspace-Id",
        "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400"
      });
      res.end();
      return;
    }

    try {
      const parsedUrl = new URL(req.url ?? "/", "http://localhost");
      const pathname = parsedUrl.pathname;
      const query = parsedUrl.searchParams;
      const method = req.method ?? "GET";

      const match = router.match(method, pathname);
      if (!match) {
        sendError(
          res,
          404,
          "ROUTE_NOT_FOUND",
          `Route not found: ${method} ${pathname}`,
          corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      // 2. Authentication check
      let user: import("../modules/auth/auth.types.ts").AuthenticatedUserContext | null = null;
      const token = extractBearerToken(req);
      if (token) {
        user = await container.tokenVerifier.verifyToken(token);
        const selectedWorkspaceId = req.headers["x-workspace-id"];
        if (user && typeof selectedWorkspaceId === "string" && selectedWorkspaceId.trim()) {
          user = { ...user, workspaceId: selectedWorkspaceId.trim() };
        }
      }

      if (match.requiresAuth && !user) {
        sendError(
          res,
          401,
          "UNAUTHORIZED",
          "Authentication required. Please provide a valid Bearer token.",
          corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }

      // 3. Body parsing for POST / PATCH / PUT
      let body: unknown = null;
      if (["POST", "PATCH", "PUT"].includes(method)) {
        try {
          body = await parseJsonBody(req);
        } catch (err: unknown) {
          sendError(
            res,
            400,
            "INVALID_JSON_BODY",
            (err as Error)?.message || "Malformed JSON body.",
            corsOrigin
          );
          return;
        }
      }

      // 4. Request context
      const ctx: RequestContext = {
        req,
        res,
        container,
        user,
        params: match.params,
        query,
        body,
        corsOrigin
      };

      // 5. Execute handler
      await match.handler(ctx);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.error("[API Error]", err);
      if (!res.writableEnded) {
        sendError(
          res,
          500,
          "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
          "An unexpected server error occurred.",
          corsOrigin
        );
      }
    }
  };
}




============================================================
FILE: backend/src/api/router.ts
============================================================
import type { HttpMethod, RouteHandler, RouteMatch } from "./types.ts";

interface RouteRegistration {
  method: HttpMethod;
  pattern: RegExp;
  paramNames: string[];
  handler: RouteHandler;
  requiresAuth: boolean;
}

export class Router {
  private readonly routes: RouteRegistration[] = [];

  register(
    method: HttpMethod,
    pathPattern: string,
    handler: RouteHandler,
    options: { requiresAuth?: boolean } = {}
  ): void {
    const paramNames: string[] = [];
    const regexPattern = pathPattern
      .replace(/\/+$/, "") // remove trailing slash
      .replace(/:([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/g, (_, paramName) => {
        paramNames.push(paramName);
        return "([^/]+)";
      });

    const pattern = new RegExp(`^${regexPattern || "/"}$`);
    this.routes.push({
      method,
      pattern,
      paramNames,
      handler,
      requiresAuth: options.requiresAuth ?? true
    });
  }

  get(path: string, handler: RouteHandler, options?: { requiresAuth?: boolean }): void {
    this.register("GET", path, handler, options);
  }

  post(path: string, handler: RouteHandler, options?: { requiresAuth?: boolean }): void {
    this.register("POST", path, handler, options);
  }

  patch(path: string, handler: RouteHandler, options?: { requiresAuth?: boolean }): void {
    this.register("PATCH", path, handler, options);
  }

  delete(path: string, handler: RouteHandler, options?: { requiresAuth?: boolean }): void {
    this.register("DELETE", path, handler, options);
  }

  match(method: string, pathname: string): RouteMatch | null {
    const cleanPath = pathname.length > 1 && pathname.endsWith("/") ? pathname.slice(0, -1) : pathname;

    for (const route of this.routes) {
      if (route.method !== method) continue;

      const match = route.pattern.exec(cleanPath);
      if (match) {
        const params: Record<string, string> = {};
        for (let i = 0; i < route.paramNames.length; i++) {
          params[route.paramNames[i]] = decodeURIComponent(match[i + 1]);
        }
        return {
          handler: route.handler,
          params,
          requiresAuth: route.requiresAuth
        };
      }
    }

    return null;
  }
}

FILE: backend/src/api/routes/project.routes.ts

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type { CreateProjectInput, UpdateProjectInput } from "../../modules/workspace/workspace.types.ts";

export function registerProjectRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/projects",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateProjectInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.createProject(ctx.user!, body);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/projects",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.listProjects(ctx.user!);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/projects/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.getProject(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/projects/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateProjectInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.updateProject(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/projects/:id/overview",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.workspaceService.getProjectOverview(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

FILE: backend/src/api/routes/goal.routes.ts

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type {
  CreateGoalInput,
  GoalScope,
  ListGoalsFilter,
  UpdateGoalInput
} from "../../modules/goal/goal.types.ts";

export function registerGoalRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/goals",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateGoalInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.createGoal(ctx.user!, body);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals/hierarchy",
    async (ctx) => {
      const scope: GoalScope = {};
      const projectId = ctx.query.get("projectId");
      const rootGoalId = ctx.query.get("rootGoalId");
      const includeArchived = ctx.query.get("includeArchived");

      if (projectId) scope.projectId = projectId;
      if (rootGoalId) scope.rootGoalId = rootGoalId;
      if (includeArchived === "true") scope.includeArchived = true;

      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.getGoalHierarchy(ctx.user!, scope);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals",
    async (ctx) => {
      const filter: ListGoalsFilter = {};
      const projectId = ctx.query.get("projectId");
      const parentGoalId = ctx.query.get("parentGoalId");
      const statusParam = ctx.query.get("status");
      const includeArchived = ctx.query.get("includeArchived");

      if (projectId !== null) filter.projectId = projectId;
      if (parentGoalId !== null) filter.parentGoalId = parentGoalId === "null" ? null : parentGoalId;
      if (statusParam) filter.status = statusParam;
      if (includeArchived === "true") filter.includeArchived = true;

      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.listGoals(ctx.user!, filter);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.getGoal(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/goals/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateGoalInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.goalService.updateGoal(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/goals/:id/children",

        ctx.params.id,

        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;

      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);

    { requiresAuth: true }

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type {
  CreateMilestoneInput,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "../../modules/milestone/milestone.types.ts";

export function registerMilestoneRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/milestones",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateMilestoneInput;

      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);

  );

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import type {
  ActionStatus,
  CreateActionInput,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput
} from "../../modules/action/action.types.ts";

export function registerActionRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/actions",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateActionInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.createAction(ctx.user!, body);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/goals/:goalId/actions",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.listActionsByGoal(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.goalId
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/actions",
    async (ctx) => {
      const milestoneId = ctx.query.get("milestoneId");
      const goalId = ctx.query.get("goalId");

      if (milestoneId) {
        const result = await ctx.container.actionService.listActionsByMilestone(
          ctx.user!,
          milestoneId
        );
        if (!result.success) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }

      if (goalId) {
        const result = await ctx.container.actionService.listActionsByGoal(
          ctx.user!,
          goalId
        );
        if (!result.success) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }

      sendError(
        ctx.res,
        400,
        "ACTION_INVALID_INPUT",
        "Either goalId or milestoneId query parameter is required.",
        ctx.corsOrigin
      );
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/actions/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.getAction(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/actions/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateActionInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.updateAction(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/actions/:id/status",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as { status?: ActionStatus };
      if (!body.status) {
        sendError(
          ctx.res,
          400,
          "ACTION_INVALID_STATUS",
          "status field is required.",
          ctx.corsOrigin
        );
        return;
      }
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.updateActionStatus(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body.status
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/actions/:id/complete",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.completeAction(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/actions/:id/execution",
    async (ctx) => {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as RecordExecutionInput;
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.recordExecution(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id,
        body
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/actions/:id/execution",
    async (ctx) => {
      const result = await ctx.container.actionService.getExecutionData(
        ctx.user!,
        ctx.params.id
      );
      if (!result.success) {
        const status = getStatusCodeForError(result.error.code);
        sendError(ctx.res, status, result.error.code, result.error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
        return;
      }
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result.data, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}
