import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import { PlanningError } from "../../modules/planning/planning.errors.ts";
import type {
  ApprovedPlanningProposal,
  CreateDailyPlanInput,
  CreateScheduleInput,
  UpdateDailyPlanInput,
  UpdateScheduleInput
} from "../../modules/planning/planning.types.ts";

export function registerPlanningRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.post(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateDailyPlanInput;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.createDailyPlan(ctx.user!, body);
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans/:date",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.getDailyPlan(ctx.user!, ctx.params.date);
        if (!result) {
          sendError(
            ctx.res,
            404,
            "DAILY_PLAN_NOT_FOUND",
            `Daily Plan not found for date ${ctx.params.date}`,
            ctx.corsOrigin
          );
          return;
        }
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateDailyPlanInput;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.updateDailyPlan(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.id,
          body
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans/:id/actions",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as Omit<CreateScheduleInput, "dailyPlanId">;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.addActionToDailyPlan(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.id,
          body
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.delete(
    "/api/planning/daily-plans/:id/actions/:scheduleId",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.removeActionFromDailyPlan(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.id,
          ctx.params.scheduleId
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/planning/schedules",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateScheduleInput;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.createSchedule(ctx.user!, body);
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/planning/schedules",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const startDate = ctx.query.get("startDate");
        const endDate = ctx.query.get("endDate");
        if (!startDate || !endDate) {
          sendError(
            ctx.res,
            400,
            "PLANNING_INVALID_INPUT",
            "startDate and endDate query parameters are required.",
            ctx.corsOrigin
          );
          return;
        }

        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.getSchedulesByDateRange(
          ctx.user!,
          { startDate, endDate }
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/planning/schedules/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.getSchedule(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.patch(
    "/api/planning/schedules/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateScheduleInput;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.updateSchedule(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.id,
          body
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.delete(
    "/api/planning/schedules/:id",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        await ctx.container.planningService.deleteSchedule(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id);
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, { deleted: true }, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/planning/proposals/apply",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as ApprovedPlanningProposal;
        const result = await ctx.container.planningService.applyApprovedPlanningProposal(
          ctx.user!,
          body
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof PlanningError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PLANNING_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import { ProgressError } from "../../modules/progress/progress.errors.ts";

export function registerProgressRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.get(
    "/api/progress/goals/:goalId",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.progressService.getGoalProgressSummary(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.goalId
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof ProgressError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PROGRESS_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.post(
    "/api/progress/summary",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as { goalIds?: string[] };
        if (!body.goalIds || !Array.isArray(body.goalIds)) {
          sendError(
            ctx.res,
            400,
            "PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT",
            "goalIds array is required.",
            ctx.corsOrigin
          );
          return;
        }

        const result = await ctx.container.progressService.getProgressSummary(
          ctx.user!,
          body.goalIds
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof ProgressError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PROGRESS_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );

  router.get(
    "/api/progress/reviews/:goalId",
    async (ctx) => {
      try {
        const result = await ctx.container.progressService.getProgressDataForReview(
          ctx.user!,
          ctx.params.goalId
        );
        sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (err instanceof ProgressError) {
          const status = getStatusCodeForError(err.code);
          sendError(ctx.res, status, err.code, err.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
          return;
        }
        sendError(ctx.res, 400, "PROGRESS_ERROR", String((err as Error)?.message), ctx.corsOrigin);
      }
    },
    { requiresAuth: true }
  );
}

FILE: backend/src/api/routes/measurement.routes.ts
============================================================
import type { Router } from "../router.ts";
import type { RequestContext } from "../types.ts";
import { getStatusCodeForError, sendError, sendSuccess } from "../http.ts";
import { MeasurementError } from "../../modules/measurement/index.ts";
import type { CreateMeasurementInput, MeasurementHistoryQuery, RecordMeasurementValueInput, UpdateMeasurementInput } from "../../modules/measurement/measurement.types.ts";

function query(ctx: RequestContext): MeasurementHistoryQuery {
  const offset = ctx.query.get("offset");
  const limit = ctx.query.get("limit");
  return {
    ...(ctx.query.has("from") ? { from: ctx.query.get("from")! } : {}),
    ...(ctx.query.has("to") ? { to: ctx.query.get("to")! } : {}),
    ...(offset !== null ? { offset: Number(offset) } : {}),
    ...(limit !== null ? { limit: Number(limit) } : {}),
  };
}

export function registerMeasurementRoutes(router: Router): void {
  router.get("/api/goals/:goalId/measurements", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      const result = await ctx.container.measurementService.listMeasurementsByGoal(ctx.user!, ctx.params.goalId);
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.post("/api/measurements", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      const result = await ctx.container.measurementService.createMeasurement(ctx.user!, (ctx.body ?? {}) as CreateMeasurementInput);
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, result, 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.get("/api/measurements/:id", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.getMeasurement(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.patch("/api/measurements/:id", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.updateMeasurement(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id, (ctx.body ?? {}) as UpdateMeasurementInput), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.delete("/api/measurements/:id", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.archiveMeasurement(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.post("/api/measurements/:id/records", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      const body = (ctx.body ?? {}) as Omit<RecordMeasurementValueInput, "measurementId">;
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.recordMeasurementValue(ctx.user!, { ...body, measurementId: ctx.params.id }), 201, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.get("/api/measurements/:id/current", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.getCurrentMeasurementValue(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });

  router.get("/api/measurements/:id/history", async (ctx) => {
    try {
      sendSuccess(ctx.res, await ctx.container.measurementService.getMeasurementHistory(ctx.user!, ctx.params.id, query(ctx)), 200, ctx.corsOrigin);
    } catch (error) { sendFailure(ctx, error); }
  }, { requiresAuth: true });
}

function sendFailure(ctx: RequestContext, error: unknown): void {
  if (error instanceof MeasurementError) {
    sendError(ctx.res, getStatusCodeForError(error.code), error.code, error.message, ctx.corsOrigin);
    return;
  }
  sendError(ctx.res, 400, "MEASUREMENT_ERROR", "Measurement request could not be completed.", ctx.corsOrigin);
}

FILE: backend/src/modules/workspace/workspace.service.ts
============================================================
import { createWorkspaceError } from "./workspace.errors";
import type { GoalSummaryReader, WorkspaceRepository } from "./workspace.repository";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext,
  CreateProjectInput,
  Project,
  ProjectOverview,
  UpdateProjectInput,
  Workspace,
  WorkspaceOverview,
  WorkspaceResult,
  WorkspaceService
} from "./workspace.types";
import {
  validateCreateProjectInput,
  validateUpdateProjectInput
} from "./workspace.validation";

function success<T>(data: T): WorkspaceResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}

function failure<T>(
  code: Parameters<typeof createWorkspaceError>[0]
): WorkspaceResult<T> {
  return { success: false, error: createWorkspaceError(code) };
}

function isTrustedUser(user: AuthenticatedUserContext | null | undefined): user is AuthenticatedUserContext {
  return Boolean(
    user &&
    user.isAuthenticated === true &&
    typeof user.userId === "string" &&
    user.userId.trim().length > 0
  );
}

function normalizePersistenceError<T>(
  error: unknown,
  fallback: Parameters<typeof createWorkspaceError>[0]
): WorkspaceResult<T> {
  const message =
    typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "message" in error
      ? String((error as { message: unknown }).message).toLowerCase()
      : "";

  if (message.includes("unique") || message.includes("duplicate")) {
    return failure("WORKSPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS");
  }

  return failure(fallback);
}

export function createWorkspaceService(
  repository: WorkspaceRepository,
  goalReader?: GoalSummaryReader
): WorkspaceService {
  async function resolveWorkspace(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Workspace>> {
    if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");

    try {
      const workspace = user.workspaceId
        ? await repository.findWorkspaceById(user.workspaceId)
        : await repository.findWorkspaceByUserId(user.userId);
      if (workspace && workspace.userId !== user.userId) {
        return failure("WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }
      return workspace
        ? success(workspace)
        : failure("WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND");
    } catch (error) {
      return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
    }
  }

  async function resolveOwnedProject(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    projectId: string
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Project>> {
    if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    if (!projectId || !projectId.trim()) return failure("PROJECT_NOT_FOUND");

    try {
      const project = await repository.findProjectById(projectId);
      if (!project) return failure("PROJECT_NOT_FOUND");

      const workspace = user.workspaceId
        ? await repository.findWorkspaceById(user.workspaceId)
        : await repository.findWorkspaceByUserId(user.userId);
      if (!workspace) return failure("WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND");
      if (workspace.userId !== user.userId) return failure("WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED");

      if (project.workspaceId !== workspace.id) {
        return failure("PROJECT_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }

      return success(project);
    } catch (error) {
      return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
    }
  }

  return {
    async listWorkspaces(user) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      try {
        return success(await repository.listWorkspacesByUserId(user.userId));
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async createWorkspace(user, name) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      const normalizedName = typeof name === "string" ? name.trim() : "";
      if (!normalizedName || normalizedName.length > 80) return failure("WORKSPACE_INVALID_NAME");
      try {
        return success(await repository.createWorkspace(user.userId, normalizedName));
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async deleteWorkspace(user, workspaceId) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      if (!workspaceId?.trim()) return failure("WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND");
      try {
        const workspace = await repository.findWorkspaceById(workspaceId);
        if (!workspace) return failure("WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND");
        if (workspace.userId !== user.userId) return failure("WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED");
        return success(await repository.deleteWorkspace(workspaceId, user.userId));
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async initializePersonalWorkspace(user) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");

      try {
        // Atomic get-or-create is delegated to D01; B02 does not trust client owner IDs.
        const workspace = await repository.getOrCreatePersonalWorkspace(user.userId);
        if (workspace.userId !== user.userId) {
          return failure("WORKSPACE_INVALID_OWNER");
        }
        return success(workspace);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_INITIALIZATION_FAILED");
      }
    },

    async getCurrentWorkspace(user) {
      return resolveWorkspace(user);
    },

    async getWorkspaceOverview(user): Promise<WorkspaceResult<WorkspaceOverview>> {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        const projects = await repository.listProjectsByWorkspaceId(
          workspaceResult.data.id
        );

        const goalSummary = goalReader
          ? {
              independentGoalCount: await goalReader.getIndependentGoalCount(
                workspaceResult.data.id
              )
            }
          : null;

        return success({
          workspace: workspaceResult.data,
          projects,
          goalSummary
        });
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async createProject(user, input: CreateProjectInput) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");

      const validation = validateCreateProjectInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        const project = await repository.createProject(
          workspaceResult.data.id,
          {
            name: input.name.trim(),
            description:
              input.description === undefined || input.description === null
                ? null
                : input.description.trim() || null
          }
        );

        if (project.workspaceId !== workspaceResult.data.id) {
          return failure("WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
        }

        return success(project);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "PROJECT_CREATION_FAILED");
      }
    },

    async getProject(user, projectId) {
      return resolveOwnedProject(user, projectId);
    },

    async listProjects(user) {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        const projects = await repository.listProjectsByWorkspaceId(
          workspaceResult.data.id
        );

        return success(
          [...projects].sort((a, b) =>
            b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt)
          )
        );
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async updateProject(user, projectId, input: UpdateProjectInput) {
      const ownedProject = await resolveOwnedProject(user, projectId);
      if (!ownedProject.success) return ownedProject;

      const validation = validateUpdateProjectInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const safeInput: UpdateProjectInput = {};
      if (input.name !== undefined) safeInput.name = input.name.trim();
      if (input.description !== undefined) {
        safeInput.description =
          input.description === null ? null : input.description.trim() || null;
      }

      try {
        const updated = await repository.updateProject(projectId, safeInput);

        // A repository implementation must not permit workspace_id or id updates.
        if (
          updated.id !== ownedProject.data.id ||
          updated.workspaceId !== ownedProject.data.workspaceId
        ) {
          return failure("PROJECT_UPDATE_FAILED");
        }

        return success(updated);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "PROJECT_UPDATE_FAILED");
      }
    },

    async getProjectOverview(user, projectId): Promise<WorkspaceResult<ProjectOverview>> {
      const projectResult = await resolveOwnedProject(user, projectId);
      if (!projectResult.success) return projectResult;

      try {
        const goalSummary = goalReader
          ? {
              goalCount: await goalReader.getProjectGoalCount(projectId)
            }
          : null;

        return success({
          project: projectResult.data,
          goalSummary
        });
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizePersistenceError(error, "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR");
      }
    },

    async validateWorkspaceOwnership(user, workspaceId) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED");

      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      if (workspaceResult.data.id !== workspaceId) {
        return failure("WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }

      return success(true);
    },

    async validateProjectOwnership(user, projectId) {
      const result = await resolveOwnedProject(user, projectId);
      if (!result.success) return result;
      return success(true);
    }
  };
}

import type {
  CreateProjectInput,
  Project,
  UpdateProjectInput,
  Workspace
} from "./workspace.types";

/**
 * D01 persistence boundary.
 *
 * getOrCreatePersonalWorkspace MUST be atomic and return a deterministic existing
 * personal workspace or create one when the owner has no workspace yet.
 */
export interface WorkspaceRepository {
  getOrCreatePersonalWorkspace(userId: string): Promise<Workspace>;
  findWorkspaceByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Workspace | null>;
  findWorkspaceById(workspaceId: string): Promise<Workspace | null>;
  listWorkspacesByUserId(userId: string): Promise<Workspace[]>;
  createWorkspace(userId: string, name: string): Promise<Workspace>;
  deleteWorkspace(workspaceId: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean>;

  createProject(
    workspaceId: string,
    input: CreateProjectInput
  ): Promise<Project>;

  findProjectById(projectId: string): Promise<Project | null>;
  listProjectsByWorkspaceId(workspaceId: string): Promise<Project[]>;

  updateProject(
    projectId: string,
    input: UpdateProjectInput
  ): Promise<Project>;
}

/**
 * B03 read-only boundary. B02 must not own or mutate Goal entities.
 * Return null from the service overview when B03 has not been connected yet.
 */
export interface GoalSummaryReader {
  getIndependentGoalCount(workspaceId: string): Promise<number>;
  getProjectGoalCount(projectId: string): Promise<number>;
}

import type { AuthenticatedUserContext } from "../auth/auth.types";

export type { AuthenticatedUserContext };

export interface Workspace {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  name: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface Project {
  id: string;
  workspaceId: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface CreateProjectInput {
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
}

export interface UpdateProjectInput {
  name?: string;
  description?: string | null;
}

export interface WorkspaceGoalSummary {
  independentGoalCount: number;
}

export interface ProjectGoalSummary {
  goalCount: number;
}

export interface WorkspaceOverview {
  workspace: Workspace;
  projects: Project[];
  goalSummary: WorkspaceGoalSummary | null;
}

export interface ProjectOverview {
  project: Project;
  goalSummary: ProjectGoalSummary | null;
}

export type WorkspaceErrorCode =
  | "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND"
  | "WORKSPACE_ALREADY_EXISTS"
  | "WORKSPACE_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "WORKSPACE_INITIALIZATION_FAILED"
  | "WORKSPACE_INVALID_OWNER"
  | "WORKSPACE_INVALID_NAME"
  | "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND"
  | "PROJECT_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "PROJECT_INVALID_NAME"
  | "PROJECT_INVALID_INPUT"
  | "PROJECT_CREATION_FAILED"
  | "PROJECT_UPDATE_FAILED"
  | "WORKSPACE_PROVIDER_ERROR"
  | "WORKSPACE_UNKNOWN_ERROR"
  | "WORKSPACE_UNAUTHENTICATED";

export interface WorkspaceError {
  code: WorkspaceErrorCode;
  message: string;
}

export type WorkspaceResult<T> =
  | { success: true; data: T }
  | { success: false; error: WorkspaceError };

export interface WorkspaceService {
  listWorkspaces(user: AuthenticatedUserContext): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Workspace[]>>;
  createWorkspace(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, name: string): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Workspace>>;
  deleteWorkspace(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, workspaceId: string): Promise<WorkspaceResult<boolean>>;
  initializePersonalWorkspace(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Workspace>>;

  getCurrentWorkspace(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Workspace>>;

  getWorkspaceOverview(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<WorkspaceOverview>>;

  createProject(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    input: CreateProjectInput
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Project>>;

  getProject(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    projectId: string
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Project>>;

  listProjects(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Project[]>>;

  updateProject(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    projectId: string,
    input: UpdateProjectInput
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<Project>>;

  getProjectOverview(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    projectId: string
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<ProjectOverview>>;

  validateWorkspaceOwnership(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    workspaceId: string
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<boolean>>;

  validateProjectOwnership(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    projectId: string
  ): Promise<WorkspaceResult<boolean>>;
}

FILE: backend/src/modules/goal/goal.service.ts
============================================================
import { createGoalError } from "./goal.errors";
import type { GoalRepository, GoalWorkspaceAccess } from "./goal.repository";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext,
  CreateGoalInput,
  Goal,
  GoalHierarchyNode,
  GoalResult,
  GoalScope,
  GoalService,
  ListGoalsFilter,
  UpdateGoalInput
} from "./goal.types";
import {
  validateCreateGoalInput,
  validateUpdateGoalInput
} from "./goal.validation";

function success<T>(data: T): GoalResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}

function failure<T>(
  code: Parameters<typeof createGoalError>[0]
): GoalResult<T> {
  return { success: false, error: createGoalError(code) };
}

function isTrustedUser(
  user: AuthenticatedUserContext | null | undefined
): user is AuthenticatedUserContext {
  return Boolean(
    user &&
    user.isAuthenticated === true &&
    typeof user.userId === "string" &&
    user.userId.trim().length > 0
  );
}

function normalizeError<T>(error: unknown): GoalResult<T> {
  const message =
    typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "message" in error
      ? String((error as { message: unknown }).message).toLowerCase()
      : "";
  if (message.includes("not found")) return failure("GOAL_NOT_FOUND");
  return failure("GOAL_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
}

function buildHierarchy(goals: Goal[]): GoalResult<GoalHierarchyNode[]> {
  const byId = new Map<string, GoalHierarchyNode>();
  for (const goal of goals) {
    if (byId.has(goal.id)) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
    byId.set(goal.id, { goal, children: [] });
  }

  const roots: GoalHierarchyNode[] = [];
  for (const goal of goals) {
    const node = byId.get(goal.id)!;
    if (goal.parentGoalId === null) {
      roots.push(node);
      continue;
    }

    const parent = byId.get(goal.parentGoalId);
    if (!parent) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
    parent.children.push(node);
  }

  const visiting = new Set<string>();
  const visited = new Set<string>();

  function visit(node: GoalHierarchyNode): boolean {
    if (visiting.has(node.goal.id)) return false;
    if (visited.has(node.goal.id)) return true;
    visiting.add(node.goal.id);
    for (const child of node.children) {
      if (!visit(child)) return false;
    }
    visiting.delete(node.goal.id);
    visited.add(node.goal.id);
    return true;
  }

  for (const root of roots) {
    if (!visit(root)) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
  }
  if (visited.size !== goals.length) {
    return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
  }

  return success(roots);
}

export function createGoalService(
  repository: GoalRepository,
  workspaceAccess: GoalWorkspaceAccess
): GoalService {
  async function resolveWorkspace(user: AuthenticatedUserContext) {
    if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure<string>("GOAL_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    try {
      const workspaceId = await workspaceAccess.getCurrentWorkspaceId(user.userId, user.workspaceId);
      return workspaceId
        ? success(workspaceId)
        : failure<string>("GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
    } catch {
      return failure<string>("GOAL_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
    }
  }

  async function resolveOwnedGoal(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    goalId: string
  ): Promise<GoalResult<{ goal: Goal; workspaceId: string }>> {
    if (!isTrustedUser(user)) {
      return failure("GOAL_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    }
    if (!goalId?.trim()) return failure("GOAL_NOT_FOUND");

    try {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      const goal = await repository.findGoalById(goalId);
      if (!goal) return failure("GOAL_NOT_FOUND");
      if (goal.workspaceId !== workspaceResult.data) {
        return failure("GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }
      return success({ goal, workspaceId: workspaceResult.data });
    } catch (error) {
      return normalizeError(error);
    }
  }

  async function validateParent(
    workspaceId: string,
    parentGoalId: string,
    childProjectId: string | null
  ): Promise<GoalResult<Goal>> {
    try {
      const parent = await repository.findGoalById(parentGoalId);
      if (!parent) return failure("GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND");
      if (parent.workspaceId !== workspaceId) {
        return failure("GOAL_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH");
      }
      if (parent.archivedAt !== null) return failure("GOAL_PARENT_INVALID");
      if (parent.projectId !== childProjectId) {
        return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
      }
      return success(parent);
    } catch (error) {
      return normalizeError(error);
    }
  }

  async function wouldCreateCycle(
    parentGoalId: string,
    childGoalId: string
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (parentGoalId === childGoalId) return true;
    const descendants = await repository.findDescendantIds(childGoalId);
    return descendants.includes(parentGoalId);
  }

  return {
    async createGoal(user, input: CreateGoalInput) {
      if (!isTrustedUser(user)) return failure("GOAL_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      const validation = validateCreateGoalInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;
      const workspaceId = workspaceResult.data;

      try {
        const projectId = input.projectId ?? null;
        if (projectId) {
          const allowed = await workspaceAccess.validateProjectInWorkspace(
            user.userId, workspaceId, projectId
          );
          if (!allowed) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
        }

        const parentGoalId = input.parentGoalId ?? null;
        if (parentGoalId) {
          const parentResult = await validateParent(
            workspaceId, parentGoalId, projectId
          );
          if (!parentResult.success) return parentResult;
        }

        const goal = await repository.createGoal(workspaceId, {
          title: input.title.trim(),
          description:
            input.description === undefined || input.description === null
              ? null
              : input.description.trim() || null,
          targetDate: input.targetDate ?? null,
          projectId,
          parentGoalId
        });

        if (
          goal.workspaceId !== workspaceId ||
          goal.projectId !== projectId ||
          goal.parentGoalId !== parentGoalId
        ) {
          return failure("GOAL_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return success(goal);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    },

    async getGoal(user, goalId) {
      const result = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, goalId);
      return result.success ? success(result.data.goal) : result;
    },

    async listGoals(user, filter: ListGoalsFilter = {}) {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        if (filter.projectId) {
          const allowed = await workspaceAccess.validateProjectInWorkspace(
            user.userId, workspaceResult.data, filter.projectId
          );
          if (!allowed) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
        }

        const goals = await repository.listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
          workspaceResult.data,
          filter
        );
        return success(goals);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    },

    async updateGoal(user, goalId, input: UpdateGoalInput) {
      const owned = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, goalId);
      if (!owned.success) return owned;

      const validation = validateUpdateGoalInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const safeInput: UpdateGoalInput = {};
      if (input.title !== undefined) safeInput.title = input.title.trim();
      if (input.description !== undefined) {
        safeInput.description =
          input.description === null ? null : input.description.trim() || null;
      }
      if (input.targetDate !== undefined) safeInput.targetDate = input.targetDate;

      try {
        const updated = await repository.updateGoal(goalId, safeInput);
        if (
          updated.id !== owned.data.goal.id ||
          updated.workspaceId !== owned.data.goal.workspaceId ||
          updated.projectId !== owned.data.goal.projectId ||
          updated.parentGoalId !== owned.data.goal.parentGoalId
        ) {
          return failure("GOAL_INVALID_HIERARCHY");
        }
        return success(updated);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    },

    async addChildGoal(user, parentGoalId, input) {
      const parentResult = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, parentGoalId);
      if (!parentResult.success) return parentResult;
      return this.createGoal(user, {
        ...input,
        projectId: parentResult.data.goal.projectId,
        parentGoalId
      });
    },

    async getGoalHierarchy(user, scope: GoalScope = {}) {
      const workspaceResult = await resolveWorkspace(user);
      if (!workspaceResult.success) return workspaceResult;

      try {
        if (scope.projectId) {
          const allowed = await workspaceAccess.validateProjectInWorkspace(
            user.userId, workspaceResult.data, scope.projectId
          );
          if (!allowed) return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
        }

        if (scope.rootGoalId) {
          const rootResult = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, scope.rootGoalId);
          if (!rootResult.success) return rootResult;
        }

        const goals = await repository.listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
          workspaceResult.data,
          {
            projectId: scope.projectId,
            includeArchived: scope.includeArchived
          }
        );

        const scopedGoals = scope.rootGoalId
          ? goals.filter((goal) => goal.id === scope.rootGoalId ||
              goal.parentGoalId === scope.rootGoalId ||
              goals.some((candidate) =>
                candidate.id === goal.parentGoalId &&
                candidate.parentGoalId === scope.rootGoalId
              ))
          : goals;

        return buildHierarchy(scopedGoals);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    },

    async validateParentChild(user, parentGoalId, childGoalId) {
      const parentResult = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, parentGoalId);
      if (!parentResult.success) return parentResult;
      const childResult = await resolveOwnedGoal(user, childGoalId);
      if (!childResult.success) return childResult;

      const parent = parentResult.data.goal;
      const child = childResult.data.goal;

      if (parent.id === child.id) return failure("GOAL_CIRCULAR_REFERENCE");
      if (parent.workspaceId !== child.workspaceId) {
        return failure("GOAL_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH");
      }
      if (parent.projectId !== child.projectId) {
        return failure("GOAL_INVALID_PLACEMENT");
      }

      try {
        if (await wouldCreateCycle(parent.id, child.id)) {
          return failure("GOAL_CIRCULAR_REFERENCE");
        }
        return success(true);
      } catch (error) {
        return normalizeError(error);
      }
    }
  };
}

/**

    userId: string,

import type { CreateGoalInput, UpdateGoalInput } from "./goal.types";

const MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 160;
const MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH = 5000;

export interface GoalValidationResult {
  valid: boolean;
  code?: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" | "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT";
}

function validOptionalText(value: unknown, maxLength: number): boolean {
  return value === undefined || value === null ||
    (typeof value === "string" && value.length <= maxLength);
}

function validDate(value: unknown): boolean {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return true;
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim() === "") return false;
  return !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value));
}

export function validateCreateGoalInput(
  input: CreateGoalInput
): GoalValidationResult {
  if (
    typeof input.title !== "string" ||
    input.title.trim().length === 0 ||
    input.title.trim().length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" };
  }

  if (
    !validOptionalText(input.description, MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH) ||
    !validDate(input.targetDate) ||
    (input.projectId !== undefined && input.projectId !== null &&
      (typeof input.projectId !== "string" || !input.projectId.trim())) ||
    (input.parentGoalId !== undefined && input.parentGoalId !== null &&
      (typeof input.parentGoalId !== "string" || !input.parentGoalId.trim()))
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

export function validateUpdateGoalInput(
  input: UpdateGoalInput
): GoalValidationResult {
  if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  if (
    input.title !== undefined &&
    (typeof input.title !== "string" ||
      input.title.trim().length === 0 ||
      input.title.trim().length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" };
  }

  if (
    !validOptionalText(input.description, MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH) ||
    !validDate(input.targetDate)
  ) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

FILE: backend/src/domain/goal-hierarchy/goal.hierarchy.ts
============================================================
import type {
  C03ValidationResult,
  Goal,
  GoalId,
  GoalReader,
} from "./goal.types";

export type HierarchyError =
  | "GOAL_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_SELF_PARENTING"
  | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"
  | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_POLICY_UNRESOLVED";

function sameWorkspace(parent: Goal, child: Goal): boolean {
  return parent.workspaceId === child.workspaceId;
}

/**
 * Enforces confirmed Workspace boundary. Different Project scopes within the
 * same Workspace remain blocked because GOAL-006 is OPEN.
 */
export function validateParentRelationship(
  childGoalId: GoalId,
  proposedParentGoalId: GoalId | null,
  reader: GoalReader,
): C03ValidationResult<HierarchyError> {
  const child = reader.getGoal(childGoalId);
  if (!child) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (proposedParentGoalId === null) return { valid: true };
  if (proposedParentGoalId === childGoalId) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SELF_PARENTING" };
  }

  const parent = reader.getGoal(proposedParentGoalId);
  if (!parent) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND" };
  if (!sameWorkspace(parent, child)) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" };
  }
  if (parent.projectId !== child.projectId) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_POLICY_UNRESOLVED" };
  }

  const visited = new Set<GoalId>([childGoalId]);
  let cursor: Goal | null = parent;

  while (cursor) {
    if (visited.has(cursor.id)) {
      return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" };
    }
    visited.add(cursor.id);
    if (cursor.parentGoalId === null) break;
    cursor = reader.getGoal(cursor.parentGoalId);
    if (!cursor) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

export function getGoalAncestors(
  goalId: GoalId,
  reader: GoalReader,
): C03ValidationResult<"GOAL_NOT_FOUND" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"> & {
  readonly goals?: readonly Goal[];
} {
  const start = reader.getGoal(goalId);
  if (!start) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };

  const ancestors: Goal[] = [];
  const visited = new Set<GoalId>([goalId]);
  let parentId = start.parentGoalId;

  while (parentId !== null) {
    if (visited.has(parentId)) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" };
    visited.add(parentId);
    const parent = reader.getGoal(parentId);
    if (!parent) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" };
    ancestors.push(parent);
    parentId = parent.parentGoalId;
  }

  return { valid: true, goals: ancestors };
}

export function getGoalDescendants(
  goalId: GoalId,
  reader: GoalReader,
): C03ValidationResult<"GOAL_NOT_FOUND" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"> & {
  readonly goals?: readonly Goal[];
} {
  const root = reader.getGoal(goalId);
  if (!root) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };

  const all = reader.getGoals();
  const childrenByParent = new Map<GoalId, Goal[]>();
  for (const goal of all) {
    if (goal.parentGoalId === null) continue;
    const list = childrenByParent.get(goal.parentGoalId) ?? [];
    list.push(goal);
    childrenByParent.set(goal.parentGoalId, list);
  }

  const descendants: Goal[] = [];
  const visited = new Set<GoalId>([goalId]);
  const stack = [...(childrenByParent.get(goalId) ?? [])].reverse();

  while (stack.length > 0) {
    const current = stack.pop()!;
    if (visited.has(current.id)) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" };
    visited.add(current.id);
    descendants.push(current);
    const children = childrenByParent.get(current.id) ?? [];
    for (let i = children.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) stack.push(children[i]);
  }

  // A dangling parent reference is invalid when encountered in the traversed tree.
  for (const goal of descendants) {
    if (goal.parentGoalId && !reader.getGoal(goal.parentGoalId)) {
      return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" };
    }
  }
  return { valid: true, goals: descendants };
}

export interface GoalTreeNode {
  readonly goal: Goal;
  readonly children: readonly GoalTreeNode[];
}

export function buildGoalTree(
  rootGoalId: GoalId,
  reader: GoalReader,
): C03ValidationResult<"GOAL_NOT_FOUND" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID" | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"> & {
  readonly tree?: GoalTreeNode;
} {
  const root = reader.getGoal(rootGoalId);
  if (!root) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };

  const all = reader.getGoals();
  const childrenByParent = new Map<GoalId, Goal[]>();
  for (const goal of all) {
    if (goal.parentGoalId === null) continue;
    const children = childrenByParent.get(goal.parentGoalId) ?? [];
    children.push(goal);
    childrenByParent.set(goal.parentGoalId, children);
  }

  const visiting = new Set<GoalId>();
  const build = (goal: Goal): GoalTreeNode | null => {
    if (visiting.has(goal.id)) return null;
    visiting.add(goal.id);
    const children: GoalTreeNode[] = [];
    for (const child of childrenByParent.get(goal.id) ?? []) {
      const node = build(child);
      if (!node) return null;
      children.push(node);
    }
    visiting.delete(goal.id);
    return { goal, children };
  };

  const tree = build(root);
  if (!tree) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" };
  return { valid: true, tree };
}

/**
 * C03 — Goal & Hierarchy domain contracts.
 * Identifier formats remain opaque until D03 standardizes shared identifiers.
 */
export type GoalId = string;
export type UserId = string;
export type WorkspaceId = string;
export type ProjectId = string;

export type GoalStatus = string;
/**
 * The specification has not approved GoalStatus values. This alias deliberately
 * does not publish an enum or imply lifecycle transitions.
 */

export interface GoalScope {
  readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId;
  /** null = independent Workspace Goal; non-null = Project-owned Goal. */
  readonly projectId: ProjectId | null;
}

export interface Goal {
  readonly id: GoalId;
  readonly workspaceId: WorkspaceId;
  readonly projectId: ProjectId | null;
  readonly parentGoalId: GoalId | null;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly description: string | null;
  /** Calendar date (YYYY-MM-DD), not a timestamp or Action Schedule. */
  readonly targetDate: string | null;
  /** Status remains opaque until GOAL-001 is approved. */
  readonly status?: GoalStatus;
  readonly createdAt: string;
  readonly updatedAt: string;
}

export interface GoalReader {
  getGoal(id: GoalId): Goal | null;
  getGoals(): readonly Goal[];
}

export interface GoalScopeValidator {
  validate(
    callerUserId: UserId,
    scope: GoalScope,
  ): { readonly valid: true } | { readonly valid: false; readonly code: string };
}

export interface GoalOwnershipValidator {
  validateGoalOwnership(
    callerUserId: UserId,
    goalId: GoalId,
  ): { readonly valid: true } | { readonly valid: false; readonly code: string };
}

export type C03ValidationResult<TCode extends string> =
  | { readonly valid: true }
  | { readonly valid: false; readonly code: TCode };

FILE: backend/src/domain/goal-hierarchy/goal.validation.ts

import type {
  C03ValidationResult,
  Goal,
  GoalId,
  GoalScope,
  GoalScopeValidator,
} from "./goal.types";

export type GoalAttributeError =
  | "GOAL_INVALID_ID"
  | "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE"
  | "GOAL_INVALID_DESCRIPTION"
  | "GOAL_INVALID_TARGET_DATE";

const DATE_ONLY = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

function isRealCalendarDate(value: string): boolean {
  if (!DATE_ONLY.test(value)) return false;
  const [year, month, day] = value.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  return date.getUTCFullYear() === year
    && date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1
    && date.getUTCDate() === day;
}

/** Only constraints explicitly supported by C03 are enforced. Length limits remain OPEN. */
export function validateGoalAttributes(input: {
  readonly id: GoalId;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly description?: string | null;
  readonly targetDate?: string | null;
}): C03ValidationResult<GoalAttributeError> {
  if (typeof input.id !== "string" || input.id.trim().length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_ID" };
  }
  if (typeof input.title !== "string" || input.title.trim().length === 0) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" };
  }
  if (input.description !== undefined && input.description !== null
      && typeof input.description !== "string") {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_DESCRIPTION" };
  }
  if (input.targetDate !== undefined && input.targetDate !== null
      && !isRealCalendarDate(input.targetDate)) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TARGET_DATE" };
  }
  return { valid: true };
}

export function toGoalScope(goal: Pick<Goal, "workspaceId" | "projectId">): GoalScope {
  return { workspaceId: goal.workspaceId, projectId: goal.projectId };
}

/** Delegates Workspace/Project truth to C02; C03 does not duplicate those rules. */
export function validateGoalScope(
  callerUserId: string,
  scope: GoalScope,
  validator: GoalScopeValidator,
): C03ValidationResult<"GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID" | "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED"> {
  if (!scope || typeof scope.workspaceId !== "string" || scope.workspaceId.trim() === ""
      || (scope.projectId !== null && (typeof scope.projectId !== "string" || scope.projectId.trim() === ""))) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID" };
  }
  const result = validator.validate(callerUserId, scope);
  if (result.valid) return { valid: true };
  return {
    valid: false,
    code: result.code.includes("ACCESS_DENIED") ? "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED" : "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID",
  };
}




============================================================
FILE: backend/src/domain/goal-hierarchy/goal.movement.ts
============================================================
import type {
  Goal,
  GoalId,
  GoalOwnershipValidator,
  GoalReader,
  GoalScope,
  GoalScopeValidator,
  UserId,
  C03ValidationResult,
} from "./goal.types";
import { validateGoalScope } from "./goal.validation";
import { validateParentRelationship } from "./goal.hierarchy";

export interface GoalScopeMovementRequest {
  readonly callerUserId: UserId;
  readonly goalId: GoalId;
  readonly destinationScope: GoalScope;
  readonly proposedParentGoalId?: GoalId | null;
}

export type GoalScopeMovementError =
  | "GOAL_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_OWNERSHIP_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_ACCESS_DENIED"
  | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_INVALID"
  | "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE"
  | "GOAL_SELF_PARENTING"
  | "GOAL_PARENT_NOT_FOUND"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_POLICY_UNRESOLVED"
  | "GOAL_SCOPE_MOVEMENT_BLOCKED"
  | "GOAL_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT";

export interface GoalMovementDependencyReader {
  hasBlockingDependencies(goalId: GoalId): boolean;
}

/**
 * Validation contract only. This function never mutates Goal data and does not
 * claim transaction/atomicity guarantees. The Application Layer/D01 must own
 * the eventual movement transaction or recovery strategy.
 */
export function validateGoalScopeMovement(
  request: GoalScopeMovementRequest,
  goals: GoalReader,
  ownership: GoalOwnershipValidator,
  scopes: GoalScopeValidator,
  dependencies: GoalMovementDependencyReader,
): C03ValidationResult<GoalScopeMovementError> {
  const goal: Goal | null = goals.getGoal(request.goalId);
  if (!goal) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND" };

  const ownerResult = ownership.validateGoalOwnership(request.callerUserId, request.goalId);
  if (!ownerResult.valid) return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_OWNERSHIP_INVALID" };

  const destination = validateGoalScope(request.callerUserId, request.destinationScope, scopes);
  if (!destination.valid) return destination;

  if (request.proposedParentGoalId !== undefined) {
    // Validate hierarchy against the proposed destination scope, not the current scope.
    const destinationAwareReader: GoalReader = {
      getGoal: (id) => {
        const found = goals.getGoal(id);
        if (!found || found.id !== request.goalId) return found;
        return {
          ...found,
          workspaceId: request.destinationScope.workspaceId,
          projectId: request.destinationScope.projectId,
        };
      },
      getGoals: () => goals.getGoals().map((found) => found.id === request.goalId
        ? { ...found, workspaceId: request.destinationScope.workspaceId, projectId: request.destinationScope.projectId }
        : found),
    };
    const hierarchy = validateParentRelationship(
      request.goalId,
      request.proposedParentGoalId,
      destinationAwareReader,
    );
    if (!hierarchy.valid) return hierarchy;
  }

  if (dependencies.hasBlockingDependencies(request.goalId)) {
    return { valid: false, code: "GOAL_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT" };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

import { createMilestoneError } from "./milestone.errors";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext,
  CreateMilestoneInput,
  Milestone,
  MilestoneResult,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "./milestone.types";
import type {
  MilestoneGoalAccess,
  MilestoneRepository
} from "./milestone.repository";
import {
  validateCreateMilestoneInput,
  validateUpdateMilestoneInput
} from "./milestone.validation";

function success<T>(data: T): MilestoneResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}

function failure<T>(
  code: Parameters<typeof createMilestoneError>[0]
): MilestoneResult<T> {
  return { success: false, error: createMilestoneError(code) };
}

function isAuthenticated(
  user: AuthenticatedUserContext | null | undefined
): user is AuthenticatedUserContext {
  return Boolean(
    user &&
    user.isAuthenticated === true &&
    typeof user.userId === "string" &&
    user.userId.trim().length > 0
  );
}

function normalizePersistenceError<T>(): MilestoneResult<T> {
  return failure("MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
}

export interface MilestoneService {
  createMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    input: CreateMilestoneInput
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>>;

  getMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    milestoneId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>>;

  listMilestonesByGoal(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    goalId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone[]>>;

  updateMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    milestoneId: string,
    input: UpdateMilestoneInput
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>>;

  completeMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    milestoneId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>>;
}

export function createMilestoneService(
  repository: MilestoneRepository,
  goalAccess: MilestoneGoalAccess,
  now: () => string = () => new Date().toISOString()
): MilestoneService {
  async function resolveMilestone(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    milestoneId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<Milestone>> {
    if (!isAuthenticated(user)) {
      return failure("MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    }
    if (!milestoneId?.trim()) return failure("MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND");

    try {
      const milestone = await repository.findMilestoneById(milestoneId);
      if (!milestone) return failure("MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND");

      const goalResult = await goalAccess.getGoalForUser(user, milestone.goalId);
      if (!goalResult.success) {
        if (goalResult.error.code === "GOAL_NOT_FOUND") {
          return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND");
        }
        return failure("MILESTONE_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }

      return success(milestone);
    } catch {
      return normalizePersistenceError();
    }
  }

  async function validateGoalAccess(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext,
    goalId: string
  ): Promise<MilestoneResult<true>> {
    try {
      const result = await goalAccess.getGoalForUser(user, goalId);
      if (result.success) return success(true);
      if (result.error.code === "GOAL_NOT_FOUND") {
        return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND");
      }
      if (result.error.code === "GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED") {
        return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
      }
      return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_INVALID");
    } catch {
      return normalizePersistenceError();
    }
  }

  return {
    async createMilestone(user, input) {
      if (!isAuthenticated(user)) {
        return failure("MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      }

      const validation = validateCreateMilestoneInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT");
      }

      const goalResult = await validateGoalAccess(user, input.goalId);
      if (!goalResult.success) return goalResult;

      try {
        const milestone = await repository.createMilestone({
          goalId: input.goalId,
          title: input.title.trim(),
          description:
            input.description === undefined || input.description === null
              ? null
              : input.description.trim() || null,
          targetDate: input.targetDate ?? null
        });
        if (milestone.goalId !== input.goalId) {
          return failure("MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return success(milestone);
      } catch {
        return normalizePersistenceError();
      }
    },

    async getMilestone(user, milestoneId) {
      return resolveMilestone(user, milestoneId);
    },

    async listMilestonesByGoal(user, goalId) {
      if (!isAuthenticated(user)) {
        return failure("MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      }
      if (!goalId?.trim()) return failure("MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND");

      const goalResult = await validateGoalAccess(user, goalId);
      if (!goalResult.success) return goalResult;

      try {
        return success(await repository.listMilestonesByGoal(goalId));
      } catch {
        return normalizePersistenceError();
      }
    },

    async updateMilestone(user, milestoneId, input) {
      const existing = await resolveMilestone(user, milestoneId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;

      const validation = validateUpdateMilestoneInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        return failure(validation.code ?? "MILESTONE_INVALID_INPUT");
      }
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") {
        return failure("MILESTONE_UPDATE_NOT_ALLOWED");
      }

      const safeInput: UpdateMilestoneInput = {};
      if (input.title !== undefined) safeInput.title = input.title.trim();
      if (input.description !== undefined) {
        safeInput.description =
          input.description === null ? null : input.description.trim() || null;
      }
      if (input.targetDate !== undefined) safeInput.targetDate = input.targetDate;

      try {
        const updated = await repository.updateMilestone(milestoneId, safeInput);
        if (
          updated.id !== existing.data.id ||
          updated.goalId !== existing.data.goalId
        ) {
          return failure("MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return success(updated);
      } catch {
        return normalizePersistenceError();
      }
    },

    async completeMilestone(user, milestoneId) {
      const existing = await resolveMilestone(user, milestoneId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") {
        return failure("MILESTONE_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
      }

      try {
        const completed = await repository.completeMilestone(
          milestoneId,
          now()
        );
        if (
          completed.id !== existing.data.id ||
          completed.goalId !== existing.data.goalId ||
          completed.status !== "COMPLETED" ||
          completed.completedAt === null
        ) {
          return failure("MILESTONE_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return success(completed);
      } catch {
        return normalizePersistenceError();
      }
    }
  };
}

import type {
  CreateMilestoneInput,
  Milestone,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "./milestone.types";

/**
 * D01 boundary. A production adapter must enforce the Goal foreign key
 * and persist completion timestamps consistently.
 */
export interface MilestoneRepository {
  createMilestone(input: {
    goalId: string;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    targetDate: string | null;
  }): Promise<Milestone>;

  findMilestoneById(milestoneId: string): Promise<Milestone | null>;

  listMilestonesByGoal(goalId: string): Promise<Milestone[]>;

  updateMilestone(
    milestoneId: string,
    input: UpdateMilestoneInput
  ): Promise<Milestone>;

  completeMilestone(
    milestoneId: string,
    completedAt: string
  ): Promise<Milestone>;
}

/**
 * B03 boundary. Use B03's GoalService.getGoal() to resolve the Goal
 * through the authenticated user context. Do not query B03 tables directly.
 */
export interface MilestoneGoalAccess {
  getGoalForUser(
    user: { userId: string; email: string | null; isAuthenticated: true },
    goalId: string
  ): Promise<
    | { success: true; data: { id: string; status?: string; archivedAt?: string | null } }
    | { success: false; error: { code: string; message: string } }
  >;
}




============================================================
FILE: backend/src/modules/action/action.service.ts
============================================================
import { createActionError } from "./action.errors";
import type {
  Action,
  ActionErrorCode,
  ActionResult,
  ActionStatus,
  CreateActionInput,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput,
  ActionExecutionData
} from "./action.types";
import type {
  ActionGoalAccess,
  ActionMilestoneAccess,
  ActionRepository
} from "./action.repository";
import {
  validateCreateActionInput,
  validateExecutionInput,
  validateUpdateActionInput
} from "./action.validation";

export interface ActionUser {
  userId: string;
  email: string | null;
  isAuthenticated: true;
}

function ok<T>(data: T): ActionResult<T> {
  return { success: true, data };
}
function fail<T>(code: ActionErrorCode): ActionResult<T> {
  return { success: false, error: createActionError(code) };
}
function authenticated(user: ActionUser | null | undefined): user is ActionUser {
  return Boolean(user?.isAuthenticated && user.userId?.trim());
}

export interface ActionService {
  createAction(user: ActionUser, input: CreateActionInput): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  getAction(user: ActionUser, actionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  listActionsByGoal(user: ActionUser, goalId: string): Promise<ActionResult<Action[]>>;
  listActionsByMilestone(user: ActionUser, milestoneId: string): Promise<ActionResult<Action[]>>;
  updateAction(user: ActionUser, actionId: string, input: UpdateActionInput): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  updateActionStatus(user: ActionUser, actionId: string, status: ActionStatus): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  completeAction(user: ActionUser, actionId: string, input?: { executionNote?: string }): Promise<ActionResult<Action>>;
  recordExecution(user: ActionUser, actionId: string, input: RecordExecutionInput): Promise<ActionResult<ActionExecutionData>>;
  getExecutionData(user: ActionUser, actionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<ActionExecutionData | null>>;
}

export function createActionService(
  repository: ActionRepository,
  goalAccess: ActionGoalAccess,
  milestoneAccess: ActionMilestoneAccess,
  now: () => string = () => new Date().toISOString()
): ActionService {
  async function checkGoal(user: ActionUser, goalId: string): Promise<ActionResult<true>> {
    try {
      const result = await goalAccess.validateGoal(user, goalId);
      if (result.success) return ok(true);
      return fail(result.error.code.includes("NOT_FOUND")
        ? "ACTION_GOAL_NOT_FOUND" : "ACTION_GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
    } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
  }

  async function checkMilestone(
    user: ActionUser, milestoneId: string, goalId: string
  ): Promise<ActionResult<true>> {
    try {
      const result = await milestoneAccess.validateMilestone(user, milestoneId, goalId);
      if (result.success) {
        if (result.data.goalId !== goalId) return fail("ACTION_INVALID_ASSOCIATION");
        return ok(true);
      }
      return fail(result.error.code.includes("NOT_FOUND")
        ? "ACTION_MILESTONE_NOT_FOUND" : "ACTION_MILESTONE_ACCESS_DENIED");
    } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
  }

  async function resolve(user: ActionUser, actionId: string): Promise<ActionResult<Action>> {
    if (!authenticated(user)) return fail("ACTION_UNAUTHENTICATED");
    if (!actionId?.trim()) return fail("ACTION_NOT_FOUND");
    try {
      const action = await repository.findById(actionId);
      if (!action) return fail("ACTION_NOT_FOUND");
      const goal = await checkGoal(user, action.goalId);
      if (!goal.success) return goal;
      if (action.milestoneId) {
        const milestone = await checkMilestone(user, action.milestoneId, action.goalId);
        if (!milestone.success) return milestone;
      }
      return ok(action);
    } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
  }

  return {
    async createAction(user, input) {
      if (!authenticated(user)) return fail("ACTION_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      const validation = validateCreateActionInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) return fail(validation.code);
      const goal = await checkGoal(user, input.goalId);
      if (!goal.success) return goal;
      if (input.milestoneId) {
        const milestone = await checkMilestone(user, input.milestoneId, input.goalId);
        if (!milestone.success) return milestone;
      }
      try {
        const created = await repository.create({
          goalId: input.goalId,
          milestoneId: input.milestoneId ?? null,
          title: input.title.trim(),
          description: input.description?.trim() || null,
          status: "TODO"
        });
        if (created.goalId !== input.goalId ||
            created.milestoneId !== (input.milestoneId ?? null)) {
          return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return ok(created);
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async getAction(user, actionId) {
      return resolve(user, actionId);
    },

    async listActionsByGoal(user, goalId) {
      if (!authenticated(user)) return fail("ACTION_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      const goal = await checkGoal(user, goalId);
      if (!goal.success) return goal;
      try {
        const actions = await repository.listByGoal(goalId);
        return ok(actions.filter(action => action.archivedAt === null));
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async listActionsByMilestone(user, milestoneId) {
      if (!authenticated(user)) return fail("ACTION_UNAUTHENTICATED");
      // Validate the Milestone through B04 first. Goal ID is obtained from B04.
      // This starter's B04 adapter must resolve the owning Goal in its contract.
      try {
        const actions = await repository.listByMilestone(milestoneId);
        const visible: Action[] = [];
        for (const action of actions) {
          if (action.milestoneId !== milestoneId || action.archivedAt !== null) continue;
          const resolved = await resolve(user, action.id);
          if (resolved.success) visible.push(action);
        }
        return ok(visible);
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async updateAction(user, actionId, input) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      const validation = validateUpdateActionInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) return fail(validation.code);
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") return fail("ACTION_UPDATE_NOT_ALLOWED");
      try {
        const updated = await repository.update(actionId, {
          ...(input.title !== undefined ? { title: input.title.trim() } : {}),
          ...(input.description !== undefined
            ? { description: input.description?.trim() || null } : {})
        });
        if (updated.id !== existing.data.id ||
            updated.goalId !== existing.data.goalId ||
            updated.milestoneId !== existing.data.milestoneId) {
          return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR");
        }
        return ok(updated);
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async updateActionStatus(user, actionId, status) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      if (!["TODO", "IN_PROGRESS", "COMPLETED"].includes(status)) {
        return fail("ACTION_INVALID_STATUS");
      }
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") return fail("ACTION_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
      const allowed: Record<ActionStatus, ActionStatus[]> = {
        TODO: ["IN_PROGRESS", "COMPLETED"],
        IN_PROGRESS: ["COMPLETED"],
        COMPLETED: []
      };
      if (!allowed[existing.data.status].includes(status)) {
        return fail("ACTION_INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION");
      }
      // Direct TODO -> COMPLETED remains a proposed transition; C05 must confirm it.
      try {
        const completedAt = status === "COMPLETED" ? now() : null;
        return ok(await repository.updateStatus(actionId, status, completedAt));
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async completeAction(user, actionId, input = {}) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      if (existing.data.status === "COMPLETED") return fail("ACTION_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
      try {
        if (input.executionNote !== undefined) {
          const saved = await repository.saveExecution(actionId, {
            executionNote: input.executionNote
          });
          void saved;
        }
        const completed = await repository.updateStatus(actionId, "COMPLETED", now());
        return ok(completed);
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async recordExecution(user, actionId, input) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      const validation = validateExecutionInput(input);
      if (!validation.valid) return fail(validation.code);
      try {
        return ok(await repository.saveExecution(actionId, input));
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    },

    async getExecutionData(user, actionId) {
      const existing = await resolve(user, actionId);
      if (!existing.success) return existing;
      try {
        return ok(await repository.getExecution(actionId));
      } catch { return fail("ACTION_PERSISTENCE_ERROR"); }
    }
  };
}

import type {
  Action,
  ActionExecutionData,
  ActionStatus,
  CreateActionInput,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput
} from "./action.types";

export interface ActionRepository {
  create(input: {
    goalId: string;
    milestoneId: string | null;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    status: "TODO";
  }): Promise<Action>;
  findById(actionId: string): Promise<Action | null>;
  listByGoal(goalId: string): Promise<Action[]>;
  listByMilestone(milestoneId: string): Promise<Action[]>;
  update(actionId: string, input: UpdateActionInput): Promise<Action>;
  updateStatus(
    actionId: string,
    status: ActionStatus,
    completedAt: string | null
  ): Promise<Action>;
  saveExecution(
    actionId: string,
    input: RecordExecutionInput
  ): Promise<ActionExecutionData>;
  getExecution(actionId: string): Promise<ActionExecutionData | null>;
}

/** Adapter boundary to B03. Implement using B03's public application service. */
export interface ActionGoalAccess {
  validateGoal(user: { userId: string; email: string | null; isAuthenticated: true },
    goalId: string): Promise<
      | { success: true; data: { id: string } }
      | { success: false; error: { code: string; message: string } }
    >;
}

/** Adapter boundary to B04. No direct access to B04 storage is allowed. */
export interface ActionMilestoneAccess {
  validateMilestone(user: { userId: string; email: string | null; isAuthenticated: true },
    milestoneId: string, goalId: string): Promise<
      | { success: true; data: { id: string; goalId: string } }
      | { success: false; error: { code: string; message: string } }
    >;
}
