FILE: backend/src/modules/planning/planning.service.ts
============================================================
import { PlanningError } from "./planning.errors";
import type {
  ActionAccess, PlanningAccess, PlanningProposalApproval, PlanningRepository, PlanningRules,
} from "./planning.repository";
import type {
  ApprovedPlanningProposal, AuthenticatedUserContext, CreateDailyPlanInput, CreateScheduleInput,
  DailyPlan, PlanningDateRange, ScheduleEntry, UpdateDailyPlanInput, UpdateScheduleInput,
} from "./planning.types";
import {
  validateCreateScheduleInput, validateDailyPlanInput, validateDateRange,
  validateScheduleTimes, validateUpdateScheduleInput,
} from "./planning.validation";

export class PlanningService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repository: PlanningRepository,
    private readonly actionAccess: ActionAccess,
    private readonly access: PlanningAccess,
    private readonly rules: PlanningRules,
    private readonly proposalApproval: PlanningProposalApproval,
    private readonly now: () => Date = () => new Date(),
  ) {}

  async createDailyPlan(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: CreateDailyPlanInput): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateDailyPlanInput(input);
    const existing = await this.repository.findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(user.userId, input.date);
    if (existing) throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_ALREADY_EXISTS", "A Daily Plan already exists for this user and date.");
    const timestamp = this.now().toISOString();
    return this.repository.createDailyPlan({
      userId: user.userId, date: input.date, createdAt: timestamp, updatedAt: timestamp,
    });
  }

  async getDailyPlan(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, date: string): Promise<{
    dailyPlan: DailyPlan; schedules: ScheduleEntry[];
  } | null> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateDailyPlanInput({ date });
    const plan = await this.repository.findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(user.userId, date);
    if (!plan) return null;
    await this.access.assertCanAccessDailyPlan(user, plan);
    return { dailyPlan: plan, schedules: await this.repository.listSchedulesByDailyPlan(plan.id) };
  }

  async updateDailyPlan(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string, patch: UpdateDailyPlanInput,
  ): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    const plan = await this.getOwnedPlan(user, id);
    validateDailyPlanInput(patch);
    if (patch.date !== undefined && patch.date !== plan.date) {
      const existing = await this.repository.findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(user.userId, patch.date);
      if (existing && existing.id !== id) {
        throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_ALREADY_EXISTS", "A Daily Plan already exists for the target date.");
      }
      const schedules = await this.repository.listSchedulesByDailyPlan(id);
      for (const schedule of schedules) {
        this.rules.assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate({ ...schedule, dailyPlan: { ...plan, date: patch.date } });
      }
    }
    return this.repository.updateDailyPlan(id, { ...patch, updatedAt: this.now().toISOString() });
  }

  async addActionToDailyPlan(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, dailyPlanId: string, input: Omit<CreateScheduleInput, "dailyPlanId">,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const plan = await this.getOwnedPlan(user, dailyPlanId);
    return this.createSchedule(user, { ...input, dailyPlanId: plan.id });
  }

  async removeActionFromDailyPlan(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, dailyPlanId: string, scheduleId: string,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const plan = await this.getOwnedPlan(user, dailyPlanId);
    const schedule = await this.getOwnedSchedule(user, scheduleId);
    if (schedule.dailyPlanId !== plan.id) {
      throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_INVALID_RELATIONSHIP", "Schedule does not belong to the requested Daily Plan.");
    }
    return this.repository.updateSchedule(schedule.id, {
      dailyPlanId: null, updatedAt: this.now().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  async createSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: CreateScheduleInput): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateCreateScheduleInput(input);
    await this.actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction(user, input.actionId);
    const plan = input.dailyPlanId ? await this.getOwnedPlan(user, input.dailyPlanId) : null;
    if (plan) this.rules.assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate({ startAt: input.startAt, endAt: input.endAt, dailyPlan: plan });
    await this.rules.assertScheduleAllowed({
      userId: user.userId, actionId: input.actionId, dailyPlan: plan, startAt: input.startAt, endAt: input.endAt,
    });
    const timestamp = this.now().toISOString();
    return this.repository.createSchedule({
      actionId: input.actionId, dailyPlanId: input.dailyPlanId ?? null,
      startAt: input.startAt, endAt: input.endAt, createdAt: timestamp, updatedAt: timestamp,
    });
  }

  async getSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    return this.getOwnedSchedule(user, id);
  }

  async updateSchedule(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string, patch: UpdateScheduleInput,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const schedule = await this.getOwnedSchedule(user, id);
    validateUpdateScheduleInput(patch);
    const startAt = patch.startAt ?? schedule.startAt;
    const endAt = patch.endAt ?? schedule.endAt;
    validateScheduleTimes(startAt, endAt);
    const dailyPlanId = patch.dailyPlanId === undefined ? schedule.dailyPlanId : patch.dailyPlanId;
    const plan = dailyPlanId ? await this.getOwnedPlan(user, dailyPlanId) : null;
    if (plan) this.rules.assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate({ startAt, endAt, dailyPlan: plan });
    await this.actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction(user, schedule.actionId);
    await this.rules.assertScheduleAllowed({
      userId: user.userId, actionId: schedule.actionId, dailyPlan: plan, startAt, endAt, excludeScheduleId: schedule.id,
    });
    return this.repository.updateSchedule(id, { ...patch, updatedAt: this.now().toISOString() });
  }

  async deleteSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.getOwnedSchedule(user, id);
    await this.repository.deleteSchedule(id);
  }

  async getSchedulesByDateRange(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, range: PlanningDateRange,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateDateRange(range);
    return this.repository.listSchedulesByDateRange(user.userId, range, user.workspaceId);
  }

  async applyApprovedPlanningProposal(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, proposal: ApprovedPlanningProposal,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    if (!proposal || proposal.approved !== true || !proposal.proposalId ||
        proposal.userId !== user.userId || !Array.isArray(proposal.schedules)) {
      throw new PlanningError("PLANNING_PROPOSAL_INVALID", "Proposal is invalid or not approved for this user.");
    }
    await this.proposalApproval.assertApprovedByUser(user, proposal.proposalId);
    const created: ScheduleEntry[] = [];
    // This starter applies through the same createSchedule operation; transaction/atomic batch
    // behavior must be supplied by D01 when persistence is integrated.
    for (const schedule of proposal.schedules) created.push(await this.createSchedule(user, schedule));
    return created;
  }

  private async getOwnedPlan(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    const plan = await this.repository.getDailyPlan(id);
    if (!plan) throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_NOT_FOUND", "Daily Plan was not found.");
    await this.access.assertCanAccessDailyPlan(user, plan);
    return plan;
  }

  private async getOwnedSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    const schedule = await this.repository.getSchedule(id);
    if (!schedule) throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_NOT_FOUND", "Schedule was not found.");
    await this.access.assertCanAccessSchedule(user, schedule);
    await this.actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction(user, schedule.actionId);
    return schedule;
  }

  private assertAuthenticated(user: AuthenticatedUserContext): void {
    if (!user || user.isAuthenticated !== true || !user.userId) {
      throw new PlanningError("DAILY_PLAN_ACCESS_DENIED", "Authenticated user context is required.");
    }
  }
}




============================================================
FILE: backend/src/modules/planning/planning.repository.ts
============================================================
import type {
  ActionId, AuthenticatedUserContext, DailyPlan, DailyPlanId, PlanningDateRange,
  ScheduleEntry, ScheduleId,
} from "./planning.types";

export interface PlanningRepository {
  createDailyPlan(input: Omit<DailyPlan, "id">): Promise<DailyPlan>;
  getDailyPlan(id: DailyPlanId): Promise<DailyPlan | null>;
  findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(userId: string, date: string): Promise<DailyPlan | null>;
  updateDailyPlan(id: DailyPlanId, patch: Partial<Pick<DailyPlan, "date" | "updatedAt">>): Promise<DailyPlan>;
  listSchedulesByDailyPlan(dailyPlanId: DailyPlanId): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]>;
  createSchedule(input: Omit<ScheduleEntry, "id">): Promise<ScheduleEntry>;
  getSchedule(id: ScheduleId): Promise<ScheduleEntry | null>;
  updateSchedule(id: ScheduleId, patch: Partial<Pick<ScheduleEntry,
    "startAt" | "endAt" | "dailyPlanId" | "updatedAt">>): Promise<ScheduleEntry>;
  deleteSchedule(id: ScheduleId): Promise<void>;
  listSchedulesByDateRange(userId: string, range: PlanningDateRange, workspaceId?: string): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]>;
  findOverlappingSchedules(userId: string, startAt: string, endAt: string, excludeScheduleId?: ScheduleId): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]>;
}

export interface ActionAccess {
  assertCanAccessAction(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, actionId: ActionId): Promise<void>;
}

export interface PlanningAccess {
  assertCanAccessDailyPlan(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, plan: DailyPlan): Promise<void>;
  assertCanAccessSchedule(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, schedule: ScheduleEntry): Promise<void>;
}

export interface PlanningRules {
  /** C07 / DEC-017 supplies approved conflict and relationship behavior. */
  assertScheduleAllowed(input: {
    userId: string; actionId: ActionId; dailyPlan: DailyPlan | null; startAt: string; endAt: string;
    excludeScheduleId?: ScheduleId;
  }): Promise<void>;
  /** C07 supplies the approved date/time-zone and date-boundary interpretation. */
  assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate(schedule: {
    startAt: string; endAt: string; dailyPlan: DailyPlan;
  }): void;
}

export interface PlanningProposalApproval {
  assertApprovedByUser(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, proposalId: string): Promise<void>;
}

FILE: backend/src/modules/progress/progress.service.ts
============================================================
import { ProgressError } from "./progress.errors";
import type { ProgressRules, ProgressSourceAccess, ProgressHistoryRepository } from "./progress.repository";
import type {
  ActionCompletionResult, ActionProgressInput, AuthenticatedUserContext, ChildGoalAggregationInput,
  GoalProgressInput, GoalProgressResult, MeasurementProgressInput, MeasurementProgressResult,
  MeasurementTargetComparison, ProgressSummary,
} from "./progress.types";
import {
  validateActionProgressInput, validateChildAggregationInput, validateGoalProgressInput,
  validateMeasurementProgressInput,
} from "./progress.validation";

export class ProgressService {
  constructor(
    private readonly sources: ProgressSourceAccess,
    private readonly rules: ProgressRules,
    private readonly history?: ProgressHistoryRepository,
  ) {}

  calculateMeasurementProgress(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: MeasurementProgressInput,
  ): MeasurementProgressResult {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateMeasurementProgressInput(input);
    return this.rules.calculateMeasurement(input);
  }

  calculateActionCompletion(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: ActionProgressInput,
  ): ActionCompletionResult {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateActionProgressInput(input);
    return this.rules.calculateActionCompletion(input);
  }

  calculateGoalProgress(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: GoalProgressInput): GoalProgressResult {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateGoalProgressInput(input);
    return this.rules.calculateGoal(input);
  }

  aggregateChildGoalProgress(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: ChildGoalAggregationInput,
  ): GoalProgressResult {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateChildAggregationInput(input);
    return this.rules.aggregateChildGoals(input);
  }

  compareMeasurementWithTarget(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: MeasurementProgressInput,
  ): MeasurementTargetComparison {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateMeasurementProgressInput(input);
    if (input.targetValue === null) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INSUFFICIENT_DATA", "Measurement has no Target Value.");
    }
    const comparison = this.rules.compareMeasurement(input);
    return {
      measurementId: input.measurementId,
      currentValue: input.currentValue,
      targetValue: input.targetValue,
      ...comparison,
    };
  }

  async getGoalProgressSummary(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: string): Promise<ProgressSummary> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    await this.sources.assertCanAccessGoal(user, goalId);
    const inputs = await this.sources.getGoalProgressInputs(user, goalId);
    const goalInput: GoalProgressInput = {
      goalId,
      measurementResults: inputs.measurementResults,
      actionResult: inputs.actionResult,
      childGoalResults: inputs.childGoalResults,
    };
    const goalProgress = this.rules.calculateGoal(goalInput);
    return {
      goalId,
      goalProgress,
      measurementProgress: inputs.measurementResults,
      actionCompletion: inputs.actionResult,
      childGoalProgress: inputs.childGoalResults,
      calculationStatus: this.rules.combineStatuses([
        goalProgress.calculationStatus,
        ...inputs.measurementResults.map((item) => item.calculationStatus),
        ...(inputs.actionResult ? [inputs.actionResult.calculationStatus] : []),
        ...inputs.childGoalResults.map((item) => item.calculationStatus),
      ]),
    };
  }

  async getProgressSummary(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalIds: string[]): Promise<ProgressSummary[]> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    if (!Array.isArray(goalIds) || goalIds.some((id) => !id?.trim())) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_INVALID_INPUT", "Goal IDs must be a list of non-empty IDs.");
    }
    const summaries: ProgressSummary[] = [];
    for (const goalId of goalIds) summaries.push(await this.getGoalProgressSummary(user, goalId));
    return summaries;
  }

  async getProgressDataForReview(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: string,
  ): Promise<ProgressSummary> {
    return this.getGoalProgressSummary(user, goalId);
  }

  async saveProgressSnapshot(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, summary: ProgressSummary): Promise<void> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    if (!this.history?.saveSnapshot) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED", "Progress snapshot persistence is not configured.");
    }
    await this.sources.assertCanAccessGoal(user, summary.goalId);
    await this.history.saveSnapshot(user.userId, summary.goalId, summary);
  }

  private assertAuthenticated(user: AuthenticatedUserContext): void {
    if (!user || user.isAuthenticated !== true || !user.userId) {
      throw new ProgressError("PROGRESS_ACCESS_DENIED", "Authenticated user context is required.");
    }
  }
}

import type {
  ActionCompletionResult, AuthenticatedUserContext, GoalId, GoalProgressResult,
  MeasurementProgressResult, ProgressSummary,
} from "./progress.types";

export interface ProgressSourceAccess {

  getMeasurementProgressInputs(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<unknown[]>;
  getActionProgressInput(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<unknown>;

  calculateActionCompletion(input: unknown): ActionCompletionResult;

FILE: backend/src/modules/review/review.service.ts
============================================================
import { ReviewError } from "./review.errors";
import type { CreateReviewInput, ReviewRecord, ReviewScope, ReviewSummary, ReviewUserContext, UpdateReviewNoteInput } from "./review.types";
import type { ReviewAccess, ReviewRepository, ReviewRules, ReviewSourceReader } from "./review.repository";
import { validateReviewNote, validateReviewScope } from "./review.validation";
export class ReviewService {
  constructor(private readonly repository: ReviewRepository, private readonly access: ReviewAccess,
    private readonly sources: ReviewSourceReader, private readonly rules: ReviewRules) {}
  async getReviewData(user: ReviewUserContext, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<ReviewSummary> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user); validateReviewScope(scope, this.rules.validation);
    await this.access.assertScopeAccess(user.userId, scope);
    let results: Awaited<ReturnType<ReviewSourceReader["getPlannedWork"]>> extends never ? never : any;
    try {
      results = await Promise.all([
        this.sources.getPlannedWork(user.userId, scope), this.sources.getActualExecution(user.userId, scope),
        this.sources.getProgress(user.userId, scope), this.sources.getMeasurement(user.userId, scope),
      ]);
    } catch (error) { throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE", "A Review source dependency is unavailable.", error); }
    const [p, a, g, m] = results;
    const plannedWork = { status: p.data.length ? "AVAILABLE" as const : "EMPTY" as const, data: p.data, limitations: p.limitations ?? [] };
    const actualExecution = { status: a.data.length ? "AVAILABLE" as const : "EMPTY" as const, data: a.data, limitations: a.limitations ?? [] };
    const progress = { status: g.status, data: g.data, limitations: g.limitations ?? [] };
    const measurement = { status: m.status, data: m.data, limitations: m.limitations ?? [] };
    let comparison;
    try {
      const compared = this.rules.matchPlannedToActual(p.data, a.data);
      comparison = { status: compared.items.length ? "AVAILABLE" as const : "EMPTY" as const, data: compared, limitations: compared.limitations };
    } catch (error) { throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_COMPARISON_FAILED", "Planned and actual comparison failed.", error); }
    try {
      return this.rules.generateSummary({ scope, plannedWork, actualExecution, comparison, progress, measurement, generatedAt: this.rules.now() });
    } catch (error) { throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_SUMMARY_GENERATION_FAILED", "Review summary generation failed.", error); }
  }
  async createReview(user: ReviewUserContext, input: CreateReviewInput): Promise<ReviewRecord> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user); validateReviewScope(input.scope, this.rules.validation);
    await this.access.assertScopeAccess(user.userId, input.scope);
    if (!this.rules.createReviewRecord) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_INVALID_STATE", "Review persistence is not enabled until its model is approved.");
    const note = input.note?.trim() || null;
    if (note && note.length > this.rules.validation.maxNoteLength) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOTE_INVALID", "Review note exceeds configured length.");
    const now = this.rules.now();
    const record: ReviewRecord = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), userId: user.userId, scope: input.scope, summary: null, note, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
    try { return await this.repository.create(record); }
    catch (error) { throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to create Review.", error); }
  }
  async getReview(user: ReviewUserContext, reviewId: string): Promise<ReviewRecord> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    const record = await this.repository.getById(reviewId);
    if (!record) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOT_FOUND", "Review was not found.");
    if (record.userId !== user.userId) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_ACCESS_DENIED", "Review access denied.");
    await this.access.assertScopeAccess(user.userId, record.scope);
    return record;
  }
  async updateReviewNote(user: ReviewUserContext, reviewId: string, input: UpdateReviewNoteInput): Promise<ReviewRecord> {
    await this.getReview(user, reviewId);
    const note = validateReviewNote(input, this.rules.validation);
    try {
      const updated = await this.repository.updateNote(reviewId, user.userId, note, this.rules.now());
      if (!updated) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOT_FOUND", "Review was not found.");
      return updated;
    } catch (error) { if (error instanceof ReviewError) throw error; throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to update Review note.", error); }
  }
  async refreshReviewSummary(user: ReviewUserContext, reviewId: string): Promise<ReviewRecord> {
    const record = await this.getReview(user, reviewId);
    const summary = await this.getReviewData(user, record.scope);
    if (!this.rules.persistSummary) return { ...record, summary };
    try {
      const updated = await this.repository.updateSummary(reviewId, user.userId, summary, this.rules.now());
      if (!updated) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_NOT_FOUND", "Review was not found.");
      return updated;
    } catch (error) { if (error instanceof ReviewError) throw error; throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_PERSISTENCE_ERROR", "Unable to refresh Review summary.", error); }
  }
  private assertAuthenticated(user: ReviewUserContext): void {
    if (!user?.isAuthenticated || !user.userId) throw new ReviewError("REVIEW_ACCESS_DENIED", "Authentication is required.");
  }
}

import type { ReviewRecord, ReviewScope, PlannedWorkItem, ActualExecutionItem, PlannedActualComparison, ReviewSummary } from "./review.types";
import type { ReviewValidationRules } from "./review.validation";
export interface ReviewRepository {
  create(record: ReviewRecord): Promise<ReviewRecord>;
  getById(reviewId: string): Promise<ReviewRecord | null>;
  updateNote(reviewId: string, userId: string, note: string, updatedAt: string): Promise<ReviewRecord | null>;
  updateSummary(reviewId: string, userId: string, summary: ReviewSummary, updatedAt: string): Promise<ReviewRecord | null>;
}
export interface ReviewAccess { assertScopeAccess(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<void>; }
export interface ReviewSourceReader {
  getPlannedWork(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<{ data: PlannedWorkItem[]; limitations?: string[] }>;
  getActualExecution(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<{ data: ActualExecutionItem[]; limitations?: string[] }>;
  getProgress(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<{ data: unknown | null; status: "AVAILABLE" | "EMPTY" | "UNAVAILABLE"; limitations?: string[] }>;
  getMeasurement(userId: string, scope: ReviewScope): Promise<{ data: unknown | null; status: "AVAILABLE" | "EMPTY" | "UNAVAILABLE" | "NOT_REQUESTED"; limitations?: string[] }>;
}
export interface ReviewRules {
  validation: ReviewValidationRules;
  matchPlannedToActual(planned: PlannedWorkItem[], actual: ActualExecutionItem[]): PlannedActualComparison;
  generateSummary(input: Omit<ReviewSummary, "limitations">): ReviewSummary;
  now(): string;
  createReviewRecord: boolean;
  persistSummary: boolean;
}

FILE: backend/src/modules/measurement/measurement.service.ts
============================================================
import { MeasurementError } from "./measurement.errors";
import type { GoalAccess, MeasurementRepository, MeasurementRules } from "./measurement.repository";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext, CreateMeasurementInput, Measurement, MeasurementHistoryQuery,
  MeasurementRecord, RecordMeasurementValueInput, UpdateMeasurementInput,
} from "./measurement.types";
import {
  validateCreateMeasurementInput, validateHistoryQuery, validateRecordInput,
  validateUpdateMeasurementInput,
} from "./measurement.validation";

export class MeasurementService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repository: MeasurementRepository,
    private readonly goalAccess: GoalAccess,
    private readonly rules: MeasurementRules,
    private readonly now: () => Date = () => new Date(),
  ) {}

  async createMeasurement(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: CreateMeasurementInput): Promise<Measurement> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    validateCreateMeasurementInput(input);
    await this.goalAccess.assertCanAccessGoal(user, input.goalId);
    this.rules.validateDefinition({
      valueType: input.valueType,
      unit: input.unit ?? null,
      targetValue: input.targetValue ?? null,
    });
    const timestamp = this.now().toISOString();
    return this.repository.createMeasurement({
      goalId: input.goalId,
      label: input.label.trim(),
      description: input.description ?? null,
      valueType: input.valueType,
      unit: input.unit ?? null,
      targetValue: input.targetValue ?? null,
      createdAt: timestamp,
      updatedAt: timestamp,
      archivedAt: null,
    });
  }

  async getMeasurement(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<Measurement> {
    const measurement = await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    return measurement;
  }

  async listMeasurementsByGoal(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: string, includeArchived = false,
  ): Promise<Measurement[]> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    await this.goalAccess.assertCanAccessGoal(user, goalId);
    return this.repository.listMeasurementsByGoal(goalId, includeArchived);
  }

  async updateMeasurement(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string, patch: UpdateMeasurementInput,
  ): Promise<Measurement> {
    const definition = await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    validateUpdateMeasurementInput(patch);
    const history = await this.repository.listRecords(id, {});
    this.rules.assertCanUpdate(definition, patch, history.length > 0);
    const candidate = {
      valueType: definition.valueType,
      unit: patch.unit === undefined ? definition.unit : patch.unit,
      targetValue: patch.targetValue === undefined ? definition.targetValue : patch.targetValue,
    };
    this.rules.validateDefinition(candidate);
    return this.repository.updateMeasurement(id, {
      ...patch,
      ...(patch.label !== undefined ? { label: patch.label.trim() } : {}),
      updatedAt: this.now().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  async recordMeasurementValue(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, input: RecordMeasurementValueInput,
  ): Promise<MeasurementRecord> {
    const definition = await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, input.measurementId);
    validateRecordInput(input);
    this.rules.assertCanRecord(definition);
    this.rules.validateValue(definition, input.value);
    const createdAt = this.now().toISOString();
    return this.repository.createRecord({
      measurementId: definition.id,
      value: input.value,
      recordedAt: input.recordedAt ?? createdAt,
      createdAt,
      note: input.note ?? null,
    });
  }

  async getCurrentMeasurementValue(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string,
  ): Promise<MeasurementRecord | null> {
    await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    const records = await this.repository.listRecords(id, {});
    return this.rules.resolveCurrent(records);
  }

  async getMeasurementHistory(
    user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string, query: MeasurementHistoryQuery = {},
  ): Promise<MeasurementRecord[]> {
    await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    validateHistoryQuery(query);
    return this.repository.listRecords(id, query);
  }

  async archiveMeasurement(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<Measurement> {
    const definition = await this.getOwnedMeasurement(user, id);
    this.rules.assertCanArchive(definition);
    return this.repository.archiveMeasurement(id, this.now().toISOString());
  }

  private async getOwnedMeasurement(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, id: string): Promise<Measurement> {
    this.assertAuthenticated(user);
    if (!id?.trim()) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_NOT_FOUND", "Measurement ID is required.");
    const measurement = await this.repository.getMeasurement(id);
    if (!measurement) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_NOT_FOUND", "Measurement was not found.");
    await this.goalAccess.assertCanAccessGoal(user, measurement.goalId);
    return measurement;
  }

  private assertAuthenticated(user: AuthenticatedUserContext): void {
    if (!user || user.isAuthenticated !== true || !user.userId) {
      throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_ACCESS_DENIED", "Authenticated user context is required.");
    }
  }
}

import type {
  GoalId, Measurement, MeasurementId, MeasurementRecord, MeasurementRecordId,
  MeasurementHistoryQuery, AuthenticatedUserContext,
} from "./measurement.types";

export interface MeasurementRepository {
  createMeasurement(input: Omit<Measurement, "id">): Promise<Measurement>;
  getMeasurement(id: MeasurementId): Promise<Measurement | null>;
  listMeasurementsByGoal(goalId: GoalId, includeArchived: boolean): Promise<Measurement[]>;
  updateMeasurement(id: MeasurementId, patch: Partial<Pick<Measurement,
    "label" | "description" | "unit" | "targetValue" | "updatedAt">>): Promise<Measurement>;
  archiveMeasurement(id: MeasurementId, archivedAt: string): Promise<Measurement>;
  createRecord(input: Omit<MeasurementRecord, "id">): Promise<MeasurementRecord>;
  listRecords(measurementId: MeasurementId, query: MeasurementHistoryQuery): Promise<MeasurementRecord[]>;
}

export interface GoalAccess {
  assertCanAccessGoal(user: AuthenticatedUserContext, goalId: GoalId): Promise<void>;
}

export interface MeasurementRules {
  /** Domain policy supplied by C06: approved value types, unit and target compatibility. */
  validateDefinition(input: {
    valueType: string; unit: string | null; targetValue: unknown;
  }): void;
  /** Domain policy supplied by C06; must reject values incompatible with the definition. */
  validateValue(definition: Measurement, value: unknown): void;
  /** C06 decides whether archived definitions accept new records. */
  assertCanRecord(definition: Measurement): void;
  /** C06 decides whether/how current record is resolved; no ordering is assumed here. */
  resolveCurrent(records: MeasurementRecord[]): MeasurementRecord | null;
  /** C06 decides whether an existing definition may be updated, especially unit changes. */
  assertCanUpdate(definition: Measurement, patch: {
    label?: string; description?: string | null; unit?: string | null; targetValue?: unknown;
  }, hasRecords: boolean): void;
  /** C06 decides whether repeated archive requests are permitted. */
  assertCanArchive(definition: Measurement): void;
}

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../infrastructure/database/sqlite.ts";
import { AIOrchestrationService, GeminiAIProvider, SqliteAIProposalRepository,
  GoalDiscoveryContextReader, GoalDiscoveryOutputValidator, GoalProposalDomainApplier,
  AIUsageLimiter } from "../modules/ai-orchestration/index.ts";
import type { AIProvider } from "../modules/ai-orchestration/ai-orchestration.repository";

import {
  SqliteWorkspaceRepository,
  SqliteGoalRepository,
  SqliteMilestoneRepository,
  SqliteActionRepository,
  SqlitePlanningRepository
} from "../infrastructure/repositories/index.ts";

import {
  createAuthProvider,
  createTokenVerifier,
  type AuthConfig,
  type TokenVerifier
} from "../infrastructure/auth/index.ts";

import {
  createAuthenticationService,
  type AuthenticationService
} from "../modules/auth/index.ts";

import {
  createAuthenticationApiService,
  type AuthenticationApiService
} from "../modules/auth/auth.service.ts";

import {
  createWorkspaceService,
  type WorkspaceService,
  type GoalSummaryReader
} from "../modules/workspace/index.ts";

import {
  createGoalService,
  type GoalService,
  type GoalWorkspaceAccess
} from "../modules/goal/index.ts";

import {
  createMilestoneService,
  type MilestoneService,
  type MilestoneGoalAccess
} from "../modules/milestone/index.ts";

import {
  createActionService,
  type ActionService,
  type ActionGoalAccess,
  type ActionMilestoneAccess
} from "../modules/action/index.ts";

import {
  PlanningService,
  PlanningError,
  type ActionAccess,
  type PlanningAccess,
  type PlanningProposalApproval,
  type PlanningRules
} from "../modules/planning/index.ts";

import {
  ProgressService,
  ProgressError,
  type ProgressRules,
  type ProgressSourceAccess
} from "../modules/progress/index.ts";
import { MeasurementError, MeasurementService, MVPMeasurementRules } from "../modules/measurement/index.ts";
import { SqliteMeasurementRepository } from "../infrastructure/repositories/sqlite-measurement.repository.ts";

export interface ApplicationContainer {
  connection: DatabaseConnection;
  authService: AuthenticationService;
  authApiService: AuthenticationApiService;
  tokenVerifier: TokenVerifier;
  workspaceService: WorkspaceService;
  goalService: GoalService;
  milestoneService: MilestoneService;
  actionService: ActionService;
  planningService: PlanningService;
  progressService: ProgressService;
  measurementService: MeasurementService;
  aiService: AIOrchestrationService;
  repositories: {
    workspace: SqliteWorkspaceRepository;
    goal: SqliteGoalRepository;
    milestone: SqliteMilestoneRepository;
    action: SqliteActionRepository;
    planning: SqlitePlanningRepository;
  };
}

export interface ContainerConfig {
  auth?: AuthConfig;
  ai?: { apiKey?: string; model?: string; timeoutMs?: number; perUserDaily?: number; globalDaily?: number; provider?: AIProvider };
}

export function createApplicationContainer(
  connection: DatabaseConnection,
  config: ContainerConfig = {}
): ApplicationContainer {
  // 1. Repositories
  const workspaceRepo = new SqliteWorkspaceRepository(connection);
  const goalRepo = new SqliteGoalRepository(connection);
  const milestoneRepo = new SqliteMilestoneRepository(connection);
  const actionRepo = new SqliteActionRepository(connection);
  const planningRepo = new SqlitePlanningRepository(connection);
  const measurementRepo = new SqliteMeasurementRepository(connection);

  // 2. Auth & Token Verifier
  const authConfig: AuthConfig = config.auth ?? {};
  const authProvider = createAuthProvider(authConfig);

  const authService = createAuthenticationService(authProvider);
  const authApiService = createAuthenticationApiService(authProvider);

  const tokenVerifier = createTokenVerifier({
    supabaseUrl: authConfig.supabaseUrl,
    supabaseKey: authConfig.supabaseAnonKey,
    devMode: authConfig.devMode ?? false
  });

  // 3. B02 Workspace Service
  const goalSummaryReader: GoalSummaryReader = {
    async getIndependentGoalCount(workspaceId: string): Promise<number> {
      return goalRepo.countIndependentGoals(workspaceId);
    },

    async getProjectGoalCount(projectId: string): Promise<number> {
      return goalRepo.countProjectGoals(projectId);
    }
  };

  const workspaceService = createWorkspaceService(
    workspaceRepo,
    goalSummaryReader
  );

  // 4. B03 Goal Service
  const goalWorkspaceAccess: GoalWorkspaceAccess = {
    async getCurrentWorkspaceId(userId: string, selectedWorkspaceId?: string): Promise<string | null> {
      const ws = selectedWorkspaceId
        ? await workspaceRepo.findWorkspaceById(selectedWorkspaceId)
        : await workspaceRepo.findWorkspaceByUserId(userId);
      if (ws && ws.userId !== userId) return null;
      return ws?.id ?? null;
    },

    async validateProjectInWorkspace(
      userId: string,
      workspaceId: string,
      projectId: string
    ): Promise<boolean> {
      const project = await workspaceRepo.findProjectById(projectId);

      if (!project || project.workspaceId !== workspaceId) {
        return false;
      }

      const ws = await workspaceRepo.findWorkspaceById(workspaceId);
      return ws?.userId === userId;
    }
  };

  const goalService = createGoalService(
    goalRepo,
    goalWorkspaceAccess
  );

  // 5. B04 Milestone Service
  const milestoneGoalAccess: MilestoneGoalAccess = {
    async getGoalForUser(user, goalId) {
      const res = await goalService.getGoal(user, goalId);

      if (!res.success) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: res.error
        };
      }

      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          id: res.data.id,
          status: res.data.status,
          archivedAt: res.data.archivedAt
        }
      };
    }
  };

  const milestoneService = createMilestoneService(
    milestoneRepo,
    milestoneGoalAccess
  );

  // 6. B05 Action Service
  const actionGoalAccess: ActionGoalAccess = {
    async validateGoal(user, goalId) {
      const res = await goalService.getGoal(user, goalId);

      if (!res.success) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: res.error
        };
      }

      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          id: res.data.id
        }
      };
    }
  };

  const actionMilestoneAccess: ActionMilestoneAccess = {
    async validateMilestone(user, milestoneId, goalId) {
      const res = await milestoneService.getMilestone(
        user,
        milestoneId
      );

      if (!res.success) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: res.error
        };
      }

      if (res.data.goalId !== goalId) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: {
            code: "ACTION_INVALID_ASSOCIATION",
            message: "Milestone does not belong to specified Goal"
          }
        };
      }

      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          id: res.data.id,
          goalId: res.data.goalId
        }
      };
    }
  };

  const actionService = createActionService(
    actionRepo,
    actionGoalAccess,
    actionMilestoneAccess
  );

  // 7. B07 Planning Service
  const actionAccess: ActionAccess = {
    async assertCanAccessAction(user, actionId) {
      const res = await actionService.getAction(user, actionId);

      if (!res.success) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "SCHEDULE_ACCESS_DENIED",
          "Cannot access the referenced Action."
        );
      }
    }
  };

  const planningAccess: PlanningAccess = {
    async assertCanAccessDailyPlan(user, plan) {
      if (plan.userId !== user.userId) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "DAILY_PLAN_ACCESS_DENIED",
          "Access denied to the specified Daily Plan."
        );
      }
    },

    async assertCanAccessSchedule(user, schedule) {
      await actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction(
        user,
        schedule.actionId
      );

      if (schedule.dailyPlanId) {
        const plan = await planningRepo.getDailyPlan(
          schedule.dailyPlanId
        );

        if (plan && plan.userId !== user.userId) {
          throw new PlanningError(
            "SCHEDULE_ACCESS_DENIED",
            "Schedule belongs to another user's Daily Plan."
          );
        }
      }
    }
  };

  const planningRules: PlanningRules = {
    async assertScheduleAllowed({ userId, startAt, endAt, excludeScheduleId }) {
      const start = Date.parse(startAt);
      const end = Date.parse(endAt);

      if (
        !Number.isFinite(start) ||
        !Number.isFinite(end) ||
        end <= start
      ) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "SCHEDULE_INVALID_TIME_RANGE",
          "Schedule end time must be after start time."
        );
      }
      const overlaps = await planningRepo.findOverlappingSchedules(userId, startAt, endAt, excludeScheduleId);
      if (overlaps.length) {
        throw new PlanningError("SCHEDULE_CONFLICT", "This time overlaps another scheduled Action. Choose a different time.");
      }
    },

    assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate({ startAt, dailyPlan }) {
      if (!startAt.startsWith(dailyPlan.date)) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "SCHEDULE_INVALID_RELATIONSHIP",
          "Schedule start time must match the Daily Plan date."
        );
      }
    }
  };

  const proposalApproval: PlanningProposalApproval = {
    async assertApprovedByUser(user, proposalId) {
      if (!user || !user.userId || !proposalId) {
        throw new PlanningError(
          "PLANNING_APPROVAL_REQUIRED",
          "Proposal approval verification failed."
        );
      }
    }
  };

  const planningService = new PlanningService(
    planningRepo,
    actionAccess,
    planningAccess,
    planningRules,
    proposalApproval
  );

  // 8. B08 Progress Service
  const progressSources: ProgressSourceAccess = {
    async assertCanAccessGoal(user, goalId) {
      const res = await goalService.getGoal(user, goalId);

      if (!res.success) {
        throw new ProgressError(
          "PROGRESS_ACCESS_DENIED",
          "Access denied to the specified Goal."
        );
      }
    },

    async getMeasurementProgressInputs() {
      // B06 is intentionally not integrated
      return [];
    },

    async getActionProgressInput(_user, goalId) {
      const actions = await actionRepo.listByGoal(goalId);
      return {
        goalId,
        actions
      };
    },

    async getChildGoalIds(user, goalId) {
      const wsId = await goalWorkspaceAccess.getCurrentWorkspaceId(
        user.userId,
        user.workspaceId
      );

      if (!wsId) return [];

      const children = await goalRepo.listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
        wsId,
        {
          parentGoalId: goalId
        }
      );

      return children.map((c) => c.id);
    },

    async getGoalProgressInputs(user, goalId) {
      const childGoalIds = await this.getChildGoalIds(
        user,
        goalId
      );

      const actions = (await actionRepo.listByGoal(goalId)).filter((action) => !action.archivedAt);
      const completedCount = actions.filter((action) => action.status === "COMPLETED").length;
      const definitions = await measurementRepo.listMeasurementsByGoal(goalId, false);
      const measurementResults = await Promise.all(definitions.map(async (measurement) => {
        const record = (await measurementRepo.listRecords(measurement.id, { limit: 1 }))[0] ?? null;
        const comparable = record !== null && measurement.targetValue !== null;
        return {
          measurementId: measurement.id,
          currentValue: record?.value ?? null,
          targetValue: measurement.targetValue,
          progressValue: null,
          calculationStatus: comparable ? "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED" as const : "INSUFFICIENT_DATA" as const,
          calculationContext: {
            unit: measurement.unit,
            latestRecordedAt: record?.recordedAt ?? null,
            reason: !record ? "No value has been recorded." : measurement.targetValue === null ? "No target is defined." : "Target direction is undefined; no percentage is inferred."
          }
        };
      }));

      return {
        measurementResults,

        actionResult: {
          goalId,
          totalActions: actions.length,
          completedActions: completedCount,
          incompleteActions: actions.length - completedCount,
          completionValue: actions.length ? (completedCount / actions.length) * 100 : null,
          calculationStatus: actions.length ? "CALCULATED" as const : "NOT_APPLICABLE" as const,
          calculationContext: {
            note: "Completed active Actions divided by total active Actions; this is execution completion, not Goal outcome progress."
          }
        },

        childGoalResults: childGoalIds.map((id) => ({
          goalId: id,
          progressValue: null,
          calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED" as const,
          contributingSources: [],
          calculationContext: {}
        }))
      };
    }
  };

  const progressRules: ProgressRules = {
    calculateMeasurement() {
      return {
        measurementId: "",
        currentValue: 0,
        targetValue: 0,
        progressValue: null,
        calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
        calculationContext: {}
      };
    },

    calculateActionCompletion(input: unknown) {
      const typed = (input ?? {}) as { goalId?: string; actions?: Array<{ status?: string; archived?: boolean }> };
      const actions = (typed.actions ?? []).filter((action) => !action.archived);
      const total = actions.length;
      const completed = actions.filter((action) => action.status === "COMPLETED").length;
      return {
        goalId: typed.goalId ?? "", totalActions: total, completedActions: completed,
        incompleteActions: total - completed,
        completionValue: total ? (completed / total) * 100 : null,
        calculationStatus: total ? "CALCULATED" as const : "NOT_APPLICABLE" as const,
        calculationContext: { rule: "completed active Actions / total active Actions", metric: "ACTION_COMPLETION" }
      };
    },

    calculateGoal(input: unknown) {
      const typed = (input ?? {}) as {
        goalId?: string;
      };

      return {
        goalId: typed.goalId ?? "",
        progressValue: null,
        calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
        contributingSources: [],
        calculationContext: {}
      };
    },

    aggregateChildGoals() {
      return {
        goalId: "",
        progressValue: null,
        calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
        contributingSources: [],
        calculationContext: {}
      };
    },

    compareMeasurement() {
      return {
        difference: null,
        comparisonStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
        progressValue: null
      };
    },

    combineStatuses() {
      return "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED";
    }
  };

  const progressService = new ProgressService(
    progressSources,
    progressRules
  );

  const measurementService = new MeasurementService(
    measurementRepo,
    {
      async assertCanAccessGoal(user, goalId) {
        const result = await goalService.getGoal(user, goalId);
        if (!result.success) throw new MeasurementError("MEASUREMENT_ACCESS_DENIED", "Goal is not accessible.");
      }
    },
    new MVPMeasurementRules(),
  );

  const aiConfig = config.ai ?? {};
  const aiService = new AIOrchestrationService(
    new GoalDiscoveryContextReader(),
    aiConfig.provider ?? new GeminiAIProvider(aiConfig.apiKey, aiConfig.model, aiConfig.timeoutMs),
    new GoalDiscoveryOutputValidator(18),
    new SqliteAIProposalRepository(connection),
    new GoalProposalDomainApplier({ goalService, milestoneService, actionService, measurementService }),
    {
      validation: { maxInputLength: 4000, maxContextBytes: 12000, maxProposalItems: 18 },
      now: () => new Date().toISOString(),
      createProposalId: randomUUID,
      proposalPersistenceEnabled: true,
    },
    new AIUsageLimiter(connection, aiConfig.perUserDaily ?? 12, aiConfig.globalDaily ?? 90),
  );

  return {
    connection,
    authService,
    authApiService,
    tokenVerifier,
    workspaceService,
    goalService,
    milestoneService,
    actionService,
    planningService,
    progressService,
    measurementService,
    aiService,
    repositories: {
      workspace: workspaceRepo,
      goal: goalRepo,
      milestone: milestoneRepo,
      action: actionRepo,
      planning: planningRepo
    }
  };
}




============================================================
FILE: backend/src/infrastructure/repositories/sqlite-goal.repository.ts
============================================================
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  CreateGoalInput,
  Goal,
  ListGoalsFilter,
  UpdateGoalInput
} from "../../modules/goal/goal.types.ts";
import type { GoalRepository } from "../../modules/goal/goal.repository.ts";

interface GoalRow {
  id: string;
  workspace_id: string;
  project_id: string | null;
  parent_goal_id: string | null;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  target_date: string | null;
  status: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  archived_at: string | null;
}

function mapGoal(row: GoalRow): Goal {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    workspaceId: row.workspace_id,
    projectId: row.project_id,
    parentGoalId: row.parent_goal_id,
    title: row.title,
    description: row.description,
    targetDate: row.target_date,
    status: row.status,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    archivedAt: row.archived_at
  };
}

export class SqliteGoalRepository implements GoalRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async createGoal(workspaceId: string, input: CreateGoalInput): Promise<Goal> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const projectId = input.projectId ?? null;
    const parentGoalId = input.parentGoalId ?? null;
    const description = input.description?.trim() ? input.description.trim() : null;
    const targetDate = input.targetDate?.trim() ? input.targetDate.trim() : null;
    const status = "ACTIVE";

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO goals (
        id, workspace_id, project_id, parent_goal_id, title, description,
        target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, archived_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL)`
    );

    stmt.run(
      id,
      workspaceId,
      projectId,
      parentGoalId,
      input.title.trim(),
      description,
      targetDate,
      status,
      now,
      now
    );

    return {
      id,
      workspaceId,
      projectId,
      parentGoalId,
      title: input.title.trim(),
      description,
      targetDate,
      status,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      archivedAt: null
    };
  }

  async findGoalById(goalId: string): Promise<Goal | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, workspace_id, project_id, parent_goal_id, title, description,
              target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, archived_at
       FROM goals WHERE id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(goalId) as GoalRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapGoal(row) : null;
  }

  async listGoalsByWorkspaceId(
    workspaceId: string,
    filter?: ListGoalsFilter
  ): Promise<Goal[]> {
    let sql = `SELECT id, workspace_id, project_id, parent_goal_id, title, description,
                      target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, archived_at
               FROM goals WHERE workspace_id = ?`;
    const params: (string | null)[] = [workspaceId];

    if (filter) {
      if (filter.projectId !== undefined) {
        if (filter.projectId === null) {
          sql += " AND project_id IS NULL";
        } else {
          sql += " AND project_id = ?";
          params.push(filter.projectId);
        }
      }

      if (filter.parentGoalId !== undefined) {
        if (filter.parentGoalId === null) {
          sql += " AND parent_goal_id IS NULL";
        } else {
          sql += " AND parent_goal_id = ?";
          params.push(filter.parentGoalId);
        }
      }

      if (filter.status !== undefined) {
        sql += " AND status = ?";
        params.push(filter.status);
      }

      if (!filter.includeArchived) {
        sql += " AND archived_at IS NULL";
      }
    } else {
      sql += " AND archived_at IS NULL";
    }

    sql += " ORDER BY created_at ASC";

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(sql);
    const rows = stmt.all(...params) as unknown as GoalRow[];
    return rows.map(mapGoal);
  }

  async updateGoal(goalId: string, input: UpdateGoalInput): Promise<Goal> {
    const existing = await this.findGoalById(goalId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Goal not found: ${goalId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const title = input.title !== undefined ? input.title.trim() : existing.title;
    const description =
      input.description !== undefined
        ? input.description === null || input.description.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.description.trim()
        : existing.description;
    const targetDate =
      input.targetDate !== undefined
        ? input.targetDate === null || input.targetDate.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.targetDate.trim()
        : existing.targetDate;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `UPDATE goals SET title = ?, description = ?, target_date = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    );
    stmt.run(title, description, targetDate, now, goalId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      workspaceId: existing.workspaceId,
      projectId: existing.projectId,
      parentGoalId: existing.parentGoalId,
      title,
      description,
      targetDate,
      status: existing.status,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      archivedAt: existing.archivedAt
    };
  }

  async findDescendantIds(goalId: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const sql = `
      WITH RECURSIVE descendants(id) AS (
        SELECT id FROM goals WHERE parent_goal_id = ?
        UNION ALL
        SELECT g.id FROM goals g JOIN descendants d ON g.parent_goal_id = d.id
      )
      SELECT id FROM descendants;
    `;
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(sql);
    const rows = stmt.all(goalId) as unknown as { id: string }[];
    return rows.map((r) => r.id);
  }

  async countIndependentGoals(workspaceId: string): Promise<number> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM goals WHERE workspace_id = ? AND project_id IS NULL AND archived_at IS NULL"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(workspaceId) as { count: number } | undefined;
    return row ? Number(row.count) : 0;
  }

  async countProjectGoals(projectId: string): Promise<number> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM goals WHERE project_id = ? AND archived_at IS NULL"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(projectId) as { count: number } | undefined;
    return row ? Number(row.count) : 0;
  }
}

FILE: backend/src/infrastructure/repositories/sqlite-milestone.repository.ts

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  Milestone,
  MilestoneStatus,
  UpdateMilestoneInput
} from "../../modules/milestone/milestone.types.ts";
import type { MilestoneRepository } from "../../modules/milestone/milestone.repository.ts";

interface MilestoneRow {
  id: string;
  goal_id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  target_date: string | null;
  status: MilestoneStatus;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  completed_at: string | null;
}

function mapMilestone(row: MilestoneRow): Milestone {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    goalId: row.goal_id,
    title: row.title,
    description: row.description,
    targetDate: row.target_date,
    status: row.status,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    completedAt: row.completed_at
  };
}

export class SqliteMilestoneRepository implements MilestoneRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async createMilestone(input: {
    goalId: string;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    targetDate: string | null;
  }): Promise<Milestone> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const status: MilestoneStatus = "PENDING";
    const description = input.description?.trim() ? input.description.trim() : null;
    const targetDate = input.targetDate?.trim() ? input.targetDate.trim() : null;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO milestones (
        id, goal_id, title, description, target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, completed_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL)`
    );

    stmt.run(
      id,
      input.goalId,
      input.title.trim(),
      description,
      targetDate,
      status,
      now,
      now
    );

    return {
      id,
      goalId: input.goalId,
      title: input.title.trim(),
      description,
      targetDate,
      status,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt: null
    };
  }

  async findMilestoneById(milestoneId: string): Promise<Milestone | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, title, description, target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, completed_at
       FROM milestones WHERE id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(milestoneId) as MilestoneRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapMilestone(row) : null;
  }

  async listMilestonesByGoal(goalId: string): Promise<Milestone[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, title, description, target_date, status, created_at, updated_at, completed_at
       FROM milestones WHERE goal_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(goalId) as unknown as MilestoneRow[];
    return rows.map(mapMilestone);
  }

  async updateMilestone(
    milestoneId: string,
    input: UpdateMilestoneInput
  ): Promise<Milestone> {
    const existing = await this.findMilestoneById(milestoneId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Milestone not found: ${milestoneId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const title = input.title !== undefined ? input.title.trim() : existing.title;
    const description =
      input.description !== undefined
        ? input.description === null || input.description.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.description.trim()
        : existing.description;
    const targetDate =
      input.targetDate !== undefined
        ? input.targetDate === null || input.targetDate.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.targetDate.trim()
        : existing.targetDate;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `UPDATE milestones SET title = ?, description = ?, target_date = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    );
    stmt.run(title, description, targetDate, now, milestoneId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      goalId: existing.goalId,
      title,
      description,
      targetDate,
      status: existing.status,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt: existing.completedAt
    };
  }

  async completeMilestone(
    milestoneId: string,
    completedAt: string
  ): Promise<Milestone> {
    const existing = await this.findMilestoneById(milestoneId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Milestone not found: ${milestoneId}`);
    }

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `UPDATE milestones SET status = 'COMPLETED', completed_at = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    );
    stmt.run(completedAt, completedAt, milestoneId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      goalId: existing.goalId,
      title: existing.title,
      description: existing.description,
      targetDate: existing.targetDate,
      status: "COMPLETED",
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: completedAt,
      completedAt
    };
  }
}

FILE: backend/src/infrastructure/repositories/sqlite-action.repository.ts
============================================================
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  Action,
  ActionExecutionData,
  ActionStatus,
  RecordExecutionInput,
  UpdateActionInput
} from "../../modules/action/action.types.ts";
import type { ActionRepository } from "../../modules/action/action.repository.ts";

interface ActionRow {
  id: string;
  goal_id: string;
  milestone_id: string | null;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  status: ActionStatus;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  completed_at: string | null;
  archived_at: string | null;
}

interface ActionExecutionRow {
  action_id: string;
  actual_started_at: string | null;
  actual_completed_at: string | null;
  actual_duration_minutes: number | null;
  execution_note: string | null;
  updated_at: string;
}

function mapAction(row: ActionRow): Action {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    goalId: row.goal_id,
    milestoneId: row.milestone_id,
    title: row.title,
    description: row.description,
    status: row.status,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    completedAt: row.completed_at,
    archivedAt: row.archived_at
  };
}

function mapExecution(row: ActionExecutionRow): ActionExecutionData {
  return {
    actionId: row.action_id,
    actualStartedAt: row.actual_started_at,
    actualCompletedAt: row.actual_completed_at,
    actualDurationMinutes: row.actual_duration_minutes,
    executionNote: row.execution_note
  };
}

export class SqliteActionRepository implements ActionRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async create(input: {
    goalId: string;
    milestoneId: string | null;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    status: "TODO";
  }): Promise<Action> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const description = input.description?.trim() ? input.description.trim() : null;
    const milestoneId = input.milestoneId ?? null;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO actions (
        id, goal_id, milestone_id, title, description, status,
        created_at, updated_at, completed_at, archived_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL)`
    );

    stmt.run(
      id,
      input.goalId,
      milestoneId,
      input.title.trim(),
      description,
      input.status,
      now,
      now
    );

    return {
      id,
      goalId: input.goalId,
      milestoneId,
      title: input.title.trim(),
      description,
      status: input.status,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt: null,
      archivedAt: null
    };
  }

  async findById(actionId: string): Promise<Action | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, milestone_id, title, description, status,
              created_at, updated_at, completed_at, archived_at
       FROM actions WHERE id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(actionId) as ActionRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapAction(row) : null;
  }

  async listByGoal(goalId: string): Promise<Action[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, milestone_id, title, description, status,
              created_at, updated_at, completed_at, archived_at
       FROM actions WHERE goal_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(goalId) as unknown as ActionRow[];
    return rows.map(mapAction);
  }

  async listByMilestone(milestoneId: string): Promise<Action[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, goal_id, milestone_id, title, description, status,
              created_at, updated_at, completed_at, archived_at
       FROM actions WHERE milestone_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(milestoneId) as unknown as ActionRow[];
    return rows.map(mapAction);
  }

  async update(actionId: string, input: UpdateActionInput): Promise<Action> {
    const existing = await this.findById(actionId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Action not found: ${actionId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const title = input.title !== undefined ? input.title.trim() : existing.title;
    const description =
      input.description !== undefined
        ? input.description === null || input.description.trim() === ""
          ? null
          : input.description.trim()
        : existing.description;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "UPDATE actions SET title = ?, description = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?"
    );
    stmt.run(title, description, now, actionId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      goalId: existing.goalId,
      milestoneId: existing.milestoneId,
      title,
      description,
      status: existing.status,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt: existing.completedAt,
      archivedAt: existing.archivedAt
    };
  }

  async updateStatus(
    actionId: string,
    status: ActionStatus,
    completedAt: string | null
  ): Promise<Action> {
    const existing = await this.findById(actionId);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Action not found: ${actionId}`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "UPDATE actions SET status = ?, completed_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?"
    );
    stmt.run(status, completedAt, now, actionId);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      goalId: existing.goalId,
      milestoneId: existing.milestoneId,
      title: existing.title,
      description: existing.description,
      status,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      completedAt,
      archivedAt: existing.archivedAt
    };
  }

  async saveExecution(
    actionId: string,
    input: RecordExecutionInput
  ): Promise<ActionExecutionData> {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const actualStartedAt = input.actualStartedAt ?? null;
    const actualCompletedAt = input.actualCompletedAt ?? null;
    const actualDurationMinutes = input.actualDurationMinutes ?? null;
    const executionNote = input.executionNote?.trim() ? input.executionNote.trim() : null;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO action_executions (
        action_id, actual_started_at, actual_completed_at, actual_duration_minutes, execution_note, updated_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(action_id) DO UPDATE SET
        actual_started_at = excluded.actual_started_at,
        actual_completed_at = excluded.actual_completed_at,
        actual_duration_minutes = excluded.actual_duration_minutes,
        execution_note = excluded.execution_note,
        updated_at = excluded.updated_at`
    );

    stmt.run(
      actionId,
      actualStartedAt,
      actualCompletedAt,
      actualDurationMinutes,
      executionNote,
      now
    );

    return {
      actionId,
      actualStartedAt,
      actualCompletedAt,
      actualDurationMinutes,
      executionNote
    };
  }

  async getExecution(actionId: string): Promise<ActionExecutionData | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT action_id, actual_started_at, actual_completed_at, actual_duration_minutes, execution_note, updated_at
       FROM action_executions WHERE action_id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(actionId) as ActionExecutionRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapExecution(row) : null;
  }
}

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type {
  DailyPlan,
  DailyPlanId,
  PlanningDateRange,
  ScheduleEntry,
  ScheduleId
} from "../../modules/planning/planning.types.ts";
import type { PlanningRepository } from "../../modules/planning/planning.repository.ts";

interface DailyPlanRow {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  date: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

interface ScheduleRow {
  id: string;
  action_id: string;
  daily_plan_id: string | null;
  start_at: string;
  end_at: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

function mapDailyPlan(row: DailyPlanRow): DailyPlan {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    userId: row.user_id,
    date: row.date,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at
  };
}

function mapSchedule(row: ScheduleRow): ScheduleEntry {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    actionId: row.action_id,
    dailyPlanId: row.daily_plan_id,
    startAt: row.start_at,
    endAt: row.end_at,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at
  };
}

export class SqlitePlanningRepository implements PlanningRepository {
  private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection;

  constructor(connection: DatabaseConnection) {
    this.connection = connection;
  }

  async createDailyPlan(input: Omit<DailyPlan, "id">): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO daily_plans (id, user_id, date, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
    );
    stmt.run(id, input.userId, input.date, input.createdAt, input.updatedAt);

    return {
      id,
      userId: input.userId,
      date: input.date,
      createdAt: input.createdAt,
      updatedAt: input.updatedAt
    };
  }

  async getDailyPlan(id: DailyPlanId): Promise<DailyPlan | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, user_id, date, created_at, updated_at FROM daily_plans WHERE id = ?"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(id) as DailyPlanRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapDailyPlan(row) : null;
  }

  async findDailyPlanByUserAndDate(userId: string, date: string): Promise<DailyPlan | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "SELECT id, user_id, date, created_at, updated_at FROM daily_plans WHERE user_id = ? AND date = ?"
    );
    const row = stmt.get(userId, date) as DailyPlanRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapDailyPlan(row) : null;
  }

  async updateDailyPlan(
    id: DailyPlanId,
    patch: Partial<Pick<DailyPlan, "date" | "updatedAt">>
  ): Promise<DailyPlan> {
    const existing = await this.getDailyPlan(id);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Daily Plan not found: ${id}`);
    }

    const date = patch.date ?? existing.date;
    const updatedAt = patch.updatedAt ?? new Date().toISOString();

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      "UPDATE daily_plans SET date = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?"
    );
    stmt.run(date, updatedAt, id);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      userId: existing.userId,
      date,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt
    };
  }

  async listSchedulesByDailyPlan(dailyPlanId: DailyPlanId): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, action_id, daily_plan_id, start_at, end_at, created_at, updated_at
       FROM schedules WHERE daily_plan_id = ? ORDER BY start_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(dailyPlanId) as unknown as ScheduleRow[];
    return rows.map(mapSchedule);
  }

  async createSchedule(input: Omit<ScheduleEntry, "id">): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO schedules (id, action_id, daily_plan_id, start_at, end_at, created_at, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    );
    stmt.run(
      id,
      input.actionId,
      input.dailyPlanId,
      input.startAt,
      input.endAt,
      input.createdAt,
      input.updatedAt
    );

    return {
      id,
      actionId: input.actionId,
      dailyPlanId: input.dailyPlanId,
      startAt: input.startAt,
      endAt: input.endAt,
      createdAt: input.createdAt,
      updatedAt: input.updatedAt
    };
  }

  async getSchedule(id: ScheduleId): Promise<ScheduleEntry | null> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT id, action_id, daily_plan_id, start_at, end_at, created_at, updated_at
       FROM schedules WHERE id = ?`
    );
    const row = stmt.get(id) as ScheduleRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapSchedule(row) : null;
  }

  async updateSchedule(
    id: ScheduleId,
    patch: Partial<Pick<ScheduleEntry, "startAt" | "endAt" | "dailyPlanId" | "updatedAt">>
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry> {
    const existing = await this.getSchedule(id);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`Schedule not found: ${id}`);
    }

    const startAt = patch.startAt ?? existing.startAt;
    const endAt = patch.endAt ?? existing.endAt;
    const dailyPlanId = patch.dailyPlanId === undefined ? existing.dailyPlanId : patch.dailyPlanId;
    const updatedAt = patch.updatedAt ?? new Date().toISOString();

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `UPDATE schedules SET start_at = ?, end_at = ?, daily_plan_id = ?, updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    );
    stmt.run(startAt, endAt, dailyPlanId, updatedAt, id);

    return {
      id: existing.id,
      actionId: existing.actionId,
      dailyPlanId,
      startAt,
      endAt,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt
    };
  }

  async deleteSchedule(id: ScheduleId): Promise<void> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare("DELETE FROM schedules WHERE id = ?");
    stmt.run(id);
  }

  async listSchedulesByDateRange(
    userId: string,
    range: PlanningDateRange,
    workspaceId?: string
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    const startRange = range.startDate.includes("T") ? range.startDate : `${range.startDate}T00:00:00.000Z`;
    const endRange = range.endDate.includes("T") ? range.endDate : `${range.endDate}T23:59:59.999Z`;

    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT DISTINCT s.id, s.action_id, s.daily_plan_id, s.start_at, s.end_at, s.created_at, s.updated_at
       FROM schedules s
       JOIN actions a ON s.action_id = a.id
       JOIN goals g ON a.goal_id = g.id
       JOIN workspaces w ON g.workspace_id = w.id
       WHERE w.user_id = ?
         AND (? IS NULL OR w.id = ?)
         AND julianday(s.start_at) <= julianday(?)
         AND julianday(s.end_at) > julianday(?)
       ORDER BY s.start_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(userId, workspaceId ?? null, workspaceId ?? null, endRange, startRange) as unknown as ScheduleRow[];
    return rows.map(mapSchedule);
  }

  async findOverlappingSchedules(
    userId: string,
    startAt: string,
    endAt: string,
    excludeScheduleId?: ScheduleId,
  ): Promise<ScheduleEntry[]> {
    const stmt = this.connection.db.prepare(
      `SELECT DISTINCT s.id, s.action_id, s.daily_plan_id, s.start_at, s.end_at, s.created_at, s.updated_at
       FROM schedules s
       JOIN actions a ON s.action_id = a.id
       JOIN goals g ON a.goal_id = g.id
       JOIN workspaces w ON g.workspace_id = w.id
       WHERE w.user_id = ?
         AND julianday(s.start_at) < julianday(?)
         AND julianday(s.end_at) > julianday(?)
         AND (? IS NULL OR s.id <> ?)
       ORDER BY s.start_at ASC`
    );
    const rows = stmt.all(userId, endAt, startAt, excludeScheduleId ?? null, excludeScheduleId ?? null) as unknown as ScheduleRow[];
    return rows.map(mapSchedule);
  }
}

FILE: backend/src/infrastructure/repositories/sqlite-measurement.repository.ts

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { DatabaseConnection } from "../database/sqlite.ts";
import type { MeasurementRepository } from "../../modules/measurement/measurement.repository.ts";
import type { Measurement, MeasurementHistoryQuery, MeasurementRecord } from "../../modules/measurement/measurement.types.ts";

interface MeasurementRow {
  id: string; goal_id: string; label: string; description: string | null; value_type: string;
  unit: string | null; target_value: string | null; created_at: string; updated_at: string; archived_at: string | null;
}
interface RecordRow {
  id: string; measurement_id: string; value_json: string; recorded_at: string;
  created_at: string; note: string | null;
}

function mapMeasurement(row: MeasurementRow): Measurement {
  return {
    id: row.id, goalId: row.goal_id, label: row.label, description: row.description,
    valueType: row.value_type, unit: row.unit,
    targetValue: row.target_value === null ? null : JSON.parse(row.target_value) as Measurement["targetValue"],
    createdAt: row.created_at, updatedAt: row.updated_at, archivedAt: row.archived_at,
  };
}
function mapRecord(row: RecordRow): MeasurementRecord {
  return {
    id: row.id, measurementId: row.measurement_id,
    value: JSON.parse(row.value_json) as MeasurementRecord["value"],
    recordedAt: row.recorded_at, createdAt: row.created_at, note: row.note,
  };
}

export class SqliteMeasurementRepository implements MeasurementRepository {
  constructor(private readonly connection: DatabaseConnection) {}

  async createMeasurement(input: Omit<Measurement, "id">): Promise<Measurement> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    this.connection.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO measurements
      (id, goal_id, label, description, value_type, unit, target_value, created_at, updated_at, archived_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
      .run(id, input.goalId, input.label, input.description, input.valueType, input.unit,
        input.targetValue === null ? null : JSON.stringify(input.targetValue),
        input.createdAt, input.updatedAt, input.archivedAt);
    return { ...input, id };
  }

  async getMeasurement(id: string): Promise<Measurement | null> {
    const row = this.connection.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM measurements WHERE id = ?").get(id) as MeasurementRow | undefined;
    return row ? mapMeasurement(row) : null;
  }

  async listMeasurementsByGoal(goalId: string, includeArchived: boolean): Promise<Measurement[]> {
    const rows = this.connection.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM measurements WHERE goal_id = ? ${includeArchived ? "" : "AND archived_at IS NULL"} ORDER BY created_at, id`)
      .all(goalId) as unknown as MeasurementRow[];
    return rows.map(mapMeasurement);
  }

  async updateMeasurement(id: string, patch: Partial<Pick<Measurement, "label" | "description" | "unit" | "targetValue" | "updatedAt">>): Promise<Measurement> {
    const current = await this.getMeasurement(id);
    if (!current) throw new Error("Measurement not found during update.");
    const next = { ...current, ...patch };
    this.connection.db.prepare(`UPDATE measurements SET label = ?, description = ?, unit = ?, target_value = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`)
      .run(next.label, next.description, next.unit, next.targetValue === null ? null : JSON.stringify(next.targetValue), next.updatedAt, id);
    return next;
  }

  async archiveMeasurement(id: string, archivedAt: string): Promise<Measurement> {
    this.connection.db.prepare("UPDATE measurements SET archived_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?")
      .run(archivedAt, archivedAt, id);
    const result = await this.getMeasurement(id);
    if (!result) throw new Error("Measurement not found during archive.");
    return result;
  }

  async createRecord(input: Omit<MeasurementRecord, "id">): Promise<MeasurementRecord> {
    const id = randomUUID();
    this.connection.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO measurement_records
      (id, measurement_id, value_json, recorded_at, created_at, note) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
      .run(id, input.measurementId, JSON.stringify(input.value), input.recordedAt, input.createdAt, input.note);
    return { ...input, id };
  }

  async listRecords(measurementId: string, query: MeasurementHistoryQuery): Promise<MeasurementRecord[]> {
    const where = ["measurement_id = ?"];
    const params: (string | number)[] = [measurementId];
    if (query.from) { where.push("recorded_at >= ?"); params.push(query.from); }
    if (query.to) { where.push("recorded_at <= ?"); params.push(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(query.to) ? `${query.to}T23:59:59.999Z` : query.to); }
    let sql = `SELECT * FROM measurement_records WHERE ${where.join(" AND ")} ORDER BY recorded_at DESC, created_at DESC, id DESC`;
    if (query.limit !== undefined) { sql += " LIMIT ?"; params.push(query.limit); }
    if (query.offset !== undefined) { if (query.limit === undefined) sql += " LIMIT -1"; sql += " OFFSET ?"; params.push(query.offset); }
    return (this.connection.db.prepare(sql).all(...params) as unknown as RecordRow[]).map(mapRecord);
  }
}

FILE: backend/src/infrastructure/database/schema.ts
