/**
 * FLOW-UP — SQLite Database Schema & Migrations
 * MVP temporary database configuration.
 *
 * Enforces:
 * - Foreign keys enabled
 * - Multiple owner-scoped workspaces per user
 * - Project workspace foreign keys
 * - Goal hierarchy relational integrity
 * - Milestone/Action/Schedule constraints
 */

export const SCHEMA_SQL = `
-- Workspaces are user-owned containers; a user may have multiple.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workspaces (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workspaces_user_id ON workspaces(user_id, created_at);

-- Projects table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES workspaces(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- Goals table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS goals (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES workspaces(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  project_id TEXT REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  parent_goal_id TEXT REFERENCES goals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  target_date TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  archived_at TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_goals_workspace_id ON goals(workspace_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_goals_project_id ON goals(project_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_goals_parent_goal_id ON goals(parent_goal_id);

-- Milestones table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS milestones (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  goal_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES goals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  target_date TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  completed_at TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_milestones_goal_id ON milestones(goal_id);

-- Actions table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actions (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  goal_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES goals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  milestone_id TEXT REFERENCES milestones(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'TODO',
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  completed_at TEXT,
  archived_at TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_actions_goal_id ON actions(goal_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_actions_milestone_id ON actions(milestone_id);

-- Action executions table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS action_executions (
  action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES actions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  actual_started_at TEXT,
  actual_completed_at TEXT,
  actual_duration_minutes INTEGER,
  execution_note TEXT,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- Daily Plans table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_plans (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  date TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE(user_id, date)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_daily_plans_user_date ON daily_plans(user_id, date);

-- Schedules table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schedules (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  action_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES actions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  daily_plan_id TEXT REFERENCES daily_plans(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  start_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  end_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schedules_action_id ON schedules(action_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schedules_daily_plan_id ON schedules(daily_plan_id);

-- B06 Measurement definitions and immutable value history.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS measurements (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  goal_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES goals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  label TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  value_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  unit TEXT,
  target_value TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  archived_at TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_measurements_goal_id ON measurements(goal_id);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS measurement_records (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  measurement_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES measurements(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  value_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  recorded_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  note TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_measurement_records_measurement_recorded
  ON measurement_records(measurement_id, recorded_at, created_at);

-- B10 owns these AI workflow records; domain tables remain owned by their modules.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ai_proposals (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  operation_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  proposal_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,
  proposal_data TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ai_proposals_user_created
  ON ai_proposals(user_id, created_at);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ai_usage (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ai_usage_user_created
  ON ai_usage(user_id, created_at);
`;

FILE: backend/src/infrastructure/database/sqlite.ts
============================================================
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { SCHEMA_SQL } from "./schema.ts";

export interface DatabaseConnection {
  readonly db: DatabaseSync;
  runInTransaction<T>(fn: () => T): T;
  close(): void;
}

export interface SqliteOptions {
  dbPath?: string;
}

/**
 * Initializes SQLite database connection with foreign keys enabled
 * and executes initial schema migrations.
 */
export function createSqliteDatabase(options: SqliteOptions = {}): DatabaseConnection {
  const dbPath = options.dbPath ?? ":memory:";

  if (dbPath !== ":memory:") {
    const dir = path.dirname(path.resolve(dbPath));
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath);

  // Enable foreign keys
  db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;");

  if (dbPath !== ":memory:") {
    try {
      db.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;");
    } catch {
      // WAL may not be supported on all environments, ignore if fails
    }
  }

  // Initialize schema
  db.exec(SCHEMA_SQL);
  migrateMultipleWorkspaces(db);

  return {
    get db() {
      return db;
    },
    runInTransaction<T>(fn: () => T): T {
      db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION;");
      try {
        const result = fn();
        db.exec("COMMIT;");
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        try {
          db.exec("ROLLBACK;");
        } catch {
          // ignore rollback failure if transaction already ended
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },
    close() {
      db.close();
    }
  };
}

/** Remove the legacy UNIQUE(user_id) constraint without changing existing workspace IDs or data. */
function migrateMultipleWorkspaces(db: DatabaseSync): void {
  const indexes = db.prepare("PRAGMA index_list('workspaces')").all() as Array<{ name: string; unique: number; origin: string }>;
  const hasLegacyUserUniqueness = indexes.some((index) => {
    if (index.unique !== 1 || index.origin !== "u") return false;
    const columns = db.prepare(`PRAGMA index_info('${index.name.replaceAll("'", "''")}')`).all() as Array<{ name: string }>;
    return columns.length === 1 && columns[0]?.name === "user_id";
  });
  if (!hasLegacyUserUniqueness) return;

  db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF;");
  try {
    db.exec(`
      BEGIN IMMEDIATE;
      CREATE TABLE workspaces_migration (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        name TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      );
      INSERT INTO workspaces_migration (id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at)
        SELECT id, user_id, name, created_at, updated_at FROM workspaces;
      DROP TABLE workspaces;
      ALTER TABLE workspaces_migration RENAME TO workspaces;
      CREATE INDEX idx_workspaces_user_id ON workspaces(user_id, created_at);
      COMMIT;
    `);
  } catch (error) {
    try { db.exec("ROLLBACK;"); } catch { /* transaction may already have rolled back */ }
    throw error;
  } finally {
    db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;");
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend/tests/integration/api.integration.test.ts
============================================================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll } from "vitest";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import os from "node:os";
import { Readable } from "node:stream";
import { EventEmitter } from "node:events";
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import { createApp, type AppInstance } from "../../src/server/app.ts";
import { createApiHandler } from "../../src/api/index.ts";

interface HttpResponse {
  status: number;
  headers: Record<string, string>;
  json: <T = unknown>() => Promise<T>;
  text: () => Promise<string>;
}

// In-process dispatcher supporting sandboxed test execution without requiring TCP sockets
async function dispatchInProcess(
  app: AppInstance,
  method: string,
  urlPath: string,
  options: { headers?: Record<string, string>; body?: string } = {}
): Promise<HttpResponse> {
  const handler = createApiHandler(app.container, "*");
  return new Promise<HttpResponse>((resolve, reject) => {
    try {
      const bodyContent = options.body ?? null;
      let pushed = false;

      const req = new Readable({
        read() {
          if (!pushed) {
            pushed = true;
            if (bodyContent) {
              this.push(bodyContent);
            }
            this.push(null);
          }
        }
      }) as unknown as IncomingMessage & { method: string; url: string; headers: Record<string, string> };

      const lowerHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};
      if (options.headers) {
        for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(options.headers)) {
          lowerHeaders[k.toLowerCase()] = v;
        }
      }

      req.method = method;
      req.url = urlPath;
      req.headers = lowerHeaders;

      let statusCode = 200;
      const resHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};
      let resBody = "";

      const res = Object.assign(new EventEmitter(), {
        writableEnded: false,
        writeHead(status: number, headers?: Record<string, string>) {
          statusCode = status;
          if (headers) Object.assign(resHeaders, headers);
        },
        end(chunk?: unknown) {
          if (typeof chunk === "string") resBody += chunk;
          else if (Buffer.isBuffer(chunk)) resBody += chunk.toString();
          this.writableEnded = true;
          resolve({
            status: statusCode,
            headers: resHeaders,
            async json<T = unknown>() {
              return JSON.parse(resBody) as T;
            },
            async text() {
              return resBody;
            }
          });
        }
      }) as unknown as ServerResponse;

      handler(req, res).catch(reject);
    } catch (err) {
      reject(err);
    }
  });
}

describe("FLOW-UP Backend API Integration Tests", () => {
  let app: AppInstance;
  let baseUrl: string;
  let useInProcess = false;

  const userA = {
    email: "user_a@example.com",
    password: "Password123!",
    token: "dev-token:user-a:user_a@example.com"
  };

  const userB = {
    email: "user_b@example.com",
    password: "Password123!",
    token: "dev-token:user-b:user_b@example.com"
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    app = createApp({
      dbPath: ":memory:",
      corsOrigin: "*",
      config: {
        auth: {
          devMode: true
        }
      }
    });

    try {
      await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        app.server.once("error", reject);
        app.server.listen(0, () => resolve());
      });

      const addr = app.server.address();
      const port = typeof addr === "object" && addr ? addr.port : 3001;
      baseUrl = `http://localhost:${port}`;

      // Probe if TCP socket connect works in this environment
      await fetch(`${baseUrl}/health`);
    } catch {
      // In sandboxed environments on Windows, TCP sockets are blocked
      useInProcess = true;
    }
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await app.close();
  });

  async function request(
    method: string,
    urlPath: string,
    options: { headers?: Record<string, string>; body?: string } = {},
    targetApp = app
  ): Promise<HttpResponse> {
    if (useInProcess) {
      return dispatchInProcess(targetApp, method, urlPath, options);
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${urlPath}`, {
        method,
        headers: options.headers,
        body: options.body
      });
      return {
        status: res.status,
        headers: Object.fromEntries(res.headers.entries()),
        json: () => res.json(),
        text: () => res.text()
      };
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const details = err && typeof err === "object" ? err as { code?: unknown; message?: unknown; cause?: unknown } : {};
      const cause = details.cause && typeof details.cause === "object" ? details.cause as { code?: unknown } : {};
      if (details.code === "EACCES" || (typeof details.message === "string" && details.message.includes("EACCES")) || cause.code === "EACCES") {
        useInProcess = true;
        return dispatchInProcess(targetApp, method, urlPath, options);
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  // FLOW 1: Server boots and health check works
  it("Flow 1: Server boots and health check responds with 200 OK", async () => {
    const res = await request("GET", "/health");
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);

    const data = await res.json<{ success: boolean; data: { status: string; timestamp: string } }>();
    expect(data.success).toBe(true);
    expect(data.data.status).toBe("ok");
    expect(typeof data.data.timestamp).toBe("string");

    const apiRes = await request("GET", "/api/health");
    expect(apiRes.status).toBe(200);
  });

  // FLOW 2: Requests without token are rejected
  it("Flow 2: Requests without token to protected endpoints are rejected with 401", async () => {
    const endpoints = [
      { method: "GET", path: "/api/workspaces/current" },
      { method: "POST", path: "/api/workspaces/initialize" },
      { method: "GET", path: "/api/goals" },
      { method: "POST", path: "/api/goals" },
      { method: "GET", path: "/api/projects" },
      { method: "POST", path: "/api/milestones" },
      { method: "POST", path: "/api/actions" },
      { method: "GET", path: "/api/planning/daily-plans/2026-11-18" },
      { method: "GET", path: "/api/progress/goals/goal-123" },
      { method: "GET", path: "/api/goals/goal-123/measurements" },
      { method: "POST", path: "/api/measurements" },
    ];

    for (const ep of endpoints) {
      const res = await request(ep.method, ep.path, {
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
      });
      expect(res.status).toBe(401);

      const json = await res.json<{ success: boolean; error: { code: string; message: string } }>();
      expect(json.success).toBe(false);
      expect(json.error.code).toBe("UNAUTHORIZED");
    }
  });

  // FLOW 3: Valid authentication & Personal Workspace initialization
  it("Flow 3: Valid login initializes and retrieves Personal Workspace and Project", async () => {
    // 1. Initialize personal workspace
    const initRes = await request("POST", "/api/workspaces/initialize", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      }
    });
    expect(initRes.status).toBe(201);
    const initData = await initRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { id: string; userId: string; name: string } }>();
    expect(initData.success).toBe(true);
    expect(initData.data.userId).toBe("user-a");
    expect(initData.data.name).toBe("Personal Workspace");

    const workspaceId = initData.data.id;

    // 2. Calling initialize again is idempotent and returns existing workspace
    const reInitRes = await request("POST", "/api/workspaces/initialize", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      }
    });
    expect(reInitRes.status).toBe(201);
    const reInitData = await reInitRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { id: string } }>();
    expect(reInitData.data.id).toBe(workspaceId);

    // 3. Get current workspace
    const currentRes = await request("GET", "/api/workspaces/current", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(currentRes.status).toBe(200);
    const currentData = await currentRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { id: string; userId: string } }>();
    expect(currentData.data.id).toBe(workspaceId);

    // 4. Create Project
    const createProjRes = await request("POST", "/api/projects", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        name: "IELTS Preparation",
        description: "Study project for target 8.0"
      })
    });
    expect(createProjRes.status).toBe(201);
    const projData = await createProjRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: { id: string; name: string; workspaceId: string; description: string };
    }>();
    expect(projData.success).toBe(true);
    expect(projData.data.name).toBe("IELTS Preparation");
    expect(projData.data.workspaceId).toBe(workspaceId);

    const projectId = projData.data.id;

    // 5. List projects
    const listProjRes = await request("GET", "/api/projects", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(listProjRes.status).toBe(200);
    const listProjData = await listProjRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: Array<{ id: string }> }>();
    expect(listProjData.data.some((p) => p.id === projectId)).toBe(true);

    // 6. Update project
    const updateProjRes = await request("PATCH", `/api/projects/${projectId}`, {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        name: "IELTS 8.0 Mastery"
      })
    });
    expect(updateProjRes.status).toBe(200);
    const updateProjData = await updateProjRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { name: string } }>();
    expect(updateProjData.data.name).toBe("IELTS 8.0 Mastery");
  });

  it("creates, scopes, lists, and deletes multiple owned Workspaces", async () => {
    const headers = {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
      "Content-Type": "application/json"
    };
    const create = await request("POST", "/api/workspaces", {
      headers,
      body: JSON.stringify({ name: "Side project" })
    });
    expect(create.status).toBe(201);
    const created = await create.json<{ data: { id: string; name: string; userId: string } }>();
    expect(created.data).toMatchObject({ name: "Side project", userId: "user-a" });

    const list = await request("GET", "/api/workspaces", { headers });
    const workspaces = await list.json<{ data: Array<{ id: string }> }>();
    expect(workspaces.data.map((workspace) => workspace.id)).toContain(created.data.id);

    const project = await request("POST", "/api/projects", {
      headers: { ...headers, "X-Workspace-Id": created.data.id },
      body: JSON.stringify({ name: "Scoped project" })
    });
    expect(project.status).toBe(201);
    const projectData = await project.json<{ data: { id: string; workspaceId: string } }>();
    expect(projectData.data.workspaceId).toBe(created.data.id);

    const goal = await request("POST", "/api/goals", {
      headers: { ...headers, "X-Workspace-Id": created.data.id },
      body: JSON.stringify({ title: "Goal in side project" })
    });
    expect(goal.status).toBe(201);
    const goalData = await goal.json<{ data: { id: string; workspaceId: string } }>();
    expect(goalData.data.workspaceId).toBe(created.data.id);

    const forbidden = await request("GET", "/api/workspaces/overview", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userB.token}`, "X-Workspace-Id": created.data.id }
    });
    expect(forbidden.status).toBe(403);

    const deleted = await request("DELETE", `/api/workspaces/${created.data.id}`, { headers });
    expect(deleted.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await app.container.repositories.workspace.findProjectById(projectData.data.id)).toBeNull();
    expect(await app.container.repositories.goal.findGoalById(goalData.data.id)).toBeNull();
    const projects = await request("GET", "/api/projects", {
      headers: { ...headers, "X-Workspace-Id": created.data.id }
    });
    expect(projects.status).toBe(404);
  });

  // FLOW 4: Create and retrieve Goal & hierarchy
  let createdGoalId: string;
  let childGoalId: string;
  let testProjectId: string;
  let measurementId: string;

  it("Flow 4: Create and retrieve Goal and Goal hierarchy", async () => {
    const projListRes = await request("GET", "/api/projects", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    const projList = await projListRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: Array<{ id: string }> }>();
    testProjectId = projList.data[0].id;

    // 1. Create Parent Goal
    const createGoalRes = await request("POST", "/api/goals", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        title: "Achieve IELTS Band 8.0",
        description: "Focus on Writing & Speaking",
        targetDate: "2026-12-31",
        projectId: testProjectId
      })
    });
    expect(createGoalRes.status).toBe(201);
    const goalData = await createGoalRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: { id: string; title: string; parentGoalId: string | null; projectId: string };
    }>();
    expect(goalData.success).toBe(true);
    expect(goalData.data.title).toBe("Achieve IELTS Band 8.0");
    expect(goalData.data.parentGoalId).toBeNull();
    createdGoalId = goalData.data.id;

    // 2. Retrieve Goal by ID
    const getGoalRes = await request("GET", `/api/goals/${createdGoalId}`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(getGoalRes.status).toBe(200);
    const getGoalData = await getGoalRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { id: string } }>();
    expect(getGoalData.data.id).toBe(createdGoalId);

    // 3. Add Child Goal
    const addChildRes = await request("POST", `/api/goals/${createdGoalId}/children`, {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        title: "Writing Sub-score 7.5",
        targetDate: "2026-11-30"
      })
    });
    expect(addChildRes.status).toBe(201);
    const childData = await addChildRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: { id: string; parentGoalId: string; projectId: string };
    }>();
    expect(childData.success).toBe(true);
    expect(childData.data.parentGoalId).toBe(createdGoalId);
    childGoalId = childData.data.id;

    // 4. Retrieve Goal hierarchy tree
    const hierarchyRes = await request("GET", "/api/goals/hierarchy", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(hierarchyRes.status).toBe(200);
    const hierarchyData = await hierarchyRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: Array<{ goal: { id: string; title: string }; children: Array<{ goal: { id: string } }> }>;
    }>();
    expect(hierarchyData.success).toBe(true);
    const parentNode = hierarchyData.data.find((node) => node.goal.id === createdGoalId);
    expect(parentNode).toBeDefined();
    expect(parentNode!.children.some((c) => c.goal.id === childGoalId)).toBe(true);
  });

  // FLOW 5: Create Milestone and Action under correct Goal
  it("Flow 5: Create Milestone and Action belonging to the Goal, execute Action, and check Progress", async () => {
    // 1. Create Milestone under Goal
    const createMsRes = await request("POST", "/api/milestones", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        goalId: createdGoalId,
        title: "Complete 20 Essay Practice Tasks",
        targetDate: "2026-11-25"
      })
    });
    expect(createMsRes.status).toBe(201);
    const msData = await createMsRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: { id: string; goalId: string; status: string; completedAt: string | null };
    }>();
    expect(msData.success).toBe(true);
    expect(msData.data.goalId).toBe(createdGoalId);
    expect(msData.data.status).toBe("PENDING");

    const milestoneId = msData.data.id;

    // List milestones by goal
    const listMsRes = await request("GET", `/api/goals/${createdGoalId}/milestones`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(listMsRes.status).toBe(200);
    const listMsData = await listMsRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: Array<{ id: string }> }>();
    expect(listMsData.data.some((m) => m.id === milestoneId)).toBe(true);

    // 2. Create Action under Goal and Milestone
    const createActRes = await request("POST", "/api/actions", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        goalId: createdGoalId,
        milestoneId,
        title: "Write Task 2 Essay: Climate Change",
        description: "Draft 250 words and self-evaluate"
      })
    });
    expect(createActRes.status).toBe(201);
    const actData = await createActRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: { id: string; goalId: string; milestoneId: string; status: string };
    }>();
    expect(actData.success).toBe(true);
    expect(actData.data.goalId).toBe(createdGoalId);
    expect(actData.data.milestoneId).toBe(milestoneId);
    expect(actData.data.status).toBe("TODO");

    const actionId = actData.data.id;

    // 3. Update Action Status to IN_PROGRESS
    const updateStatusRes = await request("PATCH", `/api/actions/${actionId}/status`, {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: "IN_PROGRESS" })
    });
    expect(updateStatusRes.status).toBe(200);
    const updatedStatusData = await updateStatusRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { status: string } }>();
    expect(updatedStatusData.data.status).toBe("IN_PROGRESS");

    // 4. Complete Action
    const completeActRes = await request("POST", `/api/actions/${actionId}/complete`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(completeActRes.status).toBe(200);
    const completedActData = await completeActRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: { status: string; completedAt: string };
    }>();
    expect(completedActData.data.status).toBe("COMPLETED");
    expect(typeof completedActData.data.completedAt).toBe("string");

    // 5. Record Action Execution data
    const recordExecRes = await request("POST", `/api/actions/${actionId}/execution`, {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        actualStartedAt: "2026-11-17T10:00:00.000Z",
        actualCompletedAt: "2026-11-17T10:45:00.000Z",
        actualDurationMinutes: 45,
        executionNote: "Finished 280 words essay on time."
      })
    });
    expect(recordExecRes.status).toBe(200);
    const execData = await recordExecRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: { actionId: string; actualDurationMinutes: number; executionNote: string };
    }>();
    expect(execData.success).toBe(true);
    expect(execData.data.actualDurationMinutes).toBe(45);

    const measurementRes = await request("POST", "/api/measurements", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ goalId: createdGoalId, label: "Practice hours", valueType: "NUMBER", unit: "hours", targetValue: 10 })
    });
    expect(measurementRes.status).toBe(201);
    const measurementData = await measurementRes.json<{ data: { id: string; label: string; unit: string } }>();
    measurementId = measurementData.data.id;
    expect(measurementData.data.unit).toBe("hours");
    const invalidMeasurement = await request("POST", "/api/measurements", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ goalId: createdGoalId, label: "Invalid unit", valueType: "TEXT", unit: "kg" })
    });
    expect(invalidMeasurement.status).toBe(400);
    const recordRes = await request("POST", `/api/measurements/${measurementId}/records`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ value: 4, note: "This week" })
    });
    expect(recordRes.status).toBe(201);
    const recordData = await recordRes.json<{ data: { value: number } }>();
    expect(recordData.data.value).toBe(4);
    const updateMeasurement = await request("PATCH", `/api/measurements/${measurementId}`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ label: "Practice hours logged" })
    });
    expect((await updateMeasurement.json<{ data: { label: string } }>()).data.label).toBe("Practice hours logged");
    const currentRes = await request("GET", `/api/measurements/${measurementId}/current`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect((await currentRes.json<{ data: { value: number } }>()).data.value).toBe(4);
    const historyRes = await request("GET", `/api/measurements/${measurementId}/history`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect((await historyRes.json<{ data: Array<{ note: string }> }>()).data[0].note).toBe("This week");

    // Retrieve Action Execution data
    const getExecRes = await request("GET", `/api/actions/${actionId}/execution`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(getExecRes.status).toBe(200);
    const fetchedExecData = await getExecRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { actualDurationMinutes: number } }>();
    expect(fetchedExecData.data.actualDurationMinutes).toBe(45);

    // 6. Complete Milestone
    const completeMsRes = await request("POST", `/api/milestones/${milestoneId}/complete`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(completeMsRes.status).toBe(200);
    const completedMsData = await completeMsRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { status: string } }>();
    expect(completedMsData.data.status).toBe("COMPLETED");

    // 7. Planning: Create Daily Plan and Schedule
    const planRes = await request("POST", "/api/planning/daily-plans", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ date: "2026-11-18" })
    });
    expect(planRes.status).toBe(201);
    const planData = await planRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { id: string; date: string } }>();
    expect(planData.data.date).toBe("2026-11-18");

    const dailyPlanId = planData.data.id;

    // Add schedule
    const schedRes = await request("POST", "/api/planning/schedules", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        actionId,
        dailyPlanId,
        startAt: "2026-11-18T09:00:00.000Z",
        endAt: "2026-11-18T10:00:00.000Z"
      })
    });
    expect(schedRes.status).toBe(201);
    const schedData = await schedRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: { id: string; actionId: string } }>();
    expect(schedData.data.actionId).toBe(actionId);

    const overlappingRes = await request("POST", "/api/planning/schedules", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ actionId, startAt: "2026-11-18T09:30:00.000Z", endAt: "2026-11-18T10:30:00.000Z" })
    });
    expect(overlappingRes.status).toBe(409);
    expect((await overlappingRes.json<{ error: { code: string } }>()).error.code).toBe("SCHEDULE_CONFLICT");

    const movedRes = await request("PATCH", `/api/planning/schedules/${schedData.data.id}`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ startAt: "2026-11-18T10:00:00.000Z", endAt: "2026-11-18T11:00:00.000Z" })
    });
    expect(movedRes.status).toBe(200);

    const adjacentRes = await request("POST", "/api/planning/schedules", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ actionId, startAt: "2026-11-18T11:00:00.000Z", endAt: "2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z" })
    });
    expect(adjacentRes.status).toBe(201);

    // 8. Progress endpoint: verify unconfigured rules return RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED & null progress
    const progRes = await request("GET", `/api/progress/goals/${createdGoalId}`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
    });
    expect(progRes.status).toBe(200);
    const progData = await progRes.json<{
      success: boolean;
      data: { goalId: string; calculationStatus: string; goalProgress: { progressValue: number | null; calculationStatus: string }; actionCompletion: { completedActions: number; completionValue: number | null } | null; measurementProgress: Array<{ currentValue: number; calculationStatus: string }> };
    }>();
    expect(progData.success).toBe(true);
    expect(progData.data.goalId).toBe(createdGoalId);
    expect(progData.data.calculationStatus).toBe("RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED");
    expect(progData.data.goalProgress.progressValue).toBeNull();
    expect(progData.data.actionCompletion?.completedActions).toBe(1);
    expect(progData.data.actionCompletion?.completionValue).toBe(100);
    expect(progData.data.measurementProgress[0]).toMatchObject({ currentValue: 4, calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED" });
  });

  // FLOW 6: Cross-user data isolation
  it("Flow 6: Another user cannot access or mutate the current user's data", async () => {
    // 1. User B initializes their own workspace
    const initB = await request("POST", "/api/workspaces/initialize", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userB.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      }
    });
    expect(initB.status).toBe(201);
    const dataB = await initB.json<{ data: { id: string; userId: string } }>();
    expect(dataB.data.userId).toBe("user-b");

    // 2. User B attempts to access User A's Goal
    const crossGoalRes = await request("GET", `/api/goals/${createdGoalId}`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userB.token}` }
    });
    expect(crossGoalRes.status).toBe(403);
    const crossGoalData = await crossGoalRes.json<{ success: boolean; error: { code: string } }>();
    expect(crossGoalData.success).toBe(false);
    expect(crossGoalData.error.code).toBe("GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED");
    const crossMeasurementRes = await request("GET", `/api/measurements/${measurementId}`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userB.token}` }
    });
    expect(crossMeasurementRes.status).toBe(403);

    // 3. User B attempts to update User A's Goal
    const crossUpdateGoalRes = await request("PATCH", `/api/goals/${createdGoalId}`, {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${userB.token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ title: "Hacked Goal Title" })
    });
    expect(crossUpdateGoalRes.status).toBe(403);

    // 4. User B attempts to access User A's Project
    const crossProjRes = await request("GET", `/api/projects/${testProjectId}`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userB.token}` }
    });
    expect(crossProjRes.status).toBe(403);
    const crossProjData = await crossProjRes.json<{ success: boolean; error: { code: string } }>();
    expect(crossProjData.error.code).toBe("PROJECT_ACCESS_DENIED");

    // 5. User B lists goals: User B sees ONLY their own goals (empty list), not User A's
    const userBGoalsRes = await request("GET", "/api/goals", {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userB.token}` }
    });
    expect(userBGoalsRes.status).toBe(200);
    const userBGoals = await userBGoalsRes.json<{ success: boolean; data: Array<{ id: string }> }>();
    expect(userBGoals.data.length).toBe(0);
  });

  // FLOW 7: Database persistence across server restart
  it("Flow 7: Data persists safely across server restart with SQLite file", async () => {
    const tempDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "flowup-test-"));
    const tempDbPath = path.join(tempDir, "flowup-restart-test.db");

    let server1: AppInstance | null = null;
    let server2: AppInstance | null = null;
    let savedGoalId: string;

    try {
      // Phase 1: Boot Server 1 on file-based DB
      server1 = createApp({
        dbPath: tempDbPath,
        config: { auth: { devMode: true } }
      });

      // Initialize workspace on Server 1
      await request(
        "POST",
        "/api/workspaces/initialize",
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
          }
        },
        server1
      );

      // Create Goal on Server 1
      const createGoal = await request(
        "POST",
        "/api/goals",
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            title: "Persistent Goal across Reboots",
            targetDate: "2027-01-01"
          })
        },
        server1
      );
      expect(createGoal.status).toBe(201);
      const goalJson = await createGoal.json<{ data: { id: string } }>();
      savedGoalId = goalJson.data.id;

      const createdMeasurement = await request("POST", "/api/measurements", {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ goalId: savedGoalId, label: "Weekly focus", valueType: "NUMBER", unit: "hours", targetValue: 5 })
      }, server1);
      expect(createdMeasurement.status).toBe(201);
      const savedMeasurement = (await createdMeasurement.json<{ data: { id: string } }>()).data;
      const recordedValue = await request("POST", `/api/measurements/${savedMeasurement.id}/records`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ value: 3 })
      }, server1);
      expect(recordedValue.status).toBe(201);

      // Create Milestone on Server 1
      const createMs = await request(
        "POST",
        "/api/milestones",
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`,
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            goalId: savedGoalId,
            title: "Persistent Milestone Checkpoint"
          })
        },
        server1
      );
      expect(createMs.status).toBe(201);

      // Stop Server 1 (simulating shutdown)
      await server1.close();
      server1 = null;

      // Phase 2: Boot Server 2 on the SAME file-based DB
      server2 = createApp({
        dbPath: tempDbPath,
        config: { auth: { devMode: true } }
      });

      // Query Server 2 for the previously created Goal
      const retrieveGoal = await request(
        "GET",
        `/api/goals/${savedGoalId}`,
        {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
        },
        server2
      );
      expect(retrieveGoal.status).toBe(200);
      const retrievedGoalJson = await retrieveGoal.json<{ success: boolean; data: { id: string; title: string } }>();
      expect(retrievedGoalJson.success).toBe(true);
      expect(retrievedGoalJson.data.id).toBe(savedGoalId);
      expect(retrievedGoalJson.data.title).toBe("Persistent Goal across Reboots");

      // Query Server 2 for the previously created Milestone
      const retrieveMilestones = await request(
        "GET",
        `/api/goals/${savedGoalId}/milestones`,
        {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
        },
        server2
      );
      expect(retrieveMilestones.status).toBe(200);
      const retrievedMsJson = await retrieveMilestones.json<{
        success: boolean;
        data: Array<{ title: string; goalId: string }>;
      }>();
      expect(retrievedMsJson.success).toBe(true);
      expect(retrievedMsJson.data.length).toBe(1);
      expect(retrievedMsJson.data[0].title).toBe("Persistent Milestone Checkpoint");
      expect(retrievedMsJson.data[0].goalId).toBe(savedGoalId);

      const retrievedMeasurements = await request("GET", `/api/goals/${savedGoalId}/measurements`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
      }, server2);
      const measurementList = await retrievedMeasurements.json<{ data: Array<{ id: string }> }>();
      expect(measurementList.data).toHaveLength(1);
      const retrievedHistory = await request("GET", `/api/measurements/${savedMeasurement.id}/history`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` }
      }, server2);
      expect((await retrievedHistory.json<{ data: Array<{ value: number }> }>()).data[0].value).toBe(3);
    } finally {
      if (server1) await (server1 as AppInstance).close();
      if (server2) await (server2 as AppInstance).close();
      try {
        fs.rmSync(tempDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
      } catch {
        // ignore cleanup error
      }
    }
  });

  it("keeps AI routes authenticated and degrades cleanly without a provider key", async () => {
    const unauthorized = await request("POST", "/api/ai/goal-understanding", {
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ input: "Learn Spanish" }),
    });
    expect(unauthorized.status).toBe(401);
    const unavailable = await request("POST", "/api/ai/goal-understanding", {
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({ input: "Learn Spanish" }),
    });
    expect(unavailable.status).toBe(503);
    expect(await unavailable.json()).toMatchObject({ success: false, error: { code: "AI_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE" } });
  });

  it("runs a mocked discovery-to-approved-domain-record journey across a database restart", async () => {
    const tempDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "flow-up-ai-e2e-"));
    const dbPath = path.join(tempDir, "flow-up.db");
    const provider = {
      async generate(request: { operationType: string }) {
        if (request.operationType === "GOAL_CLARIFICATION") return { content: {
          outcome: "Learn conversational Spanish", successCriteria: ["Hold a 10-minute conversation"],
          knownFacts: ["The user wants to learn Spanish"], assumptions: [], missingInformation: ["Target date"], questions: [],
        } };
        if (request.operationType === "GOAL_FEASIBILITY") return { content: {
          assessment: "INSUFFICIENT_INFORMATION", summary: "A target date and available time are unknown.",
          concerns: [], alternatives: [], assumptions: [], missingInformation: ["Target date", "Available weekly time"],
        } };
        return { content: { summary: "A small starter plan", items: [
          { id: "root", type: "GOAL", title: "Learn conversational Spanish", description: "", targetDate: null, parentItemId: null, rationale: "Desired outcome" },
          { id: "checkpoint", type: "MILESTONE", title: "Complete beginner lessons", description: "", targetDate: null, parentItemId: "root", rationale: "First checkpoint" },
          { id: "practice", type: "ACTION", title: "Practice vocabulary for 15 minutes", description: "", targetDate: null, parentItemId: "checkpoint", rationale: "A manageable first step" },
          { id: "minutes", type: "MEASUREMENT", title: "Weekly practice time", description: "Record practice time each week.", targetDate: null, parentItemId: "root", rationale: "A measurable signal", valueType: "NUMBER", unit: "minutes", targetValue: null },
        ] } };
      },
    };
    let first = createApp({ dbPath, config: { auth: { devMode: true }, ai: { provider } } });
    const auth = { Authorization: `Bearer ${userA.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" };
    const invoke = (target: AppInstance, method: string, url: string, body?: unknown) =>
      dispatchInProcess(target, method, url, { headers: auth, ...(body === undefined ? {} : { body: JSON.stringify(body) }) });
    try {
      expect((await invoke(first, "POST", "/api/workspaces/initialize")).status).toBe(201);
      expect((await invoke(first, "POST", "/api/ai/goal-understanding", { input: "I want to learn Spanish but do not know where to begin." })).status).toBe(200);
      expect((await invoke(first, "POST", "/api/ai/feasibility", { input: "I want to learn Spanish but do not know where to begin." })).status).toBe(200);
      const proposed = await invoke(first, "POST", "/api/ai/goal-proposals", { input: "I want to learn Spanish but do not know where to begin." });
      expect(proposed.status).toBe(200);
      const proposal = (await proposed.json<{ data: { id: string; proposalData: { items: { id: string }[] } } }>()).data;
      expect(proposal.proposalData.items).toHaveLength(4);
      const applied = await invoke(first, "POST", `/api/ai/proposals/${proposal.id}/apply`, {
        selection: { selectedItemIds: proposal.proposalData.items.map((item) => item.id), editedData: proposal.proposalData },
      });
      expect(applied.status).toBe(200);
      expect(await applied.json()).toMatchObject({ data: { status: "APPLIED" } });
      await first.close();
      first = null as unknown as AppInstance;

      const second = createApp({ dbPath, config: { auth: { devMode: true } } });
      try {
        const goals = await invoke(second, "GET", "/api/goals");
        const goal = (await goals.json<{ data: { id: string; title: string }[] }>()).data[0];
        expect(goal.title).toBe("Learn conversational Spanish");
        const milestones = await invoke(second, "GET", `/api/goals/${goal.id}/milestones`);
        const milestone = (await milestones.json<{ data: { id: string; title: string }[] }>()).data[0];
        expect(milestone.title).toBe("Complete beginner lessons");
        const actions = await invoke(second, "GET", `/api/goals/${goal.id}/actions`);
        expect((await actions.json<{ data: { title: string; milestoneId: string }[] }>()).data[0]).toMatchObject({
          title: "Practice vocabulary for 15 minutes", milestoneId: milestone.id,
        });
        const measurements = await invoke(second, "GET", `/api/goals/${goal.id}/measurements`);
        expect((await measurements.json<{ data: { label: string; unit: string }[] }>()).data[0]).toMatchObject({ label: "Weekly practice time", unit: "minutes" });
        const duplicate = await invoke(second, "POST", `/api/ai/proposals/${proposal.id}/apply`, {
          selection: { selectedItemIds: proposal.proposalData.items.map((item) => item.id), editedData: proposal.proposalData },
        });
        expect(duplicate.status).toBe(409);
      } finally { await second.close(); }
    } finally {
      if (first) await first.close();
      fs.rmSync(tempDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createGoalService } from "../../src/modules/goal/goal.service";
import type {
  GoalRepository,
  GoalWorkspaceAccess
} from "../../src/modules/goal/goal.repository";
import type { Goal } from "../../src/modules/goal/goal.types";

const user = {
  userId: "user-1",
  email: "user@example.com",
  isAuthenticated: true as const
};

const goal: Goal = {
  id: "goal-1",
  workspaceId: "workspace-1",
  projectId: null,
  parentGoalId: null,
  title: "Complete FLOW-UP MVP",
  description: null,
  targetDate: null,
  status: "ACTIVE",
  createdAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z",
  updatedAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z",
  archivedAt: null
};

function makeRepository(): GoalRepository {
  return {
    createGoal: vi.fn(async (_workspaceId, input) => ({
      ...goal,
      ...input,
      id: "goal-created",
      workspaceId: "workspace-1",
      projectId: input.projectId ?? null,
      parentGoalId: input.parentGoalId ?? null,
      title: input.title,
      description: input.description ?? null,
      targetDate: input.targetDate ?? null
    })),
    findGoalById: vi.fn(async (id) => id === "goal-1" ? goal : null),
    listGoalsByWorkspaceId: vi.fn(async () => [goal]),
    updateGoal: vi.fn(async (_id, input) => ({
      ...goal,
      ...input,
      title: input.title ?? goal.title,
      description: input.description === undefined ? goal.description : input.description,
      targetDate: input.targetDate === undefined ? goal.targetDate : input.targetDate
    })),
    findDescendantIds: vi.fn(async () => [])
  };
}

function makeWorkspaceAccess(): GoalWorkspaceAccess {
  return {
    getCurrentWorkspaceId: vi.fn(async () => "workspace-1"),
    validateProjectInWorkspace: vi.fn(async () => true)
  };
}

describe("B03 Goal service", () => {
  it("creates an independent Goal in the authenticated Workspace", async () => {
    const repository = makeRepository();
    const service = createGoalService(repository, makeWorkspaceAccess());

    const result = await service.createGoal(user, {
      title: "  Complete FLOW-UP MVP  "
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(repository.createGoal).toHaveBeenCalledWith("workspace-1", {
      title: "Complete FLOW-UP MVP",
      description: null,
      targetDate: null,
      projectId: null,
      parentGoalId: null
    });
  });

  it("rejects an invalid title", async () => {
    const repository = makeRepository();
    const service = createGoalService(repository, makeWorkspaceAccess());

    const result = await service.createGoal(user, { title: " " });

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: false,
      error: { code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" }
    });
    expect(repository.createGoal).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("rejects access to a Goal outside the current Workspace", async () => {
    const repository = makeRepository();
    repository.findGoalById = vi.fn(async () => ({
      ...goal,
      workspaceId: "workspace-other"
    }));
    const service = createGoalService(repository, makeWorkspaceAccess());

    const result = await service.getGoal(user, "goal-other");

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: false,
      error: { code: "GOAL_ACCESS_DENIED" }
    });
  });

  it("rejects unauthenticated requests", async () => {
    const service = createGoalService(makeRepository(), makeWorkspaceAccess());

    const result = await service.listGoals({
      userId: "",
      email: null,
      isAuthenticated: true
    });

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: false,
      error: { code: "GOAL_UNAUTHENTICATED" }
    });
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  validateCreateGoalInput,
  validateUpdateGoalInput
} from "../../src/modules/goal/goal.validation";

describe("B03 Goal validation", () => {
  it("accepts a valid Goal", () => {
    expect(validateCreateGoalInput({ title: "Complete FLOW-UP MVP" }).valid)
      .toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects an empty title", () => {
    expect(validateCreateGoalInput({ title: " " })).toMatchObject({
      valid: false,
      code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE"
    });
  });

  it("rejects an invalid target date", () => {
    expect(validateCreateGoalInput({
      title: "Goal",
      targetDate: "not-a-date"
    })).toMatchObject({
      valid: false,
      code: "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT"
    });
  });

  it("rejects an empty update", () => {
    expect(validateUpdateGoalInput({})).toMatchObject({
      valid: false,
      code: "GOAL_INVALID_INPUT"
    });
  });
});

FILE: backend/tests/domain/goal-hierarchy/goal.hierarchy.test.ts
============================================================
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  buildGoalTree, getGoalAncestors, getGoalDescendants, validateParentRelationship,
  type Goal, type GoalReader,
} from "../../../src/domain/goal-hierarchy";

const goal = (id: string, parentGoalId: string | null): Goal => ({
  id, workspaceId: "w-1", projectId: null, parentGoalId,
  title: id, description: null, targetDate: null, createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
  updatedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
});

function reader(items: Goal[]): GoalReader {
  return {
    getGoal: (id) => items.find((item) => item.id === id) ?? null,
    getGoals: () => items,
  };
}

describe("C03 Goal hierarchy", () => {
  const items = [goal("root", null), goal("child", "root"), goal("leaf", "child")];

  it("validates a parent relationship", () => {
    expect(validateParentRelationship("leaf", "root", reader(items))).toEqual({ valid: true });
  });

  it("rejects self-parenting", () => {
    expect(validateParentRelationship("root", "root", reader(items)))
      .toEqual({ valid: false, code: "GOAL_SELF_PARENTING" });
  });

  it("rejects a cycle", () => {
    expect(validateParentRelationship("root", "leaf", reader(items)))
      .toEqual({ valid: false, code: "GOAL_HIERARCHY_CYCLE" });
  });

  it("returns ancestors from immediate parent to root", () => {
    expect(getGoalAncestors("leaf", reader(items))).toEqual({
      valid: true, goals: [items[1], items[0]],
    });
  });

  it("returns descendants without duplicates", () => {
    expect(getGoalDescendants("root", reader(items))).toEqual({
      valid: true, goals: [items[1], items[2]],
    });
  });

  it("builds a nested Goal tree", () => {
    const result = buildGoalTree("root", reader(items));
    expect(result.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(result.tree?.children[0].children[0].goal.id).toBe("leaf");
  });

  it("rejects parent-child scope mismatch while policy is OPEN", () => {
    const differentProject = { ...goal("p-child", "root"), projectId: "p-2" };
    expect(validateParentRelationship("p-child", "root", reader([items[0], differentProject])))
      .toEqual({ valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_POLICY_UNRESOLVED" });
  });
});

FILE: backend/tests/domain/goal-hierarchy/goal.validation.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { validateGoalAttributes, validateGoalScope } from "../../../src/domain/goal-hierarchy";

describe("C03 Goal validation", () => {
  it("accepts valid required attributes and optional values", () => {
    expect(validateGoalAttributes({
      id: "g-1", title: "Learn TypeScript", description: null, targetDate: "2027-01-31",
    })).toEqual({ valid: true });
  });

  it("rejects empty title", () => {
    expect(validateGoalAttributes({ id: "g-1", title: "  " }))
      .toEqual({ valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TITLE" });
  });

  it("rejects impossible calendar dates", () => {
    expect(validateGoalAttributes({ id: "g-1", title: "Valid", targetDate: "2027-02-30" }))
      .toEqual({ valid: false, code: "GOAL_INVALID_TARGET_DATE" });
  });

  it("delegates Workspace/Project rules to C02", () => {
    const scopeValidator = { validate: () => ({ valid: true as const }) };
    expect(validateGoalScope("u-1", { workspaceId: "w-1", projectId: null }, scopeValidator))
      .toEqual({ valid: true });
  });

  it("does not invent an independent Goal without a Workspace", () => {
    const scopeValidator = { validate: () => ({ valid: true as const }) };
    expect(validateGoalScope("u-1", { workspaceId: "", projectId: null }, scopeValidator))
      .toEqual({ valid: false, code: "GOAL_SCOPE_INVALID" });
  });
});

FILE: backend/tests/milestone/milestone.service.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createMilestoneService } from "../../src/modules/milestone/milestone.service";
import type {
  MilestoneGoalAccess,
  MilestoneRepository
} from "../../src/modules/milestone/milestone.repository";
import type { Milestone } from "../../src/modules/milestone/milestone.types";

const user = {
  userId: "user-1",
  email: "user@example.com",
  isAuthenticated: true as const
};

const milestone: Milestone = {
  id: "milestone-1",
  goalId: "goal-1",
  title: "Reach Reading Band 6.0",
  description: null,
  targetDate: null,
  status: "PENDING",
  createdAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z",
  updatedAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z",
  completedAt: null
};

function makeRepository(): MilestoneRepository {
  return {
    createMilestone: vi.fn(async (input) => ({
      ...milestone,
      ...input,
      id: "milestone-created"
    })),
    findMilestoneById: vi.fn(async (id) =>
      id === milestone.id ? milestone : null
    ),
    listMilestonesByGoal: vi.fn(async () => [milestone]),
    updateMilestone: vi.fn(async (_id, input) => ({
      ...milestone,
      ...input,
      title: input.title ?? milestone.title,
      description: input.description === undefined
        ? milestone.description
        : input.description,
      targetDate: input.targetDate === undefined
        ? milestone.targetDate
        : input.targetDate
    })),
    completeMilestone: vi.fn(async (_id, completedAt) => ({
      ...milestone,
      status: "COMPLETED" as const,
      completedAt,
      updatedAt: completedAt
    }))
  };
}

function makeGoalAccess(): MilestoneGoalAccess {
  return {
    getGoalForUser: vi.fn(async () => ({
      success: true as const,
      data: { id: "goal-1", status: "ACTIVE", archivedAt: null }
    }))
  };
}

describe("B04 Milestone service", () => {
  it("creates a Milestone after validating its owning Goal", async () => {
    const repository = makeRepository();
    const goalAccess = makeGoalAccess();
    const service = createMilestoneService(repository, goalAccess);

    const result = await service.createMilestone(user, {
      goalId: "goal-1",
      title: "  Reach Reading Band 6.0  "
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(goalAccess.getGoalForUser).toHaveBeenCalledWith(user, "goal-1");
    expect(repository.createMilestone).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      goalId: "goal-1",
      title: "Reach Reading Band 6.0",
      description: null,
      targetDate: null
    });
  });

  it("rejects creation when the Goal does not exist", async () => {
    const goalAccess = makeGoalAccess();
    goalAccess.getGoalForUser = vi.fn(async () => ({
      success: false as const,
      error: { code: "GOAL_NOT_FOUND", message: "Not found" }
    }));
    const repository = makeRepository();
    const service = createMilestoneService(repository, goalAccess);

    const result = await service.createMilestone(user, {
      goalId: "missing-goal",
      title: "Milestone"
    });

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: false,
      error: { code: "MILESTONE_GOAL_NOT_FOUND" }
    });
    expect(repository.createMilestone).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("completes a Milestone and records completedAt", async () => {
    const repository = makeRepository();
    const service = createMilestoneService(
      repository,
      makeGoalAccess(),
      () => "2026-11-17T10:00:00.000Z"
    );

    const result = await service.completeMilestone(user, "milestone-1");

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: true,
      data: {
        status: "COMPLETED",
        completedAt: "2026-11-17T10:00:00.000Z"
      }
    });
  });

  it("rejects unauthenticated access", async () => {
    const service = createMilestoneService(
      makeRepository(),
      makeGoalAccess()
    );

    const result = await service.listMilestonesByGoal({
      userId: "",
      email: null,
      isAuthenticated: true
    }, "goal-1");

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: false,
      error: { code: "MILESTONE_UNAUTHENTICATED" }
    });
  });
});

FILE: backend/tests/action/action.service.test.ts
============================================================
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createActionService } from "../../src/modules/action/action.service";
import type {
  ActionGoalAccess,
  ActionMilestoneAccess,
  ActionRepository
} from "../../src/modules/action/action.repository";
import type { Action } from "../../src/modules/action/action.types";

const user = {
  userId: "user-1",
  email: "user@example.com",
  isAuthenticated: true as const
};

const action: Action = {
  id: "action-1",
  goalId: "goal-1",
  milestoneId: null,
  title: "Complete reading test",
  description: null,
  status: "TODO",
  createdAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z",
  updatedAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z",
  completedAt: null,
  archivedAt: null
};

function repository(): ActionRepository {
  return {
    create: vi.fn(async input => ({ ...action, ...input, id: "action-new" })),
    findById: vi.fn(async id => id === action.id ? action : null),
    listByGoal: vi.fn(async () => [action]),
    listByMilestone: vi.fn(async () => []),
    update: vi.fn(async (_id, input) => ({ ...action, ...input })),
    updateStatus: vi.fn(async (_id, status, completedAt) => ({
      ...action, status, completedAt
    })),
    saveExecution: vi.fn(async (_actionId, input) => ({
      actionId: action.id,
      actualStartedAt: input.actualStartedAt ?? null,
      actualCompletedAt: input.actualCompletedAt ?? null,
      actualDurationMinutes: input.actualDurationMinutes ?? null,
      executionNote: input.executionNote ?? null
    })),
    getExecution: vi.fn(async () => null)
  };
}

function goalAccess(): ActionGoalAccess {
  return {
    validateGoal: vi.fn(async () => ({
      success: true as const, data: { id: "goal-1" }
    }))
  };
}

function milestoneAccess(): ActionMilestoneAccess {
  return {
    validateMilestone: vi.fn(async (_user, id, goalId) => ({
      success: true as const, data: { id, goalId }
    }))
  };
}

describe("B05 Action service", () => {
  it("creates an Action after validating Goal", async () => {
    const repo = repository();
    const service = createActionService(repo, goalAccess(), milestoneAccess());

    const result = await service.createAction(user, {
      goalId: "goal-1",
      title: "  Complete reading test  "
    });

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(repo.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      goalId: "goal-1",
      milestoneId: null,
      title: "Complete reading test",
      description: null,
      status: "TODO"
    });
  });

  it("rejects a Milestone associated with another Goal", async () => {
    const access = milestoneAccess();
    access.validateMilestone = vi.fn(async () => ({
      success: true as const,
      data: { id: "milestone-1", goalId: "different-goal" }
    }));
    const repo = repository();
    const service = createActionService(repo, goalAccess(), access);

    const result = await service.createAction(user, {
      goalId: "goal-1",
      milestoneId: "milestone-1",
      title: "Practice"
    });

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: false,
      error: { code: "ACTION_INVALID_ASSOCIATION" }
    });
    expect(repo.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("updates Action to IN_PROGRESS", async () => {
    const repo = repository();
    const service = createActionService(repo, goalAccess(), milestoneAccess());

    const result = await service.updateActionStatus(user, "action-1", "IN_PROGRESS");

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: true,
      data: { status: "IN_PROGRESS" }
    });
  });

  it("records execution data", async () => {
    const repo = repository();
    const service = createActionService(repo, goalAccess(), milestoneAccess());

    const result = await service.recordExecution(user, "action-1", {
      actualDurationMinutes: 35,
      executionNote: "Reviewed mistakes"
    });

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      success: true,
      data: {
        actualDurationMinutes: 35,
        executionNote: "Reviewed mistakes"
      }
    });
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { PlanningService } from "../../src/modules/planning";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext, DailyPlan, ScheduleEntry,
} from "../../src/modules/planning";

const user: AuthenticatedUserContext = { userId: "user-1", email: null, isAuthenticated: true };
const plan: DailyPlan = {
  id: "plan-1", userId: "user-1", date: "2026-11-18",
  createdAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z",
};
const schedule: ScheduleEntry = {
  id: "schedule-1", actionId: "action-1", dailyPlanId: "plan-1",
  startAt: "2026-11-18T08:00:00Z", endAt: "2026-11-18T09:00:00Z",
  createdAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z",
};

function setup() {
  const repo = {
    createDailyPlan: vi.fn(async (input: Omit<DailyPlan, "id">) => ({ ...input, id: "plan-1" })),
    getDailyPlan: vi.fn(async () => plan),
    findDailyPlanByUserAndDate: vi.fn(async () => null),
    updateDailyPlan: vi.fn(async (_id: string, patch: Partial<DailyPlan>) => ({ ...plan, ...patch })),
    listSchedulesByDailyPlan: vi.fn(async () => [schedule]),
    createSchedule: vi.fn(async (input: Omit<ScheduleEntry, "id">) => ({ ...input, id: "schedule-1" })),
    getSchedule: vi.fn(async () => schedule),
    updateSchedule: vi.fn(async (_id: string, patch: Partial<ScheduleEntry>) => ({ ...schedule, ...patch })),
    deleteSchedule: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
    listSchedulesByDateRange: vi.fn(async () => [schedule]),
    findOverlappingSchedules: vi.fn(async () => []),
  };
  const actionAccess = { assertCanAccessAction: vi.fn(async () => undefined) };
  const access = {
    assertCanAccessDailyPlan: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
    assertCanAccessSchedule: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
  };
  const rules = {
    assertScheduleAllowed: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
    assertScheduleMatchesPlanDate: vi.fn(),
  };
  const proposalApproval = { assertApprovedByUser: vi.fn(async () => undefined) };
  return { service: new PlanningService(repo, actionAccess, access, rules, proposalApproval), repo, actionAccess, access, rules, proposalApproval };
}

describe("PlanningService", () => {
  it("creates a Daily Plan for the authenticated user", async () => {
    const { service, repo } = setup();
    const result = await service.createDailyPlan(user, { date: "2026-11-18" });
    expect(result.userId).toBe(user.userId);
    expect(repo.createDailyPlan).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("creates a Schedule after validating Action access", async () => {
    const { service, actionAccess, repo, rules } = setup();
    const result = await service.createSchedule(user, {
      actionId: "action-1", startAt: schedule.startAt, endAt: schedule.endAt, dailyPlanId: plan.id,
    });
    expect(result.actionId).toBe("action-1");
    expect(actionAccess.assertCanAccessAction).toHaveBeenCalledWith(user, "action-1");
    expect(rules.assertScheduleAllowed).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(repo.createSchedule).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("deletes a Schedule without deleting its Action", async () => {
    const { service, repo } = setup();
    await service.deleteSchedule(user, schedule.id);
    expect(repo.deleteSchedule).toHaveBeenCalledWith(schedule.id);
  });

  it("requires approval before applying a proposal", async () => {
    const { service, proposalApproval } = setup();
    await service.applyApprovedPlanningProposal(user, {
      proposalId: "proposal-1", userId: user.userId, approved: true, schedules: [],
    });
    expect(proposalApproval.assertApprovedByUser).toHaveBeenCalledWith(user, "proposal-1");
  });
});

FILE: backend/tests/progress/progress.service.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ProgressService } from "../../src/modules/progress";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext, GoalProgressInput, GoalProgressResult, MeasurementProgressInput,
} from "../../src/modules/progress";

const user: AuthenticatedUserContext = { userId: "user-1", email: null, isAuthenticated: true };
const goalResult: GoalProgressResult = {
  goalId: "goal-1", progressValue: null, calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
  contributingSources: [], calculationContext: {},
};

function setup() {
  const sources = {
    assertCanAccessGoal: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
    getMeasurementProgressInputs: vi.fn(async () => []),
    getActionProgressInput: vi.fn(async () => ({ goalId: "goal-1", actions: [] })),
    getChildGoalIds: vi.fn(async () => []),
    getGoalProgressInputs: vi.fn(async () => ({
      measurementResults: [], actionResult: null, childGoalResults: [],
    })),
  };
  const rules = {
    calculateMeasurement: vi.fn((input: MeasurementProgressInput) => ({
      measurementId: input.measurementId, currentValue: input.currentValue,
      targetValue: input.targetValue, progressValue: null,
      calculationStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED" as const, calculationContext: {},
    })),
    calculateActionCompletion: vi.fn(),
    calculateGoal: vi.fn((input: GoalProgressInput) => ({ ...goalResult, goalId: input.goalId })),
    aggregateChildGoals: vi.fn(),
    compareMeasurement: vi.fn(() => ({
      difference: 0.5, comparisonStatus: "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED", progressValue: null,
    })),
    combineStatuses: vi.fn(() => "RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED" as const),
  };
  return { service: new ProgressService(sources, rules), sources, rules };
}

describe("ProgressService", () => {
  it("delegates Measurement calculation to approved rules", () => {
    const { service, rules } = setup();
    service.calculateMeasurementProgress(user, {
      measurementId: "m-1", currentValue: 6.5, targetValue: 7, unit: "Band Score", valueType: "NUMBER",
    });
    expect(rules.calculateMeasurement).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("delegates Action Completion to approved rules", () => {
    const { service, rules } = setup();
    service.calculateActionCompletion(user, {
      goalId: "goal-1", actions: [{ id: "a-1", status: "COMPLETED" }],
    });
    expect(rules.calculateActionCompletion).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("checks Goal ownership before building a summary", async () => {
    const { service, sources, rules } = setup();
    const result = await service.getGoalProgressSummary(user, "goal-1");
    expect(sources.assertCanAccessGoal).toHaveBeenCalledWith(user, "goal-1");
    expect(rules.calculateGoal).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(result.goalId).toBe("goal-1");
  });

  it("does not invent a percentage when rules are not configured", () => {
    const { service } = setup();
    const result = service.calculateMeasurementProgress(user, {
      measurementId: "m-1", currentValue: 6.5, targetValue: 7, unit: "Band Score", valueType: "NUMBER",
    });
    expect(result.progressValue).toBeNull();
    expect(result.calculationStatus).toBe("RULE_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  });
});

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ReviewService } from "../../src/modules/review/review.service";
import type { ReviewAccess, ReviewRepository, ReviewRules, ReviewSourceReader } from "../../src/modules/review/review.repository";
import type { ReviewUserContext } from "../../src/modules/review/review.types";
const user: ReviewUserContext = { userId: "u1", isAuthenticated: true };
describe("ReviewService", () => {
  let repo: ReviewRepository, access: ReviewAccess, sources: ReviewSourceReader, rules: ReviewRules, service: ReviewService;
  beforeEach(() => {
    repo = { create: vi.fn(async r => r), getById: vi.fn(async () => null), updateNote: vi.fn(async () => null), updateSummary: vi.fn(async () => null) };
    access = { assertScopeAccess: vi.fn(async () => undefined) };
    sources = {
      getPlannedWork: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: [] })), getActualExecution: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: [] })),
      getProgress: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: null, status: "UNAVAILABLE" as const, limitations: ["No history contract"] })),
      getMeasurement: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: null, status: "NOT_REQUESTED" as const })),
    };
    rules = {
      validation: { maxNoteLength: 500, requireGoalId: false, allowPeriodOnlyScope: true },
      matchPlannedToActual: vi.fn(() => ({ items: [], limitations: ["Matching contract pending"] })),
      generateSummary: vi.fn(input => ({ ...input, limitations: ["Policy fixture only"] })),
      now: () => "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z", createReviewRecord: false, persistSummary: false,
    };
    service = new ReviewService(repo, access, sources, rules);
  });
  it("preserves unavailable progress as unavailable", async () => {
    const result = await service.getReviewData(user, { goalId: "g1" });
    expect(result.progress.status).toBe("UNAVAILABLE");
    expect(result.progress.data).toBeNull();
  });
  it("blocks persistence until the model is approved", async () => {
    await expect(service.createReview(user, { scope: { goalId: "g1" } })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "REVIEW_INVALID_STATE" });
  });
});

FILE: backend/tests/measurement/measurement.service.test.ts
============================================================
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { MeasurementService } from "../../src/modules/measurement";
import type {
  AuthenticatedUserContext, Measurement, MeasurementRecord,
} from "../../src/modules/measurement";

const user: AuthenticatedUserContext = {
  userId: "user-1", email: null, isAuthenticated: true,
};
const definition: Measurement = {
  id: "measurement-1", goalId: "goal-1", label: "IELTS Overall",
  description: null, valueType: "NUMBER", unit: "Band Score", targetValue: 7,
  createdAt: "2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  archivedAt: null,
};
const record: MeasurementRecord = {
  id: "record-1", measurementId: definition.id, value: 6.5,
  recordedAt: "2026-11-15T00:00:00.000Z", createdAt: "2026-11-15T00:00:00.000Z",
  note: null,
};

function setup() {
  const repo = {
    createMeasurement: vi.fn(async (input: Omit<Measurement, "id">) => ({ ...input, id: "measurement-1" })),
    getMeasurement: vi.fn(async () => definition),
    listMeasurementsByGoal: vi.fn(async () => [definition]),
    updateMeasurement: vi.fn(async (_id: string, patch: Partial<Measurement>) => ({ ...definition, ...patch })),
    archiveMeasurement: vi.fn(async (_id: string, archivedAt: string) => ({ ...definition, archivedAt })),
    createRecord: vi.fn(async (input: Omit<MeasurementRecord, "id">) => ({ ...input, id: "record-1" })),
    listRecords: vi.fn(async () => [record]),
  };
  const goalAccess = { assertCanAccessGoal: vi.fn(async () => undefined) };
  const rules = {
    validateDefinition: vi.fn(),
    validateValue: vi.fn(),
    assertCanRecord: vi.fn(),
    resolveCurrent: vi.fn((records: MeasurementRecord[]) => records[0] ?? null),
    assertCanUpdate: vi.fn(),
    assertCanArchive: vi.fn(),
  };
  return { service: new MeasurementService(repo, goalAccess, rules), repo, goalAccess, rules };
}

describe("MeasurementService", () => {
  it("creates a definition after checking Goal access and domain policy", async () => {
    const { service, repo, goalAccess, rules } = setup();
    const result = await service.createMeasurement(user, {
      goalId: "goal-1", label: "IELTS Overall", valueType: "NUMBER",
    });
    expect(result.id).toBe("measurement-1");
    expect(goalAccess.assertCanAccessGoal).toHaveBeenCalledWith(user, "goal-1");
    expect(rules.validateDefinition).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(repo.createMeasurement).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("records a value only after definition access and domain checks", async () => {
    const { service, repo, rules } = setup();
    const result = await service.recordMeasurementValue(user, {
      measurementId: definition.id, value: 6.5,
    });
    expect(result.value).toBe(6.5);
    expect(rules.assertCanRecord).toHaveBeenCalledWith(definition);
    expect(rules.validateValue).toHaveBeenCalledWith(definition, 6.5);
    expect(repo.createRecord).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("returns the current value through the injected C06 resolver", async () => {
    const { service, rules } = setup();
    const result = await service.getCurrentMeasurementValue(user, definition.id);
    expect(rules.resolveCurrent).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(result).toEqual(record);
  });

  it("archives without deleting record history", async () => {
    const { service, repo } = setup();
    await service.archiveMeasurement(user, definition.id);
    expect(repo.archiveMeasurement).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(repo.listRecords).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
